# M41a — Feel (1.0.0-rc.3) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Fences stop feeling dead — themes with a little life, titles that read well, motion that answers the mouse, a click that just opens, widgets in three designed sizes with a gallery, a flip side and Now playing — and a desktop hidden by a double-click is entirely the user's.

**Architecture:** Core (test-first) gains the choices and their rules: `Clicks` / `OpenWith` / `FenceMenuStyle`, `RunState.HandlesRightButton` / `RevealWanted`, `FenceTheme` / `TitleStyle` / `NameStyle` / `Motion` + `MotionPolicy` and the themes in `FenceLook.Resolve`, `Settings.NewInstall`, `Emoji`, `Titles`, `WidgetSize` / `WidgetSizes`, `CalendarMonth`, `WorldClocks`, `NowPlaying`, `WidgetBoards`, the new `Fence` / `OwnLook` / `WidgetOptions` fields with their normalizing and lenient JSON. Shell gains the left-up in the mouse hook, `DesktopReveal` (the shell's minimize-all), `MediaSessions` (Windows' media sessions over the Windows SDK projection), `VolumeKeys`, `PlayerWindows`, and the blur switch. The App draws it: `FenceThemeLayer`, the title styles and name styles in `FenceWindow`, `FenceWindow.Motion`, the Guided / Compact menu (`ArrangeMenu`), one click in the list, `WidgetFace` / `NowPlayingCard`, `FenceWindow.Widgets` (gallery, flip side, snap), `FenceHost.Clicks` / `.Media`, the new Settings and Fence settings controls.

**Tech Stack:** .NET 10, C#, WPF, xUnit; the Windows SDK projection (`net10.0-windows10.0.22621.0`: Windows.Media.Control, no NuGet); Windows PowerShell 5.1 in the VM guest; a test-only WinForms FakePlayer for the VM.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design.md` (approved 2026-10-11). Decisions: ADR-063 (themes, titles, names, motion), ADR-064 (one click, the menu's shape), ADR-065 (widgets, Now playing, the build target), ADR-066 (a hidden desktop, reveal) — added by Task 3.

## How this plan is written

Built in a scratch worktree (`neo_fences-m41a-proto`, branch `m41a-proto` from `bd417e2`), part by part (clicks, menus and
the desktop; the look; widgets), each checked in the test VM `NF-Win11-Dev` — nothing ran on the owner's PC:

- **Live checks** (`Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Live`, fresh start): **14 of 14** — the M38 Peek checks, and one click opens a folder, Ctrl+click selects without opening, the Guided headings and the Compact icon row, a quick-hidden desktop shows Explorer's own menu (and the replayed one with fences shown), Click wallpaper to reveal moves Notepad aside and back, Now playing shows FakePlayer's song and its pause and next reach the player
- **Looks probe** (`-Script probe-looks.ps1`): six fences side by side (Aurora + Clean, HUD + Caps, Neon + Pill, Light Mica +
  Clean, Classic + Simple, Aurora + Caps), the names Crisp / Chips / Plain, the hover, Fence settings — screenshots sent to
  the owner.
- **Widgets probe** (`-Script probe-widgets.ps1`): a fence with Clock S, Calendar M, Stats M, Now playing L; a floating
  board with Clock L (two world clocks) and Calendar L; the hover handles; the clock's back; the gallery — screenshots sent.

Found and fixed while probing: the reveal first used `ToggleDesktop`, which does nothing right after a click on the
wallpaper (the desktop has the focus) — now the shell's MinimizeAll / UndoMinimizeALL; the "+" never showed, because the
title is a caption (no WPF mouse events) — now from `WM_NCMOUSEMOVE` with a short leave watch; a VM has no audio device,
so no player reports a song there — the test-only `tools/vm/FakePlayer` puts one into Windows' media controls; a typo in a
new enum setting would have failed config.json — lenient converters (test first); the gallery as a popup opened under the
fence (a fence is never the active window, so WPF does not raise its popups) — it is drawn inside the fence now, and the
widget's back (a popup) is raised topmost when it opens; a Large widget in a fence narrower than four cells was cut off —
its face now shrinks to the clamped span. Size: the self-contained publish grows
from 144 MB to 170 MB (the SDK projection, ADR-065).

Replay-verified on a fresh worktree of `main` at `bd417e2`: `dotnet test` → 889 passed; patch 1a alone fails to build (`error CS0103: The name 'FenceTheme' does not exist in the current context` — the RED); 1b → 1002 passed (also on Linux, WSL Ubuntu); patch 2 builds with 0 warnings, 1002 pass; patch 3 applies; the tree is identical to the prototype (`git diff --cached m41a-proto` empty). Tasks 1–3 are **patches to apply** (`git apply --whitespace=nowarn <file>`; if one does not apply, stop).

**Calls made while prototyping (ledger them as rulings at the task named):**
- Task 1: a desktop **Click** is a left press and release within the double-click slop on empty desktop (not a
  double-click's second release); the right button is held back only for the draw gesture and never while quick-hidden
  (`HandlesRightButton = DrawGesture && !QuickHidden`); **Reset settings** now gives the new-install look
  (`Settings.NewInstall`: Aurora, 30 % transparency, Clean titles, `ThemesIntroduced`) — an M36 test's expectation moved
  with it; a theme's strength is its tone's Settings value (Light Mica: light; the others: dark); HUD and Neon frame in the
  accent, HUD's title mono unless the fence chose a font; the emoji is the first grapheme, at most 16 UTF-16 units; the
  title count leaves widgets out; widget sizes from a stored span: ≥ 3 columns Large, else ≥ 2 rows Medium, else Small (an
  old 1×1 clock is Small — an M25 test's expectation moved); the drag snaps at 3 columns / 1.5 rows; the Date widget is
  named Calendar (an M25 test moved); world clocks are IANA zones from a 17-city list, at most two, unknown ones dropped on
  load; the clock's 12/24-hour follows Windows (the spec's "24-hour" switch is not added); player names from a known list,
  else the app id's last part; every new enum reads leniently.
- Task 2: widgets keep the double-click (their face is for reading) and folder panels keep Explorer's double-click inside
  their list; a deliberate selection (Ctrl+click, the box) stays until a plain click, a right-click's selection only while
  its menu is open (the spec said "only while Ctrl is held" — that would lose a selection before it is dragged); Colour stays
  in the fence menu until Fence settings has swatches (M41b) and Refresh stays (an action); "Open items with" sits on Settings
  → Fences, "Fence menu" on Appearance; the Compact icon row is Add item, Rename, Fence settings, Lock, Add a widget; Caps'
  letter-spacing is hair spaces (WPF has no tracking — a ponytail); emoji draw in one colour (WPF has no colour fonts);
  Chips are opaque so ClearType can work; covers and folder panels keep their own hover (no lift, tilt or ripple); the
  themes tip uses the undo bar of the top-left fence for 30 s; the gallery is drawn inside the fence (the spec's "flyout":
  a popup stayed invisible), with a ✕, no search or categories (four widgets) and no dragging onto another fence yet (a
  ponytail); the widget's back is a popup raised topmost, mouse only (a fence never takes the keyboard outside Peek — rc.2's
  Win+D fix); the widget menu is Widget settings…, Size ▸, Remove (its options moved to the back,
  Properties… left it); the mouse wheel sends Windows' volume keys (its flyout shows the level — a ponytail for an in-widget
  bar); "Nothing playing" shows the last song but its Play does nothing until a player has a session; Shell and App target
  `net10.0-windows10.0.22621.0` (minimum 10.0.22000), and the CA1416 suppression goes.
- Task 3: the live-check helpers move to `live-helpers.ps1` (shared with the probes and the release-candidate pass, which
  gains `one-click-open` and `hidden-desktop-passthrough`); `Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Live` takes `-Script` and publishes
  FakePlayer next to the build.

## Global Constraints

- Hard rules stand: NeoFences never modifies, moves, renames or deletes a user file; native desktop icons always come back;
  no hook but `WH_MOUSE_LL` (it now also reads left-up, still on its own thread); Win32/COM only in `NeoFences.Shell`
  (WinRT through the SDK projection there too, ADR-065); bindings via CsWin32 except the WinRT media API and the
  late-bound Shell.Application (commented); no new NuGet dependency (the projection comes with the .NET SDK); Core pure and
  test-first; every shell failure logged, never a crash.
- A fence never takes the keyboard outside Peek: the gallery and the widget's back are mouse-only popups (rc.2, Win+D).
- Motion is off in a game, in safe mode and with Windows' animation effects off; theme motion ≤ 20 fps, Now playing's record
  ≤ 30 fps, only while shown.
- Existing setups keep their look (Classic, Simple); new installs get Aurora + Clean; no config schema bump (new fields
  default when absent; an rc.2 reading an rc.3 config ignores them).
- Copy, exactly: "Open items with" (One click / Double click), "Click wallpaper to reveal desktop", "Fence menu" (Guided
  (with headings) / Compact (icon row)), headings "Add to this fence", "This fence", "Everything", "Theme" (Classic, Aurora
  glass, Game HUD, Neon, Light Mica), "Title style" (Simple, Clean, Pill, Caps), "Item names" (Crisp, Chips, Plain),
  "Motion" (Lively, Calm, Off), "Transparency", "Emoji", "Widgets float", "Widget…", "Add a widget", "Widget settings…",
  "Size" (Small, Medium, Large), "Done", "Nothing playing", "Last: <title> · <artist>", "New: fence themes — Aurora glass,
  Game HUD, Neon, Light Mica" / "Try them", "Only widgets here — let them float?" / "Float".
- Commits: single line, Conventional Commits, past tense, **no Co-Authored-By trailer**; secret scan
  (`gmail|claude\.ai/artifact`) before each commit; GUIDE.md changes with every menu entry, setting and gesture (ADR-050).
- Version stays `1.0.0-rc.2` until the release step; the release is 1.0.0-rc.3.

## Review Focus

1. **Many themed fences with Motion Lively** (ten Aurora / HUD / Neon fences, idle): CPU stays low (< 2 % average on the
   owner's PC), nothing moves in a game, while quick-hidden, paused or rolled up; Calm and Off really calm or stop it.
   (Checklist AZ11–AZ12; Task 4 asks the owner first.)
2. **Players that misbehave**: no media sessions API, a player that throws on a command, a session with no title, a live
   stream (no end), a cover that fails to decode, two players switching — the widget shows what it can or "Nothing
   playing", logs once, never crashes. (AZ13; FakePlayer covers the happy path in the VM.)
3. **One click everywhere items open**: covers (games), a fence of tabs, a Free layout, a rolled-up fence opened by hover,
   Peek's keyboard (Enter still opens), a drag that ends where it began, a double-click in one-click mode (opens once),
   Double click chosen in Settings or one fence. (AZ2–AZ4.)
4. **Windows light / dark and its accent changing while fences show**: Classic follows, the themes keep their tone, the
   chips and names stay readable, the widget cards and bars follow. (AZ10.)
5. **Widgets in odd places**: a Large widget in a two-column fence (clamped), an old free-sized widget from rc.2, a widget
   in a folder-view fence (none can be added), a floating board turned back to glass, the gallery and the back opened on a
   fence near a screen edge. (AZ14.)

```diff
diff --git a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Appearance/ThemesTests.cs b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Appearance/ThemesTests.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..4cb02d9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Appearance/ThemesTests.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,223 @@
+using NeoFences.Core.Appearance;
+using NeoFences.Core.Config;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Core.Tests.Appearance;
+
+/// <summary>M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §1–4): themes, title styles, emoji, name styles, motion.</summary>
+public class ThemesTests
+{
+    private static readonly AppearanceSettings Classic = new();
+    private static readonly Fence Plain = Fence.Create("Apps");
+    private static readonly Argb Red = FenceLook.Swatches[TabColor.Red];
+
+    private static FenceStyle Resolve(AppearanceSettings appearance, Fence fence, bool light = false, Argb? wallpaper = null) =>
+        FenceLook.Resolve(appearance, fence, light, wallpaper);
+
+    // ---------- §1 themes ----------
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void Defaults_AreTodaysLook_ForExistingSetups()
+    {
+        Assert.Equal((FenceTheme.Classic, TitleStyle.Simple, NameStyle.Crisp, Motion.Lively),
+            (Classic.Theme, Classic.TitleStyle, Classic.NameStyle, Classic.Motion));
+        var style = Resolve(Classic, Plain);
+        Assert.Equal((FenceTheme.Classic, TitleStyle.Simple), (style.Theme, style.TitleStyle));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ANewInstall_StartsWithAuroraAndCleanTitles()
+    {
+        var config = NeoFencesConfig.CreateDefault();
+        Assert.Equal((FenceTheme.Aurora, TitleStyle.Clean), (config.Settings.Appearance.Theme, config.Settings.Appearance.TitleStyle));
+        Assert.True(config.Settings.Appearance.StrengthDark > 0); // Aurora's glass has a body from the start
+        Assert.True(config.Settings.ThemesIntroduced); // no "New: fence themes" note on a fresh start
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ResetSettings_GivesTheNewInstallLook()
+    {
+        var reset = SettingsReset.Apply(new NeoFencesConfig { Settings = new Settings { Appearance = Classic with { Theme = FenceTheme.Neon } } });
+        Assert.Equal(NeoFencesConfig.CreateDefault().Settings.Appearance, reset.Settings.Appearance);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AFencesOwnTheme_WinsOverAllFences()
+    {
+        var fence = Plain with { Look = new OwnLook { Theme = FenceTheme.Hud } };
+        Assert.Equal(FenceTheme.Hud, Resolve(Classic with { Theme = FenceTheme.Aurora }, fence).Theme);
+        Assert.Equal(FenceTheme.Aurora, Resolve(Classic with { Theme = FenceTheme.Aurora }, Plain).Theme);
+    }
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.Aurora, false)]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.Hud, false)]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.Neon, false)]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.LightMica, true)]
+    public void ANewTheme_HasItsOwnTone_WhateverWindowsUses(FenceTheme theme, bool lightTone)
+    {
+        foreach (var windowsLight in new[] { false, true })
+            Assert.Equal(lightTone, Resolve(Classic with { Theme = theme }, Plain, light: windowsLight).LightTone);
+    }
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(false)]
+    [InlineData(true)]
+    public void Classic_FollowsWindows(bool light) => Assert.Equal(light, Resolve(Classic, Plain, light).LightTone);
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.Aurora)]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.Hud)]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.Neon)]
+    [InlineData(FenceTheme.LightMica)]
+    public void Transparency_IsTheStrengthSlider_InEveryTheme(FenceTheme theme)
+    {
+        var clear = Resolve(Classic with { Theme = theme }, Plain with { Look = new OwnLook { Strength = 0 } });
+        var dense = Resolve(Classic with { Theme = theme }, Plain with { Look = new OwnLook { Strength = 85 } });
+        Assert.True(dense.Veil.A > clear.Veil.A + 150, $"{theme}: {clear.Veil.A} → {dense.Veil.A}");
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ANewTheme_KeepsTheFencesColourAsItsAccent()
+    {
+        var red = Plain with { TabColor = TabColor.Red };
+        foreach (var theme in new[] { FenceTheme.Aurora, FenceTheme.Hud, FenceTheme.Neon, FenceTheme.LightMica })
+            Assert.Equal(Red, Resolve(Classic with { Theme = theme }, red).Accent);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void WithoutAColour_HudIsCyan_TheOthersTakeWindowsAccent()
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(new Argb(0xFF, 0x00, 0xE5, 0xFF), Resolve(Classic with { Theme = FenceTheme.Hud }, Plain).Accent);
+        Assert.Null(Resolve(Classic with { Theme = FenceTheme.Aurora }, Plain).Accent); // null: the window uses Windows' accent
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void Hud_UsesAMonoTitle_UnlessTheFenceChoseAFont()
+    {
+        Assert.Equal("Cascadia Mono", Resolve(Classic with { Theme = FenceTheme.Hud }, Plain).Font.Family);
+        var own = Plain with { Look = new OwnLook { Theme = FenceTheme.Hud, TitleFont = new TitleFont("Bahnschrift", null, null) } };
+        Assert.Equal("Bahnschrift", Resolve(Classic, own).Font.Family);

+    [Fact]
+    public void AnOldConfig_HasNotSeenTheThemesNote()
+    {
+        var config = ConfigJson.Deserialize("""{ "schemaVersion": 5, "settings": { "gameMode": true }, "fences": [] }""");
+        Assert.False(config.Settings.ThemesIntroduced);
+        Assert.Equal(FenceTheme.Classic, config.Settings.Appearance.Theme);
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>M41a: a hand-edited typo in a new setting is repaired, never a lost config.json (the M14/M36 rule).</summary>
+public class FeelTyposTests
+{
+    [Fact]
+    public void TyposInTheNewSettings_AreRepaired_NotALostFile()
+    {
+        var json = """
+            { "schemaVersion": 5,
+              "settings": { "openItemsWith": "tripleClick", "fenceMenu": "fancy",
+                            "appearance": { "theme": "matrix", "titleStyle": "gothic", "nameStyle": "huge", "motion": "wild" } },
+              "fences": [ { "id": "a", "title": "Apps", "openWith": "sometimes", "look": { "theme": "matrix", "titleStyle": "gothic" } } ] }
+            """;
+        var config = ConfigNormalizer.Normalize(ConfigJson.Deserialize(json));
+        Assert.Equal((OpenWith.OneClick, FenceMenuStyle.Guided), (config.Settings.OpenItemsWith, config.Settings.FenceMenu));
+        var appearance = config.Settings.Appearance;
+        Assert.Equal((FenceTheme.Classic, TitleStyle.Simple, NameStyle.Crisp, Motion.Lively), (appearance.Theme, appearance.TitleStyle, appearance.NameStyle, appearance.Motion));
+        Assert.Null(config.Fences[0].OpenWith);
+        Assert.Null(config.Fences[0].Look);
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Input/DesktopGestureTrackerTests.cs b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Input/DesktopGestureTrackerTests.cs
index bdc442d..4be8337 100644
--- a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Input/DesktopGestureTrackerTests.cs
+++ b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Input/DesktopGestureTrackerTests.cs
@@ -119,4 +119,48 @@ public class DesktopGestureTrackerTests
         Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.RightDragCancelled, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 600, 600, 9_000, overDesktop: true));
         Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.None, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.RightUp, 600, 600, 9_100, overDesktop: true));
     }
+
+    // ---------- M41a (spec §7): a click on empty wallpaper, for "Click wallpaper to reveal desktop" ----------
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void PressAndReleaseInPlaceOnDesktop_IsAClick()
+    {
+        var tracker = NewTracker();
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.None, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 100, 100, 1_000, overDesktop: true));
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.Click, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftUp, 101, 100, 1_080, overDesktop: true));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ADoubleClicksSecondRelease_IsNotAClick()
+    {
+        var tracker = NewTracker();
+        tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 100, 100, 1_000, overDesktop: true);
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.Click, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftUp, 100, 100, 1_050, overDesktop: true));
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.DoubleClick, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 100, 100, 1_200, overDesktop: true));
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.None, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftUp, 100, 100, 1_250, overDesktop: true));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ADragOnTheDesktop_IsNotAClick()
+    {
+        var tracker = NewTracker();
+        tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 100, 100, 1_000, overDesktop: true);
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.None, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftUp, 160, 140, 1_400, overDesktop: true));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void APressElsewhere_ReleasedOnTheDesktop_IsNotAClick()
+    {
+        var tracker = NewTracker();
+        tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 100, 100, 1_000, overDesktop: false);
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.None, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftUp, 100, 100, 1_050, overDesktop: true));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AReleaseOverAWindow_IsNotAClick()
+    {
+        var tracker = NewTracker();
+        tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftDown, 100, 100, 1_000, overDesktop: true);
+        Assert.Equal(DesktopGesture.None, tracker.OnMouse(MouseAction.LeftUp, 100, 100, 1_050, overDesktop: false));
+    }
 }
diff --git a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Items/WidgetsFeelTests.cs b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Items/WidgetsFeelTests.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9366432
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Items/WidgetsFeelTests.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,211 @@
+using System.Globalization;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Core.Tests.Items;
+
+/// <summary>M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6): widget sizes, the calendar, world clocks, Now playing.</summary>
+public class WidgetsFeelTests
+{
+    private static readonly CultureInfo English = CultureInfo.GetCultureInfo("en-GB");
+
+    // ---------- §6.2 sizes ----------
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(WidgetSize.Small, 2, 1)]
+    [InlineData(WidgetSize.Medium, 2, 2)]
+    [InlineData(WidgetSize.Large, 4, 2)]
+    public void EachSize_HasItsSpan(WidgetSize size, int columns, int rows) => Assert.Equal(new GridSpan(columns, rows), WidgetSizes.SpanOf(size));
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(WidgetKind.Clock, WidgetSize.Small)]
+    [InlineData(WidgetKind.Date, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(WidgetKind.Stats, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(WidgetKind.NowPlaying, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    public void WithoutAStoredSize_EachKindHasItsDefault(WidgetKind kind, WidgetSize size) => Assert.Equal(size, WidgetSizes.Of(null, kind));
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(1, 1, WidgetSize.Small)]
+    [InlineData(2, 1, WidgetSize.Small)]
+    [InlineData(1, 3, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(2, 2, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(2, 4, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(3, 1, WidgetSize.Large)]
+    [InlineData(4, 4, WidgetSize.Large)]
+    public void AnOldSpan_IsTheNearestSize(int columns, int rows, WidgetSize size) => Assert.Equal(size, WidgetSizes.Of(new GridSpan(columns, rows), WidgetKind.Stats));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AWidgetsLayoutSpan_IsItsSizesSpan_AnItemKeepsItsOwn()
+    {
+        var clock = new VirtualItem { Id = "c", Target = Widgets.Target(WidgetKind.Clock), Size = new GridSpan(3, 3) };
+        Assert.Equal(new GridSpan(4, 2), FenceGrid.SpanOf(clock));
+        Assert.Equal(new GridSpan(2, 1), FenceGrid.SpanOf(clock with { Size = null }));
+        Assert.Equal(new GridSpan(3, 3), FenceGrid.SpanOf(new VirtualItem { Id = "i", Target = @"C:\Games", Size = new GridSpan(3, 3) }));
+    }
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(1.2, 0.8, WidgetSize.Small)]
+    [InlineData(2.4, 1.3, WidgetSize.Small)]
+    [InlineData(2.4, 1.6, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(2.9, 2.8, WidgetSize.Medium)]
+    [InlineData(3.1, 1.2, WidgetSize.Large)]
+    [InlineData(4.6, 3.0, WidgetSize.Large)]
+    public void ACornerDrag_SnapsToTheNearestSize(double columns, double rows, WidgetSize size) => Assert.Equal(size, WidgetSizes.Snap(columns, rows));
+
+    // ---------- §6.2 calendar ----------
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void October2026_StartingMonday_HasFiveWeeks_TodayAndWeekendsMarked()
+    {
+        var month = CalendarMonth.Build(new DateOnly(2026, 10, 1), DayOfWeek.Monday, today: new DateOnly(2026, 10, 11));
+        Assert.Equal(35, month.Days.Count); // 1 October is a Thursday: three days of September before it
+        Assert.Equal((28, false), (month.Days[0].Day, month.Days[0].InMonth));
+        Assert.Equal((1, true), (month.Days[3].Day, month.Days[3].InMonth));
+        var today = Assert.Single(month.Days, day => day.Today);
+        Assert.Equal((11, true), (today.Day, today.Weekend)); // a Sunday
+        Assert.Equal((1, false), (month.Days[^1].Day, month.Days[^1].InMonth));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AMonthStartingOnSunday_TakesSixWeeks_WhenTheWeekStartsMonday()
+    {
+        var month = CalendarMonth.Build(new DateOnly(2026, 3, 1), DayOfWeek.Monday, today: new DateOnly(2026, 1, 1)); // 1 March 2026 is a Sunday
+        Assert.Equal(42, month.Days.Count);
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(month.Days, day => day.Today);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void TheWeekStart_FollowsTheSetting()
+    {
+        var sunday = CalendarMonth.Build(new DateOnly(2026, 10, 1), DayOfWeek.Sunday, today: new DateOnly(2026, 10, 11));
+        Assert.Equal(27, sunday.Days[0].Day); // Sunday 27 September
+        Assert.Equal(["S", "M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S"], CalendarMonth.Headers(English, DayOfWeek.Sunday));
+        Assert.Equal(["M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S", "S"], CalendarMonth.Headers(English, DayOfWeek.Monday));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void February2027_StartingMonday_FitsFourWeeks()
+    {
+        var month = CalendarMonth.Build(new DateOnly(2027, 2, 1), DayOfWeek.Monday, today: new DateOnly(2027, 2, 14));
+        Assert.Equal(28, month.Days.Count);
+        Assert.All(month.Days, day => Assert.True(day.InMonth));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ALeapFebruary_HasItsTwentyNinth() =>
+        Assert.Contains(CalendarMonth.Build(new DateOnly(2028, 2, 1), DayOfWeek.Monday, today: new DateOnly(2028, 1, 1)).Days, day => day is { Day: 29, InMonth: true });
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(2026, 10, 11, 41)]
+    [InlineData(2027, 1, 1, 53)] // ISO: 1 January 2027 still belongs to 2026's week 53
+    public void TheWeekNumber_IsIso(int year, int month, int day, int week) => Assert.Equal(week, CalendarMonth.WeekOf(new DateOnly(year, month, day)));
+
+    // ---------- §6.2 world clocks ----------
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AWorldClock_ShowsTheCitysTime()
+    {
+        var instant = new DateTimeOffset(2026, 10, 11, 0, 13, 0, TimeSpan.Zero); // London is on summer time (+1), Tokyo +9
+        Assert.Equal(("London", "01:13"), WorldClocks.Show("Europe/London", instant, English));
+        Assert.Equal(("Tokyo", "09:13"), WorldClocks.Show("Asia/Tokyo", instant, English));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnknownCities_AreDropped_AndAtMostTwoKept() =>
+        Assert.Equal(["Europe/London", "Asia/Tokyo"], WorldClocks.Normalize(["Europe/London", "Mars/Olympus", "Asia/Tokyo", "America/New_York"]));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void TheCityList_HasNamesAndKnownZones()
+    {
+        Assert.Contains(WorldClocks.Cities, city => city is { Id: "Asia/Kolkata", Name: "India (Kolkata)" } or { Id: "Asia/Kolkata" });
+        Assert.All(WorldClocks.Cities, city => Assert.NotNull(TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(city.Id)));
+    }
+
+    // ---------- §6.6 Now playing ----------
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(101, 243, "1:41", "−2:22")]
+    [InlineData(0, 59, "0:00", "−0:59")]
+    [InlineData(3725, 7200, "1:02:05", "−57:55")]
+    public void TheTimeline_ShowsElapsedAndLeft(int position, int end, string elapsed, string left) =>
+        Assert.Equal((elapsed, left, false), NowPlaying.Timeline(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(position), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(end)));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AStreamWithoutAnEnd_IsLive() => Assert.Equal(("", "", true), NowPlaying.Timeline(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(80), end: null));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void APositionPastTheEnd_IsClamped() => Assert.Equal(("4:03", "−0:00", false), NowPlaying.Timeline(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(300), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243)));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void NothingPlaying_ShowsTheLastSongDimmed_ElseSaysSo()
+    {
+        var song = new MediaTrack("Midnight City", "M83", "Hurry Up, We're Dreaming", "Spotify");
+        Assert.Equal(new NowPlayingFace(song, Idle: false, "Midnight City", "M83"), NowPlaying.Face(current: song, last: null));
+        Assert.Equal(new NowPlayingFace(song, Idle: true, "Nothing playing", "Last: Midnight City · M83"), NowPlaying.Face(current: null, last: song));
+        Assert.Equal(new NowPlayingFace(null, Idle: true, "Nothing playing", ""), NowPlaying.Face(current: null, last: null));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void NowPlaying_IsAWidgetKind() =>
+        Assert.Equal((WidgetKind.NowPlaying, "Now playing"), (Widgets.Of("neofences:widget/nowplaying"), Widgets.NameOf(WidgetKind.NowPlaying)));
+}
+
+/// <summary>M41a (spec §6.6): the live position between Windows' timeline updates, and the players' names.</summary>
+public class NowPlayingTimeTests
+{
+    private static readonly DateTimeOffset Updated = new(2026, 10, 11, 1, 0, 0, TimeSpan.Zero);
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void WhilePlaying_ThePositionRunsOnFromTheLastUpdate() =>
+        Assert.Equal(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(104), NowPlaying.LivePosition(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(101), Updated, Updated.AddSeconds(3), playing: true, end: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243)));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void Paused_ThePositionStays() =>
+        Assert.Equal(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(101), NowPlaying.LivePosition(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(101), Updated, Updated.AddSeconds(30), playing: false, end: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243)));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void ThePosition_NeverRunsPastTheEnd() =>
+        Assert.Equal(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243), NowPlaying.LivePosition(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(240), Updated, Updated.AddMinutes(5), playing: true, end: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243)));
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("Spotify.exe", "Spotify")]
+    [InlineData("SpotifyAB.SpotifyMusic_zpdnekdrzrea0!Spotify", "Spotify")]
+    [InlineData("Chrome", "Chrome")]
+    [InlineData("MSEdge", "Edge")]
+    [InlineData("Microsoft.ZuneMusic_8wekyb3d8bbwe!Microsoft.ZuneMusic", "Media Player")]
+    [InlineData("firefox.exe", "Firefox")]
+    [InlineData("vlc.exe", "VLC")]
+    [InlineData("SomeApp.exe", "SomeApp")]
+    [InlineData("", "A player")]
+    public void APlayersName_ComesFromItsAppId(string appId, string name) => Assert.Equal(name, NowPlaying.AppName(appId));
+}
+
+/// <summary>M41a (spec §6.5): widget-only fences may float (no glass, no title).</summary>
+public class WidgetBoardTests
+{
+    [Fact]
+    public void AFence_DoesNotFloatUntilAsked_AndTheSwitchSurvivesASave()
+    {
+        var fence = NeoFences.Core.Model.Fence.Create("Dashboard");
+        Assert.False(fence.WidgetsFloat);
+        var config = NeoFences.Core.Model.FenceEdits.SetWidgetsFloat(new NeoFences.Core.Model.NeoFencesConfig { Fences = [fence] }, fence.Id, true);
+        var restored = NeoFences.Core.Config.ConfigJson.Deserialize(NeoFences.Core.Config.ConfigJson.Serialize(config));
+        Assert.True(restored.Fences[0].WidgetsFloat);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void OnlyWidgets_IsAFenceOfWidgetsAlone()
+    {
+        string[] widgets = [Widgets.Target(WidgetKind.Clock), Widgets.Target(WidgetKind.Stats)];
+        Assert.True(WidgetBoards.OnlyWidgets(widgets));
+        Assert.False(WidgetBoards.OnlyWidgets([.. widgets, @"C:\Games"]));
+        Assert.False(WidgetBoards.OnlyWidgets([]));
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AClocksCities_AreRepairedWhenLoaded()
+    {
+        var clock = new VirtualItem { Id = "c", Target = Widgets.Target(WidgetKind.Clock), Widget = new WidgetOptions { Cities = ["Asia/Tokyo", "Nowhere/City", "Asia/Tokyo", "Europe/London", "UTC"] } };
+        var repaired = ItemEdits.Repair(new ItemsDocument().With("f", [clock])).Of("f")[0];
+        Assert.Equal(["Asia/Tokyo", "Europe/London"], repaired.Widget!.Cities);
+    }
+}

         Assert.Equal(new GridSpan(2, 2), FenceGrid.SpanOf(VirtualItem.Create(Widgets.Target(WidgetKind.Stats))));
-        Assert.Equal(new GridSpan(1, 1), FenceGrid.SpanOf(VirtualItem.Create(Widgets.Target(WidgetKind.Clock)) with { Size = GridSpan.One }));

 }

         Assert.True(new RunState(HideIcons: true, QuickHidden: true, Paused: false, GameMode: false, IconsHiddenByUser: true).IconsHidden);
+
+    // ---------- M41a (spec §7, ADR-066): a hidden desktop belongs to the user ----------
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void QuickHidden_TheRightButtonGoesStraightToWindows()
+    {
+        var state = new RunState(HideIcons: false, QuickHidden: true, Paused: false, GameMode: false) { DrawGesture = true };
+        Assert.True(state.MouseHookWanted); // still watching for the double-click that shows the fences again
+        Assert.False(state.HandlesRightButton);
+    }
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(true, true)]
+    [InlineData(false, false)]
+    public void Shown_TheRightButtonIsHandledOnlyForTheDrawGesture(bool drawGesture, bool handled) =>
+        Assert.Equal(handled, new RunState(HideIcons: false, QuickHidden: false, Paused: false, GameMode: false) { DrawGesture = drawGesture }.HandlesRightButton);
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void TheRevealGestureAlone_WantsTheHook()
+    {
+        var state = new RunState(HideIcons: false, QuickHidden: false, Paused: false, GameMode: false) { QuickHideGesture = false, DrawGesture = false, RevealGesture = true };
+        Assert.True(state.MouseHookWanted);
+        Assert.True(state.RevealWanted);
+    }
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(true, false, false)]
+    [InlineData(false, true, false)]
+    [InlineData(false, false, true)]
+    public void Reveal_OnlyWhileTheFencesShowAndNotGaming(bool quickHidden, bool gameMode, bool wanted) =>
+        Assert.Equal(wanted, new RunState(HideIcons: false, QuickHidden: quickHidden, Paused: false, GameMode: gameMode) { RevealGesture = true }.RevealWanted);
 }
diff --git a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/ClicksTests.cs b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/ClicksTests.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..684903c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/ClicksTests.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,67 @@
+using NeoFences.Core.Config;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Core.Tests.Model;
+
+/// <summary>M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §4, §5, §7): how items open, the fence menu's shape, the reveal switch.</summary>
+public class ClicksTests
+{
+    [Fact]
+    public void Defaults_OneClickOpens_GuidedMenu_RevealOff()
+    {
+        var settings = new Settings();
+        Assert.Equal((OpenWith.OneClick, FenceMenuStyle.Guided, false), (settings.OpenItemsWith, settings.FenceMenu, settings.RevealGesture));
+        Assert.Null(Fence.Create("Apps").OpenWith);
+    }
+
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData(null, OpenWith.OneClick, true)]
+    [InlineData(null, OpenWith.DoubleClick, false)]
+    [InlineData(OpenWith.OneClick, OpenWith.DoubleClick, true)]
+    [InlineData(OpenWith.DoubleClick, OpenWith.OneClick, false)]
+    public void AFencesOwnChoice_WinsOverAllFences(OpenWith? own, OpenWith all, bool oneClick) =>
+        Assert.Equal(oneClick, Clicks.OneClickOpens(Fence.Create("Games") with { OpenWith = own }, new Settings { OpenItemsWith = all }));
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void AnOldConfig_GetsTheDefaults()
+    {
+        var config = ConfigJson.Deserialize("""{ "schemaVersion": 5, "settings": { "gameMode": true }, "fences": [ { "id": "a", "title": "Apps" } ] }""");
+        Assert.Equal(OpenWith.OneClick, config.Settings.OpenItemsWith);
+        Assert.Equal(FenceMenuStyle.Guided, config.Settings.FenceMenu);
+        Assert.Null(config.Fences[0].OpenWith);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void HandEditedNumbers_AreRepaired()
+    {
+        var config = ConfigNormalizer.Normalize(new NeoFencesConfig
+        {
+            Settings = new Settings { OpenItemsWith = (OpenWith)7, FenceMenu = (FenceMenuStyle)9 },
+            Fences = [Fence.Create("Apps") with { OpenWith = (OpenWith)5 }],
+        });
+        Assert.Equal((OpenWith.OneClick, FenceMenuStyle.Guided), (config.Settings.OpenItemsWith, config.Settings.FenceMenu));
+        Assert.Null(config.Fences[0].OpenWith);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void TheChoices_SurviveASaveAndLoad()
+    {
+        var original = new NeoFencesConfig
+        {
+            Settings = new Settings { OpenItemsWith = OpenWith.DoubleClick, FenceMenu = FenceMenuStyle.Compact, RevealGesture = true },
+            Fences = [Fence.Create("Games") with { OpenWith = OpenWith.OneClick }],
+        };
+        var restored = ConfigJson.Deserialize(ConfigJson.Serialize(original));
+        Assert.Equal((OpenWith.DoubleClick, FenceMenuStyle.Compact, true), (restored.Settings.OpenItemsWith, restored.Settings.FenceMenu, restored.Settings.RevealGesture));
+        Assert.Equal(OpenWith.OneClick, restored.Fences[0].OpenWith);
+    }
+
+    [Fact]
+    public void FenceSettings_SetsAndClearsAFencesOwnChoice()
+    {
+        var games = Fence.Create("Games");
+        var config = FenceEdits.SetOpenWith(new NeoFencesConfig { Fences = [games] }, games.Id, OpenWith.DoubleClick);
+        Assert.Equal(OpenWith.DoubleClick, config.Fences[0].OpenWith);
+        Assert.Null(FenceEdits.SetOpenWith(config, games.Id, null).Fences[0].OpenWith);
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/FenceSettingsTests.cs b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/FenceSettingsTests.cs
index 3e25dff..af46781 100644
--- a/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/FenceSettingsTests.cs
+++ b/tests/NeoFences.Core.Tests/Model/FenceSettingsTests.cs
@@ -209,7 +209,7 @@ public class FenceSettingsTests
             },
         };
         var reset = SettingsReset.Apply(config);
-        Assert.Equal(new Settings(), reset.Settings);
+        Assert.Equal(Settings.NewInstall, reset.Settings); // M41a: as NeoFences comes now
         Assert.Equal(config.Fences, reset.Fences);
         Assert.Equal(config.Presets, reset.Presets);

```

```diff
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Core/Items/WidgetFeel.cs b/src/NeoFences.Core/Items/WidgetFeel.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b7b7b48
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Core/Items/WidgetFeel.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,150 @@
+using System.Globalization;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Core.Items;
+
+/// <summary>A widget's size (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6.2): three designed layouts, a bigger one shows more.</summary>
+public enum WidgetSize { Small, Medium, Large }
+
+/// <summary>Widget sizes as grid spans; old free spans map to the nearest size (nothing is rewritten until a resize).</summary>
+public static class WidgetSizes
+{
+    public static GridSpan SpanOf(WidgetSize size) => size switch
+    {
+        WidgetSize.Small => new GridSpan(2, 1),
+        WidgetSize.Large => new GridSpan(4, 2),
+        _ => new GridSpan(2, 2),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>A stored span's size: three columns or more is Large; else one row Small, more Medium. None: the kind's default.</summary>
+    public static WidgetSize Of(GridSpan? stored, WidgetKind kind) =>
+        stored is { } span ? span.Columns >= 3 ? WidgetSize.Large : span.Rows >= 2 ? WidgetSize.Medium : WidgetSize.Small
+        : kind == WidgetKind.Clock ? WidgetSize.Small : WidgetSize.Medium;
+
+    /// <summary>A corner drag (in cells, fractions included) snaps to the size it is nearest.</summary>
+    public static WidgetSize Snap(double columns, double rows) =>
+        columns >= 3 ? WidgetSize.Large : rows >= 1.5 ? WidgetSize.Medium : WidgetSize.Small;
+}
+
+/// <summary>One day of the calendar widget's month.</summary>
+public sealed record CalendarDay(int Day, bool InMonth, bool Today, bool Weekend);
+
+/// <summary>The calendar widget's month (M41a): whole weeks from the week's first day, as many as the month needs.</summary>
+public sealed record CalendarMonth(IReadOnlyList<CalendarDay> Days)
+{
+    public static CalendarMonth Build(DateOnly anyDayOfMonth, DayOfWeek firstDayOfWeek, DateOnly today)
+    {
+        var first = new DateOnly(anyDayOfMonth.Year, anyDayOfMonth.Month, 1);
+        var start = first.AddDays(-(((int)first.DayOfWeek - (int)firstDayOfWeek + 7) % 7));
+        var last = first.AddMonths(1).AddDays(-1);
+        var days = new List<CalendarDay>();
+        for (var day = start; day <= last || days.Count % 7 != 0; day = day.AddDays(1))
+            days.Add(new CalendarDay(day.Day, day.Month == first.Month, day == today, day.DayOfWeek is DayOfWeek.Saturday or DayOfWeek.Sunday));
+        return new CalendarMonth(days);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The weekday letters from the week's first day, in the culture's language.</summary>
+    public static IReadOnlyList<string> Headers(CultureInfo culture, DayOfWeek firstDayOfWeek) =>
+        [.. Enumerable.Range(0, 7).Select(offset => StringInfo.GetNextTextElement(culture.DateTimeFormat.ShortestDayNames[((int)firstDayOfWeek + offset) % 7]))];
+
+    public static int WeekOf(DateOnly date) => ISOWeek.GetWeekOfYear(date.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue));
+}
+
+/// <summary>A city for the clock's world clocks (M41a): its time zone (IANA id) and the name shown.</summary>
+public sealed record WorldCity(string Id, string Name);
+
+public static class WorldClocks
+{
+    public const int Max = 2;
+
+    /// <summary>The cities offered on the clock's back (a short, well-known list; any IANA zone stored by hand works too).</summary>
+    public static IReadOnlyList<WorldCity> Cities { get; } =
+    [
+        new("America/Los_Angeles", "Los Angeles"), new("America/Chicago", "Chicago"), new("America/New_York", "New York"),
+        new("America/Sao_Paulo", "São Paulo"), new("Europe/London", "London"), new("Europe/Paris", "Paris"), new("Europe/Berlin", "Berlin"),
+        new("Europe/Moscow", "Moscow"), new("Asia/Dubai", "Dubai"), new("Asia/Kolkata", "Kolkata"), new("Asia/Singapore", "Singapore"),
+        new("Asia/Shanghai", "Shanghai"), new("Asia/Seoul", "Seoul"), new("Asia/Tokyo", "Tokyo"), new("Australia/Sydney", "Sydney"),
+        new("Pacific/Auckland", "Auckland"), new("UTC", "UTC"),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>Known zones only, no repeats, at most <see cref="Max"/>.</summary>
+    public static IReadOnlyList<string> Normalize(IEnumerable<string>? ids) =>
+        [.. (ids ?? []).Where(Known).Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).Take(Max)];
+
+    /// <summary>The city's name and its time now, in the culture's short time format.</summary>
+    public static (string Name, string Time) Show(string id, DateTimeOffset now, CultureInfo culture)
+    {
+        var zone = TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(id);
+        var name = Cities.FirstOrDefault(city => string.Equals(city.Id, id, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))?.Name ?? id[(id.LastIndexOf('/') + 1)..].Replace('_', ' ');
+        return (name, TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(now, zone).ToString(culture.DateTimeFormat.ShortTimePattern, culture));
+    }
+
+    private static bool Known(string id)
+    {
+        try { return !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(id) && TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById(id) is not null; }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is TimeZoneNotFoundException or InvalidTimeZoneException) { return false; }
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>What plays now (M41a, spec §6.6): read from Windows' media sessions by the Shell.</summary>
+public sealed record MediaTrack(string Title, string Artist, string Album, string App);
+
+/// <summary>What the widget's face says: the track (or the last one, dimmed), its title and the line under it.</summary>
+public sealed record NowPlayingFace(MediaTrack? Track, bool Idle, string Title, string Subtitle);
+
+public static class NowPlaying
+{
+    private const char Minus = '−';
+
+    /// <summary>Elapsed and left ("1:41", "−2:22"); a stream with no end is live (no times).</summary>
+    public static (string Elapsed, string Left, bool Live) Timeline(TimeSpan position, TimeSpan? end)
+    {
+        if (end is not { } length || length <= TimeSpan.Zero) return ("", "", true);
+        var at = position < TimeSpan.Zero ? TimeSpan.Zero : position > length ? length : position;
+        return (Clock(at), $"{Minus}{Clock(length - at)}", false);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Nothing playing: the last song dimmed ("Last: …"), or just "Nothing playing".</summary>
+    public static NowPlayingFace Face(MediaTrack? current, MediaTrack? last) =>
+        current is { } playing ? new(playing, Idle: false, playing.Title, playing.Artist)
+        : last is { } earlier ? new(earlier, Idle: true, "Nothing playing", $"Last: {earlier.Title} · {earlier.Artist}")
+        : new(null, Idle: true, "Nothing playing", "");
+
+    /// <summary>Windows updates a player's timeline now and then: while playing, the position runs on from that update (to the end).</summary>
+    public static TimeSpan LivePosition(TimeSpan position, DateTimeOffset updated, DateTimeOffset now, bool playing, TimeSpan? end)
+    {
+        var live = playing && now > updated ? position + (now - updated) : position;
+        return end is { } length && length > TimeSpan.Zero && live > length ? length : live;
+    }
+
+    private static readonly (string Key, string Name)[] KnownApps =
+    [
+        ("spotify", "Spotify"), ("msedge", "Edge"), ("chrome", "Chrome"), ("firefox", "Firefox"), ("zunemusic", "Media Player"),
+        ("vlc", "VLC"), ("brave", "Brave"), ("opera", "Opera"), ("foobar2000", "foobar2000"), ("musicbee", "MusicBee"), ("itunes", "iTunes"),
+        ("applemusic", "Apple Music"), ("tidal", "TIDAL"), ("deezer", "Deezer"), ("amazonmusic", "Amazon Music"), ("youtubemusic", "YouTube Music"),
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A player's name from the app id Windows gives its media session ("Spotify.exe", a Store app's "Publisher.App_…!App"):
+    /// a known player's name, else the id's last part without ".exe".
+    /// </summary>
+    public static string AppName(string appId)
+    {
+        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(appId)) return "A player";
+        var lower = appId.ToLowerInvariant();
+        foreach (var (key, name) in KnownApps)
+            if (lower.Contains(key, StringComparison.Ordinal)) return name;
+        var last = appId[(appId.LastIndexOfAny(['!', '\\', '/']) + 1)..];
+        return last.EndsWith(".exe", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? last[..^4] : last;
+    }
+
+    private static string Clock(TimeSpan time) =>
+        time.TotalHours >= 1 ? $"{(int)time.TotalHours}:{time.Minutes:00}:{time.Seconds:00}" : $"{time.Minutes}:{time.Seconds:00}";
+}
+
+/// <summary>M41a (spec §6.5): fences that hold only widgets may float.</summary>
+public static class WidgetBoards
+{
+    /// <summary>The fence holds widgets and nothing else (by its items' targets).</summary>
+    public static bool OnlyWidgets(IReadOnlyCollection<string> targets) =>
+        targets.Count > 0 && targets.All(target => target.StartsWith(Widgets.TargetPrefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
+}

--- a/src/NeoFences.Core/Items/Widgets.cs
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Core/Items/Widgets.cs
@@ -3,7 +3,8 @@ using System.Globalization;
 namespace NeoFences.Core.Items;
 
 /// <summary>The widgets a fence can hold (M25, spec 2026-10-05-widgets-design).</summary>
-public enum WidgetKind { Clock, Date, Stats }
+/// <summary>M41a: Date is shown as the Calendar (its Small size is the old date page); Now playing is new.</summary>
+public enum WidgetKind { Clock, Date, Stats, NowPlaying }
 
 /// <summary>A widget's options: the clock's seconds and date line (M25); the stats' temperatures in °F (M31).</summary>
 public sealed record WidgetOptions

+namespace NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+/// <summary>How items open (M41a, ADR-064): one click (the default) or Windows' double click.</summary>
+public enum OpenWith { OneClick, DoubleClick }
+
+/// <summary>The fence menu's shape (M41a, spec §5): sections with headings (the default), or an icon row and a short list.</summary>
+public enum FenceMenuStyle { Guided, Compact }
+
+public static class Clicks
+{
+    /// <summary>A fence's own "Open items with" wins over Settings' (null: like all fences).</summary>
+    public static bool OneClickOpens(Fence fence, Settings settings) => (fence.OpenWith ?? settings.OpenItemsWith) == OpenWith.OneClick;
+}

diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Core/Model/Titles.cs b/src/NeoFences.Core/Model/Titles.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f7d2d16
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Core/Model/Titles.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,26 @@
+using System.Globalization;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+/// <summary>A fence's emoji (M41a, spec §2): one grapheme — what a person sees as one character — or none.</summary>
+public static class Emoji
+{
+    public static string? Normalize(string? typed)
+    {
+        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(typed)) return null;
+        var first = StringInfo.GetNextTextElement(typed.Trim());
+        return first.Length is 0 or > 16 ? null : first; // 16 UTF-16 units: the longest family or flag sequences fit
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>What a fence's title shows besides its name (M41a, spec §2).</summary>
+public static class Titles
+{
+    /// <summary>The count beside the title: the fence's items by their targets, widgets left out; empty when there are none.</summary>
+    public static string CountText(IEnumerable<string> targets, CultureInfo culture)
+    {
+        var count = targets.Count(target => !target.StartsWith(Widgets.TargetPrefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
+        return count == 0 ? "" : count.ToString(culture);
+    }
+}
```

```diff
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceHost.Clicks.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceHost.Clicks.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..07fd6c3
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceHost.Clicks.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+using System.Windows;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+using Serilog;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §4–5, ADR-064): one click opens (Settings and each fence's own choice), and the
+/// fence menu's shape.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed partial class FenceHost
+{
+    private void SetOpenItemsWith(OpenWith openWith)
+    {
+        _config = _config with { Settings = _config.Settings with { OpenItemsWith = openWith } };
+        Log.Information("open items with: {OpenWith}", openWith);
+        RestyleAll(); // each window takes its fence's choice (ApplyStyle)
+        ScheduleSave();
+        RefreshSettings();
+        RefreshAllFenceSettings(); // "Like all fences (…)" names the new default
+    }
+
+    private void SetFenceMenu(FenceMenuStyle style)
+    {
+        _config = _config with { Settings = _config.Settings with { FenceMenu = style } };
+        Log.Information("fence menu: {Style}", style);
+        RestyleAll();
+        ScheduleSave();
+        RefreshSettings();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// M41a (spec §1): a setup from before rc.3 keeps its look, so once — not in a game — the top-left fence says the themes
+    /// are there, with a button to Fence settings. A new install starts with Aurora and never sees it.
+    /// </summary>
+    private void IntroduceThemes()
+    {
+        if (_config.Settings.ThemesIntroduced || _gameMode || SafeMode || !Current.FencesVisible) return;
+        if (_windows.Values.Where(window => window.IsVisible).OrderBy(window => window.Left + window.Top).FirstOrDefault() is not { } first) return;
+        first.ShowTip("New: fence themes — Aurora glass, Game HUD, Neon, Light Mica", "Try them", () => OpenFenceSettings(first.FenceId));
+        _config = _config with { Settings = _config.Settings with { ThemesIntroduced = true } };
+        Log.Information("themes introduced");
+        ScheduleSave();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>What a window needs besides its look: how its items open, its menu's shape, the names' style and the motion.</summary>
+    private void ApplyFeel(FenceWindow window, Fence shown)
+    {
+        window.OneClickOpens = Clicks.OneClickOpens(shown, _config.Settings);
+        window.MenuStyle = _config.Settings.FenceMenu;
+        window.NameStyle = Appearance.NameStyle;
+        window.Motion = EffectiveMotion;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>M41a (spec §3): Settings' motion, off in a game, in safe mode and with Windows' animation effects off.</summary>
+    private Motion EffectiveMotion =>
+        MotionPolicy.Effective(Appearance.Motion, gameMode: _gameMode, windowsAnimations: SystemParameters.ClientAreaAnimation, safeMode: SafeMode);
+}

     private void RefreshAllFenceSettings()
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceHost.Media.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceHost.Media.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ccca823
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceHost.Media.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+using System.IO;
+using System.Windows.Media;
+using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+using NeoFences.Shell;
+using Serilog;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Now playing (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6.6, ADR-065): Windows' media sessions are read on the widget tick
+/// while a Now playing widget can be seen (one read at a time, off the UI thread); covers are read once per track and kept
+/// in memory only. Nothing is read in a game, paused, hidden or in safe mode (the tick does not run then).
+/// </summary>
+public sealed partial class FenceHost
+{
+    private MediaSessions? _mediaSessions;
+    private bool _mediaInFlight;
+    private IReadOnlyList<MediaSessionInfo> _sessions = [];
+    private MediaTrack? _lastTrack;
+    private readonly Dictionary<string, string> _mediaChoice = new(StringComparer.Ordinal); // a widget's chosen player (this run)
+    private readonly Dictionary<string, (string Key, ImageSource? Cover, ImageSource? Glow)> _covers = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
+
+    /// <summary>From the widget tick: a fresh reading when a Now playing widget is shown, then every such widget updated.</summary>
+    private void TickMedia(IReadOnlyList<FenceWindow> windows)
+    {
+        var showing = windows.Where(window => window.ShowsWidget(WidgetKind.NowPlaying)).ToList();
+        if (showing.Count == 0) return;
+        foreach (var window in showing) window.ShowMedia(MediaFor); // the position runs on between readings
+        if (_mediaInFlight) return;
+        _mediaInFlight = true;
+        _mediaSessions ??= new MediaSessions(message => Log.Warning("{Message}", message));
+        var sessions = _mediaSessions;
+        var dispatcher = System.Windows.Threading.Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher;
+        Task.Run(sessions.ReadAsync).ContinueWith(read => dispatcher.BeginInvoke(() =>
+        {
+            _mediaInFlight = false;
+            _sessions = read.IsCompletedSuccessfully ? read.Result : [];
+            if (_sessions.FirstOrDefault()?.Track is { Title.Length: > 0 } playing) _lastTrack = playing;
+            foreach (var session in _sessions.Where(session => !_covers.TryGetValue(session.AppId, out var known) || known.Key != session.CoverKey)) ReadCover(session);
+            foreach (var window in WidgetWindows()) window.ShowMedia(MediaFor);
+        }));
+    }
+
+    private NowPlayingData MediaFor(string widgetKey)
+    {
+        var shown = _mediaChoice.TryGetValue(widgetKey, out var chosen) && _sessions.FirstOrDefault(session => session.AppId == chosen) is { } picked
+            ? picked : _sessions.FirstOrDefault();
+        var face = NowPlaying.Face(current: shown?.Track, last: _lastTrack);
+        var art = shown is not null && _covers.TryGetValue(shown.AppId, out var cover) ? cover : default;
+        return new NowPlayingData(_sessions, shown, face, art.Cover, art.Glow);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The track's cover, once per track: decoded for the sleeve, and a few pixels wide for the glow.</summary>
+    private void ReadCover(MediaSessionInfo session)
+    {
+        _covers[session.AppId] = (session.CoverKey, _covers.GetValueOrDefault(session.AppId).Cover, _covers.GetValueOrDefault(session.AppId).Glow); // asked once
+        var sessions = _mediaSessions!;
+        var dispatcher = System.Windows.Threading.Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher;
+        Task.Run(() => sessions.CoverAsync(session.AppId)).ContinueWith(read => dispatcher.BeginInvoke(() =>
+        {
+            if (!read.IsCompletedSuccessfully || read.Result is not { } bytes) { _covers[session.AppId] = (session.CoverKey, null, null); return; }
+            _covers[session.AppId] = (session.CoverKey, Decode(bytes, width: 300), Decode(bytes, width: 6));
+            foreach (var window in WidgetWindows()) window.ShowMedia(MediaFor);
+        }));
+    }
+
+    private static ImageSource? Decode(byte[] bytes, int width)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            var image = new BitmapImage();
+            image.BeginInit();
+            image.StreamSource = new MemoryStream(bytes);
+            image.DecodePixelWidth = width;
+            image.CacheOption = BitmapCacheOption.OnLoad;
+            image.EndInit();
+            image.Freeze();
+            return image;
+        }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException)
+        {
+            Log.Warning("now playing: a cover could not be decoded ({Message})", failure.Message);
+            return null;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A widget's button: the command to the player (then a fresh reading), or (no command) the player to the front.</summary>
+    private void OnMediaRequested(string appId, MediaCommand? command, TimeSpan? at)
+    {
+        if (command is not { } asked)
+        {
+            PlayerWindows.BringToFront(appId, log: message => Log.Information("{Message}", message));
+            return;
+        }
+        var sessions = _mediaSessions;
+        if (sessions is null) return;
+        var dispatcher = System.Windows.Threading.Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher;
+        Task.Run(() => sessions.CommandAsync(appId, asked, at)).ContinueWith(_ => dispatcher.BeginInvoke(() => TickMedia(WidgetWindows())));
+    }
+
+    private void OnMediaPlayerChosen(string widgetKey, string appId)
+    {
+        _mediaChoice[widgetKey] = appId;
+        foreach (var window in WidgetWindows()) window.ShowMedia(MediaFor);
+    }
+}

+    private DispatcherTimer? _revealTimer;

+    /// <summary>
+    /// M41a (ADR-066): a click on empty wallpaper shows the desktop (or brings the windows back) once Windows' double-click
+    /// time has passed without a second click — a double-click stays quick-hide. Not over a visible native icon (its click

+    /// </summary>
+    private void ScheduleReveal(int screenX, int screenY)
+    {
+        _revealTimer?.Stop();
+        _revealTimer = new DispatcherTimer { Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(DesktopReveal.DoubleClickTime) };

+        {
+            _revealTimer?.Stop();
+            if (!Current.RevealWanted) return;

+            if (!DesktopReveal.Set(revealed: !_revealed, log: message => Log.Warning("{Message}", message))) return;

+            Log.Information("reveal desktop: {Revealed}", _revealed);
+        };
+        _revealTimer.Start();
+    }
 }

+                                <TextBlock x:Name="TitleStyleOwn" Text="This fence's own" Style="{StaticResource Description}" Foreground="{DynamicResource AccentTextFillColorPrimaryBrush}" Visibility="Collapsed" />
+                            </StackPanel>
+                        </DockPanel>
+                        <DockPanel Margin="0,8,0,0">
+                            <TextBox x:Name="EmojiBox" DockPanel.Dock="Right" Width="60" MaxLength="16" FontFamily="Segoe UI Emoji" FontSize="16" HorizontalContentAlignment="Center"
+                                     VerticalAlignment="Center" AutomationProperties.Name="Emoji" />
+                            <StackPanel VerticalAlignment="Center">
+                                <TextBlock Text="Emoji" />
+                                <TextBlock Text="Before the title (in the pill with the Pill style). Pick one or type your own; empty for none." Style="{StaticResource Description}" TextWrapping="Wrap" />
+                            </StackPanel>
+                        </DockPanel>
+                        <WrapPanel x:Name="EmojiPicks" Margin="0,6,0,0" />
+                    </StackPanel>
+                </Border>
                 <Border Style="{StaticResource Card}">
                     <DockPanel>
                         <ComboBox x:Name="StyleBox" DockPanel.Dock="Right" Width="210" VerticalAlignment="Center" AutomationProperties.Name="Colour style" />
@@ -43,7 +71,7 @@
                         <DockPanel>
                             <Button x:Name="StrengthLikeAllButton" Content="Like all fences" DockPanel.Dock="Right" Visibility="Collapsed" />
                             <StackPanel VerticalAlignment="Center">
-                                <TextBlock Text="Background" />
+                                <TextBlock Text="Transparency" />
                                 <TextBlock x:Name="StrengthDescription" Style="{StaticResource Description}" />
                             </StackPanel>
                         </DockPanel>
@@ -51,7 +79,7 @@

+                        <!-- M41a (spec §6.5): a fence of widgets drops its glass and title. -->
```
