diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceThemeLayer.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceThemeLayer.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3848341
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceThemeLayer.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,196 @@
+using System.Windows;
+using System.Windows.Controls;
+using System.Windows.Media;
+using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
+using System.Windows.Media.Imaging;
+using System.Windows.Shapes;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// What a theme draws behind a fence's items besides the veil (M41a, ADR-063, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §1):
+/// Aurora's drifting colour light, HUD's cut corners and scan line, Neon's breathing edge, Light Mica's grain and highlight.
+/// Everything is brushes and shapes (no per-frame effects: a layered window renders effects in software); what moves runs at
+/// most <see cref="FramesPerSecond"/> frames a second, only while motion is on and the fence shows.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class FenceThemeLayer
+{
+    public const int FramesPerSecond = 20;
+    private const double Chamfer = 12;
+
+    private readonly Grid _host;
+    private FenceTheme _theme;
+    private Color _accent;
+    private Brush _veil = Brushes.Transparent;
+    private Motion _motion = Motion.Off;
+    private bool _paused;
+    private readonly List<(IAnimatable Target, DependencyProperty Property, Func<double, AnimationTimeline> Make)> _moving = [];
+    private Path? _hudFrame;
+    private Rectangle? _scan;
+
+    public FenceThemeLayer(Grid host)
+    {
+        _host = host;
+        _host.SizeChanged += (_, _) => Rebuild();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>HUD draws the fence's veil itself, inside its cut-corner frame (the window's rounded frame stays empty).</summary>
+    public bool DrawsVeil => _theme == FenceTheme.Hud;
+
+    public void Apply(FenceTheme theme, Color accent, Brush veil, Motion motion)
+    {
+        if (theme == _theme && accent == _accent && Equals(veil, _veil) && motion == _motion) return;
+        (_theme, _accent, _veil, _motion) = (theme, accent, veil, motion);
+        Rebuild();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Hidden, rolled up or gaming: nothing moves; shown again: it moves on.</summary>
+    public void Pause(bool paused)
+    {
+        if (paused == _paused) return;
+        _paused = paused;
+        Animate();
+    }
+
+    private void Rebuild()
+    {
+        _host.Children.Clear();
+        _moving.Clear();
+        _hudFrame = null;
+        _scan = null;
+        var (width, height) = (_host.ActualWidth, _host.ActualHeight);
+        if (width < 1 || height < 1) return;
+        _host.Clip = _theme == FenceTheme.Hud ? null : new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(0, 0, width, height), 7, 7);
+        switch (_theme)
+        {
+            case FenceTheme.Aurora: BuildAurora(width, height); break;
+            case FenceTheme.Hud: BuildHud(width, height); break;
+            case FenceTheme.Neon: BuildNeon(); break;
+            case FenceTheme.LightMica: BuildMica(); break;
+        }
+        Animate();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Three soft lights in the accent and two neighbours, drifting slowly (one loop about 20 s).</summary>
+    private void BuildAurora(double width, double height)
+    {
+        Color[] lights = [_accent, Mix(_accent, Color.FromRgb(0x9B, 0x7B, 0xFF), 0.55), Mix(_accent, Color.FromRgb(0xFF, 0x7A, 0xB4), 0.55)];
+        (double X, double Y, double DriftX, double DriftY, double Seconds)[] places =
+            [(-0.25, -0.35, 0.22, 0.18, 21), (0.45, 0.25, -0.2, -0.16, 17), (-0.1, 0.55, 0.16, -0.22, 25)];
+        for (var index = 0; index < lights.Length; index++)
+        {
+            var place = places[index];
+            var size = Math.Max(width, height) * 0.9;
+            var light = new Ellipse
+            {
+                Width = size, Height = size, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
+                Fill = new RadialGradientBrush(Color.FromArgb(0x5C, lights[index].R, lights[index].G, lights[index].B), Colors.Transparent),
+                Opacity = index == 0 ? 1 : 0.8,
+            };
+            var move = new TranslateTransform(width * place.X, height * place.Y);
+            light.RenderTransform = move;
+            _host.Children.Add(light);
+            Drift(move, TranslateTransform.XProperty, move.X, move.X + width * place.DriftX, place.Seconds);
+            Drift(move, TranslateTransform.YProperty, move.Y, move.Y + height * place.DriftY, place.Seconds * 0.83);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A cut-corner frame in the accent, faint corner ticks, and a thin scan line sweeping down every ~8 s.</summary>
+    private void BuildHud(double width, double height)
+    {
+        var figure = new PathFigure { StartPoint = new Point(Chamfer, 0.5), IsClosed = true };
+        foreach (var point in new[] { new Point(width - 0.5, 0.5), new Point(width - 0.5, height - Chamfer), new Point(width - Chamfer, height - 0.5),
+                     new Point(0.5, height - 0.5), new Point(0.5, Chamfer) })
+            figure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(point, isStroked: true));
+        var accent = new SolidColorBrush(_accent);
+        _hudFrame = new Path { Data = new PathGeometry([figure]), Fill = _veil, Stroke = accent, StrokeThickness = 1 };
+        _host.Children.Add(_hudFrame);
+        var tick = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(0xB0, _accent.R, _accent.G, _accent.B));
+        _host.Children.Add(new Path { Data = Geometry.Parse($"M {width - 22},5 H {width - 5} V 22 M 5,{height - 22} V {height - 5} H 22"), Stroke = tick, StrokeThickness = 2 });
+        _scan = new Rectangle
+        {
+            Height = 36, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top, Margin = new Thickness(1, 0, 1, 0),
+            Fill = new LinearGradientBrush(new GradientStopCollection
+            {
+                new(Colors.Transparent, 0), new(Color.FromArgb(0x38, _accent.R, _accent.G, _accent.B), 0.85), new(Color.FromArgb(0x70, _accent.R, _accent.G, _accent.B), 1),
+            }, new Point(0, 0), new Point(0, 1)),
+            RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform(0, -40),
+        };
+        _host.Children.Add(_scan);
+        var sweep = (TranslateTransform)_scan.RenderTransform;
+        _moving.Add((sweep, TranslateTransform.YProperty, speed =>
+        {
+            var seconds = 8 / speed;
+            var frames = new DoubleAnimationUsingKeyFrames { Duration = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds), RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever };
+            frames.KeyFrames.Add(new DiscreteDoubleKeyFrame(-40, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.Zero)));
+            frames.KeyFrames.Add(new EasingDoubleKeyFrame(height, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds * 0.3)), new SineEase()));
+            frames.KeyFrames.Add(new DiscreteDoubleKeyFrame(height, KeyTime.FromTimeSpan(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds))));
+            return frames;
+        }));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A lit accent edge with a soft inner glow that breathes (4 s in, 4 s out).</summary>
+    private void BuildNeon()
+    {
+        var glow = new Grid();
+        foreach (var (thickness, alpha) in new[] { (9.0, (byte)0x14), (5.0, (byte)0x26), (2.5, (byte)0x48) })
+            glow.Children.Add(new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8), BorderThickness = new Thickness(thickness), BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(alpha, _accent.R, _accent.G, _accent.B)) });
+        glow.Children.Add(new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8), BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.5), BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(_accent) });
+        _host.Children.Add(glow);
+        _moving.Add((glow, UIElement.OpacityProperty, speed =>
+            new DoubleAnimation(0.55, 1, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4 / speed)) { AutoReverse = true, RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever, EasingFunction = new SineEase() }));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Fine grain and a top highlight: bright glass with some depth. Nothing moves.</summary>
+    private void BuildMica()
+    {
+        _host.Children.Add(new Rectangle { Fill = Grain.Value, Opacity = 0.06 });
+        _host.Children.Add(new Rectangle
+        {
+            Height = 44, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
+            Fill = new LinearGradientBrush(Color.FromArgb(0x99, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF), Color.FromArgb(0x00, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF), 90),
+        });
+    }
+
+    private void Drift(IAnimatable target, DependencyProperty property, double from, double to, double seconds) =>
+        _moving.Add((target, property, speed => new DoubleAnimation(from, to, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(seconds / speed))
+        {
+            AutoReverse = true, RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever, EasingFunction = new SineEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseInOut },
+        }));
+
+    /// <summary>Calm runs at half speed; Off or paused stops every animation where it is.</summary>
+    private void Animate()
+    {
+        var running = !_paused && _motion != Motion.Off;
+        var speed = _motion == Motion.Calm ? 0.5 : 1;
+        foreach (var (target, property, make) in _moving)
+        {
+            if (!running)
+            {
+                target.BeginAnimation(property, null);
+                continue;
+            }
+            var animation = make(speed);
+            Timeline.SetDesiredFrameRate(animation, FramesPerSecond);
+            target.BeginAnimation(property, animation);
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static Color Mix(Color first, Color second, double amount) => Color.FromRgb(
+        (byte)(first.R + (second.R - first.R) * amount), (byte)(first.G + (second.G - first.G) * amount), (byte)(first.B + (second.B - first.B) * amount));
+
+    /// <summary>A 64 px tile of soft noise, made once.</summary>
+    private static readonly Lazy<ImageBrush> Grain = new(() =>
+    {
+        const int size = 64;
+        var pixels = new byte[size * size];
+        var random = new Random(41);
+        random.NextBytes(pixels);
+        var bitmap = BitmapSource.Create(size, size, 96, 96, PixelFormats.Gray8, null, pixels, size);
+        bitmap.Freeze();
+        var brush = new ImageBrush(bitmap) { TileMode = TileMode.Tile, Viewport = new Rect(0, 0, size, size), ViewportUnits = BrushMappingMode.Absolute };
+        brush.Freeze();
+        return brush;
+    });
+}
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.Motion.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.Motion.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..05ec478
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.Motion.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,160 @@
+using System.Windows;
+using System.Windows.Controls;
+using System.Windows.Input;
+using System.Windows.Media;
+using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
+using System.Windows.Media.Effects;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §3, ADR-063): how items answer the mouse. Calm: a 2 px lift with a soft accent
+/// glow, a dip when pressed, items fading in as they arrive. Lively adds a slight tilt and a light sweep on hover, a ripple
+/// on press, and items rising in one after another. Off (also in a game, in safe mode, with Windows' animation effects off):
+/// nothing moves. Only render transforms and brushes move — no layout passes; the one glow is on the hovered item only.
+/// </summary>
+public partial class FenceWindow
+{
+    private static readonly Duration Quick = new(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(160));
+    private static readonly IEasingFunction Spring = new BackEase { Amplitude = 0.35, EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut };
+
+    static FenceWindow() =>
+        EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(System.Windows.Controls.ToolTip), System.Windows.Controls.ToolTip.OpenedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(OnToolTipOpened));
+
+    private void InitializeMotion()
+    {
+        // handledEventsToo: the one-click press handles the event, the dip must still play.
+        ItemList.AddHandler(PreviewMouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler((_, press) => PressFeedback(press, pressed: true)), handledEventsToo: true);
+        ItemList.AddHandler(PreviewMouseLeftButtonUpEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler((_, release) => PressFeedback(release, pressed: false)), handledEventsToo: true);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The chrome that moves; none for covers (they lift themselves, M34) and folder panels (too big to tilt).</summary>
+    private static Border? ChromeOf(ListBoxItem container) =>
+        container.DataContext is FenceItemView { IsTile: false, IsPanel: false } ? container.Template?.FindName("Chrome", container) as Border : null;
+
+    /// <summary>The chrome's own transforms, made once: a lift, a press scale and a tilt.</summary>
+    private static (TranslateTransform Lift, ScaleTransform Press, RotateTransform Tilt) TransformsOf(FrameworkElement element)
+    {
+        if (element.RenderTransform is TransformGroup { Children: [TranslateTransform lift, ScaleTransform press, RotateTransform tilt] }) return (lift, press, tilt);
+        (lift, press, tilt) = (new TranslateTransform(), new ScaleTransform(1, 1), new RotateTransform());
+        element.RenderTransform = new TransformGroup { Children = [lift, press, tilt] };
+        element.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0.5, 0.5);
+        return (lift, press, tilt);
+    }
+
+    private static void To(IAnimatable target, DependencyProperty property, double value, Duration duration, IEasingFunction? easing = null)
+    {
+        var animation = new DoubleAnimation(value, duration) { EasingFunction = easing };
+        target.BeginAnimation(property, animation);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Hover: lift and glow (Calm); a tilt toward the pointer and a light sweep across the icon too (Lively).</summary>
+    private void LiftItem(ListBoxItem container, bool lifted)
+    {
+        if (ChromeOf(container) is not { } chrome) return;
+        if (_motion == Motion.Off)
+        {
+            if (chrome.Effect is not null) chrome.Effect = null;
+            return;
+        }
+        var (lift, _, tilt) = TransformsOf(chrome);
+        To(lift, TranslateTransform.YProperty, lifted ? -2 : 0, Quick, Spring);
+        chrome.Effect = lifted && _style is { } style ? Glow(AccentColour(style), radius: 14, opacity: 0.45) : null;
+        if (_motion != Motion.Lively) return;
+        var toward = lifted ? Math.Clamp((Mouse.GetPosition(chrome).X / Math.Max(1, chrome.ActualWidth) - 0.5) * 3, -1.5, 1.5) : 0;
+        To(tilt, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, toward, Quick, Spring);
+        if (lifted) Sweep(chrome);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Lively: a soft band of light crosses the item once (the hover tint stays underneath).</summary>
+    private void Sweep(Border chrome)
+    {
+        var tint = (Resources["FenceHover"] as SolidColorBrush)?.Color ?? Colors.Transparent;
+        var band = Color.FromArgb(0x38, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF);
+        var move = new TranslateTransform(-1, 0);
+        chrome.Background = new LinearGradientBrush(new GradientStopCollection { new(tint, 0.3), new(band, 0.5), new(tint, 0.7) }, new Point(0, 0), new Point(1, 1))
+        {
+            RelativeTransform = move,
+        };
+        var sweep = new DoubleAnimation(-1, 1, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(650))) { EasingFunction = new SineEase() };
+        sweep.Completed += (_, _) => chrome.ClearValue(Border.BackgroundProperty); // the template's hover / selection again
+        move.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.XProperty, sweep);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Press: a dip to 96 % and back (Calm, Lively); a ripple from the pointer (Lively).</summary>
+    private void PressFeedback(MouseButtonEventArgs press, bool pressed)
+    {
+        if (_motion == Motion.Off || press.OriginalSource is not DependencyObject source || FindAncestor<ListBoxItem>(source) is not { } container
+            || ChromeOf(container) is not { } chrome) return;
+        var (_, scale, _) = TransformsOf(chrome);
+        To(scale, ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, pressed ? 0.96 : 1, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(pressed ? 80 : 180)), pressed ? null : Spring);
+        To(scale, ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, pressed ? 0.96 : 1, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(pressed ? 80 : 180)), pressed ? null : Spring);
+        if (!pressed || _motion != Motion.Lively || _style is not { } style) return;
+        var accent = AccentColour(style);
+        var at = press.GetPosition(chrome);
+        var ripple = new RadialGradientBrush(Color.FromArgb(0x55, accent.R, accent.G, accent.B), Color.FromArgb(0, accent.R, accent.G, accent.B))
+        {
+            Center = new Point(at.X / Math.Max(1, chrome.ActualWidth), at.Y / Math.Max(1, chrome.ActualHeight)),
+            GradientOrigin = new Point(at.X / Math.Max(1, chrome.ActualWidth), at.Y / Math.Max(1, chrome.ActualHeight)),
+            RadiusX = 0.05, RadiusY = 0.05,
+        };
+        chrome.Background = ripple;
+        var grow = new DoubleAnimation(0.05, 1.1, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(420))) { EasingFunction = new QuadraticEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut } };
+        grow.Completed += (_, _) => chrome.ClearValue(Border.BackgroundProperty);
+        ripple.BeginAnimation(RadialGradientBrush.RadiusXProperty, grow);
+        ripple.BeginAnimation(RadialGradientBrush.RadiusYProperty, grow);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Items arriving (start, a tab switch, items added, the fences shown again): they fade in (Calm), rising one after another (Lively).</summary>
+    private void Arrive(IReadOnlyCollection<FenceItemView> views)
+    {
+        if (_motion == Motion.Off || views.Count == 0 || !IsVisible) return;
+        Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(System.Windows.Threading.DispatcherPriority.Loaded, () =>
+        {
+            var index = 0;
+            foreach (var view in views)
+            {
+                if (ItemList.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(view) is not ListBoxItem container) continue;
+                var delay = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(_motion == Motion.Lively ? Math.Min(index++ * 25, 300) : 0);
+                container.BeginAnimation(OpacityProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0, 1, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(220))) { BeginTime = delay, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop });
+                if (_motion != Motion.Lively) continue;
+                var rise = container.RenderTransform as TranslateTransform ?? new TranslateTransform();
+                container.RenderTransform = rise;
+                rise.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.YProperty, new DoubleAnimation(6, 0, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(260)))
+                {
+                    BeginTime = delay, EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut }, FillBehavior = FillBehavior.Stop,
+                });
+            }
+        });
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A tooltip over a fence grows in from 96 % with its fade (Lively and Calm).</summary>
+    private static void OnToolTipOpened(object sender, RoutedEventArgs opened)
+    {
+        if (sender is not System.Windows.Controls.ToolTip { PlacementTarget: { } target } tip || Window.GetWindow(target) is not FenceWindow { Motion: not Motion.Off }) return;
+        var grow = new ScaleTransform(0.96, 0.96);
+        tip.RenderTransform = grow;
+        tip.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0.5, 0);
+        var duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(140));
+        grow.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, new DoubleAnimation(1, duration) { EasingFunction = Spring });
+        grow.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, new DoubleAnimation(1, duration) { EasingFunction = Spring });
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Title bar on hover (M36) fades in and out over 150 ms with motion on; at once with it off.</summary>
+    private void FadeTitle(double opacity)
+    {
+        foreach (var element in new UIElement[] { TitleBar, TitleStripFill, TitleColorBar })
+        {
+            if (_motion == Motion.Off) { element.BeginAnimation(OpacityProperty, null); element.Opacity = opacity; }
+            else element.BeginAnimation(OpacityProperty, new DoubleAnimation(opacity, new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(150))));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static DropShadowEffect Glow(Color colour, double radius, double opacity)
+    {
+        var glow = new DropShadowEffect { Color = colour, ShadowDepth = 0, BlurRadius = radius, Opacity = opacity };
+        glow.Freeze();
+        return glow;
+    }
+}
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.Widgets.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.Widgets.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a281510
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.Widgets.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,281 @@
+using System.Windows;
+using System.Windows.Controls;
+using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
+using System.Windows.Input;
+using System.Windows.Media;
+using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6.3–6.4): adding widgets from a gallery that opens from the fence (the "+" on the
+/// title, the menu's Widget…), and setting one up on its back — the widget flips over to its options and its size, Done
+/// flips it back. Both are NeoFences' own popups in the menus' colours (MenuTheme), mouse-only (a fence never takes the
+/// keyboard outside Peek: activating it would break Win+D, rc.2). The gallery is drawn in the fence; the back is a popup.
+/// </summary>
+public partial class FenceWindow
+{
+    /// <summary>A widget's back changed its options (the item's key, the options).</summary>
+    public event Action<string, WidgetOptions>? WidgetOptionsChanged;
+
+    /// <summary>Small, Medium or Large chosen for a widget (its back, or a corner drag).</summary>
+    public event Action<string, WidgetSize>? WidgetSizeChosen;
+
+    /// <summary>The fence's grid cell in DIPs: a corner drag snaps by it.</summary>
+    public Size CellSize => new((double)Resources["GridCellWidth"], (double)Resources["GridCellHeight"]);
+
+    public void ChooseWidgetSize(string key, WidgetSize size) => WidgetSizeChosen?.Invoke(key, size);
+
+    private Popup? _widgetBack;
+
+    private static readonly Duration Flip = new(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(160));
+
+    // ---------- the back ----------
+
+    /// <summary>The widget flips over (squash through zero) to its settings; changes apply at once; Done flips it back.</summary>
+    public void FlipWidget(string key)
+    {
+        _widgetBack?.SetValue(Popup.IsOpenProperty, false);
+        if (_items.FirstOrDefault(view => view.Key == key && view.IsWidget) is not { Widget: { } kind } view
+            || ItemList.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(view) is not ListBoxItem container) return;
+        var face = FindChild<WidgetFace>(container);
+        var squash = new ScaleTransform(1, 1);
+        if (face is not null) { face.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0.5, 0.5); face.RenderTransform = squash; }
+        var panel = BackPanel(view, kind, close: () => _widgetBack!.IsOpen = false);
+        var flipIn = new ScaleTransform(0, 1);
+        panel.RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0.5, 0.5);
+        panel.RenderTransform = flipIn;
+        var popup = new Popup
+        {
+            PlacementTarget = container, Placement = PlacementMode.Relative, AllowsTransparency = true, StaysOpen = false,
+            Child = panel, MinWidth = Math.Max(container.ActualWidth, 270),
+        };
+        popup.Closed += (_, _) =>
+        {
+            if (_motion == Motion.Off) squash.ScaleX = 1;
+            else squash.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0, 1, Flip) { EasingFunction = new CubicEase() });
+        };
+        popup.Opened += (_, _) => RaisePopup(panel);
+        _widgetBack = popup;
+        if (_motion == Motion.Off)
+        {
+            squash.ScaleX = 0;
+            flipIn.ScaleX = 1;
+            popup.IsOpen = true;
+            return;
+        }
+        var fold = new DoubleAnimation(1, 0, Flip) { EasingFunction = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseIn } };
+        fold.Completed += (_, _) =>
+        {
+            popup.IsOpen = true;
+            flipIn.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0, 1, Flip) { EasingFunction = new CubicEase() });
+        };
+        squash.BeginAnimation(ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, fold);
+    }
+
+    private Border BackPanel(FenceItemView view, WidgetKind kind, Action close)
+    {
+        var options = view.Options;
+        void Set(WidgetOptions changed)
+        {
+            options = changed;
+            WidgetOptionsChanged?.Invoke(view.Key, changed);
+        }
+        var stack = new StackPanel();
+        var header = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10) };
+        var done = Pill("Done", accent: true, () => close());
+        DockPanel.SetDock(done, Dock.Right);
+        header.Children.Add(done);
+        header.Children.Add(MenuText(Widgets.NameOf(kind), 14, FontWeights.SemiBold));
+        stack.Children.Add(header);
+        stack.Children.Add(Row("Size", Segmented([.. Enum.GetValues<WidgetSize>().Select(size => (size.ToString(), size == view.WidgetSize, (Action)(() => ChooseWidgetSize(view.Key, size))))])));
+        switch (kind)
+        {
+            case WidgetKind.Clock:
+                stack.Children.Add(Row("Seconds", Switch(options.Seconds, on => Set(options with { Seconds = on }))));
+                stack.Children.Add(Row("Date line", Switch(options.Date, on => Set(options with { Date = on }))));
+                for (var index = 0; index < WorldClocks.Max; index++)
+                {
+                    var slot = index;
+                    var box = new ComboBox { Width = 150, ItemsSource = WorldClocks.Cities.Select(city => city.Name).Prepend("None").ToList() };
+                    var chosen = options.Cities?.ElementAtOrDefault(slot);
+                    box.SelectedIndex = chosen is null ? 0 : Math.Max(0, WorldClocks.Cities.ToList().FindIndex(city => city.Id == chosen) + 1);
+                    box.SelectionChanged += (_, _) =>
+                    {
+                        var cities = (options.Cities ?? []).ToList();
+                        while (cities.Count <= slot) cities.Add("");
+                        cities[slot] = box.SelectedIndex > 0 ? WorldClocks.Cities[box.SelectedIndex - 1].Id : "";
+                        var kept = WorldClocks.Normalize(cities.Where(id => id.Length > 0));
+                        Set(options with { Cities = kept.Count == 0 ? null : kept });
+                    };
+                    stack.Children.Add(Row(slot == 0 ? "World clock" : "Second city", box));
+                }
+                stack.Children.Add(Note("World clocks show on the Large size."));
+                break;
+            case WidgetKind.Stats:
+                stack.Children.Add(Row("Temperatures in °F", Switch(options.Fahrenheit, on => Set(options with { Fahrenheit = on }))));
+                break;
+            case WidgetKind.Date:
+                stack.Children.Add(Note("The week starts as in Windows' region settings. Small: today; Medium: the month; Large: both."));
+                break;
+            case WidgetKind.NowPlaying:
+                stack.Children.Add(Note("Plays what Windows' media controls show (Spotify, a browser, Media Player). Several players? Switch with the pills on the Large size. The mouse wheel changes the volume."));
+                break;
+        }
+        var card = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(14), Padding = new Thickness(16, 14, 16, 14), BorderThickness = new Thickness(1), Child = stack, Margin = new Thickness(2, 2, 12, 14) };
+        card.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "MenuBackground");
+        card.SetResourceReference(Border.BorderBrushProperty, "MenuBorder");
+        card.Effect = new System.Windows.Media.Effects.DropShadowEffect { BlurRadius = 16, ShadowDepth = 4, Direction = 270, Opacity = 0.32 };
+        return card;
+    }
+
+    private static TextBlock MenuText(string text, double size = 12.5, FontWeight? weight = null)
+    {
+        var block = new TextBlock { Text = text, FontSize = size, FontWeight = weight ?? FontWeights.Normal, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, TextWrapping = TextWrapping.Wrap };
+        block.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "MenuText");
+        return block;
+    }
+
+    private static TextBlock Note(string text)
+    {
+        var note = MenuText(text, 11.5);
+        note.Opacity = 0.7;
+        note.Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0);
+        note.MaxWidth = 300;
+        return note;
+    }
+
+    private static DockPanel Row(string label, UIElement control)
+    {
+        var row = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 4) };
+        DockPanel.SetDock(control, Dock.Right);
+        row.Children.Add(control);
+        row.Children.Add(MenuText(label));
+        return row;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A pill button (Done, a size, a gallery size) in the menus' colours; the accent for the chosen one.</summary>
+    private static Button Pill(string text, bool accent, Action click)
+    {
+        var face = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8), Padding = new Thickness(11, 4, 11, 5), Child = MenuText(text, 12, FontWeights.SemiBold) };
+        if (accent)
+        {
+            face.Background = SystemColorsAccent();
+            ((TextBlock)face.Child).Foreground = Brushes.White;
+        }
+        else face.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "MenuHover");
+        var button = new Button { Content = face, Cursor = Cursors.Hand, Focusable = false, Margin = new Thickness(3, 0, 0, 0) };
+        button.Template = BareButton;
+        button.Click += (_, _) => click();
+        return button;
+    }
+
+    private static Brush SystemColorsAccent()
+    {
+        var accent = new SolidColorBrush(SystemColors.AccentColor);
+        accent.Freeze();
+        return accent;
+    }
+
+    private static readonly ControlTemplate BareButton = Bare();
+
+    private static ControlTemplate Bare()
+    {
+        var template = new ControlTemplate(typeof(Button)) { VisualTree = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ContentPresenter)) };
+        template.Seal();
+        return template;
+    }
+
+    private static StackPanel Segmented(IReadOnlyList<(string Text, bool On, Action Click)> choices)
+    {
+        var row = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal };
+        foreach (var (text, on, click) in choices) row.Children.Add(Pill(text, on, click));
+        return row;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>On / Off as a pill that flips (no keyboard here, so a plain mouse switch).</summary>
+    private static Button Switch(bool on, Action<bool> set)
+    {
+        var state = on;
+        Button? button = null;
+        button = Pill(state ? "On" : "Off", state, () =>
+        {
+            state = !state;
+            set(state);
+            if (button?.Content is Border face && face.Child is TextBlock label)
+            {
+                label.Text = state ? "On" : "Off";
+                if (state) { face.Background = SystemColorsAccent(); label.Foreground = Brushes.White; }
+                else { face.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "MenuHover"); label.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "MenuText"); }
+            }
+        });
+        return button;
+    }
+
+    // ---------- the gallery ----------
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The widget gallery (spec §6.3), drawn in the fence itself (a popup over a fence — never the active window — stayed
+    /// invisible in the VM probe): each widget's live preview with Small / Medium / Large — a click adds it in that size at
+    /// the end of the fence and closes the gallery; ✕ closes it. From the "+" on the title or the menu's Widget….
+    /// ponytail: click to add (no dragging a widget onto another fence yet); a drag source if the owner misses it.
+    /// </summary>
+    public void OpenGallery()
+    {
+        if (_kind != FenceKind.Items) return;
+        GalleryCards.Children.Clear();
+        foreach (var kind in new[] { WidgetKind.Clock, WidgetKind.Date, WidgetKind.Stats, WidgetKind.NowPlaying })
+        {
+            var sample = new FenceItemView(new ShownItem($"gallery-{kind}", Widgets.Target(kind), Span: WidgetSizes.SpanOf(WidgetSize.Small)));
+            sample.RenderWidget(DateTime.Now, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, stats: null);
+            var preview = new WidgetFace { IsHitTestVisible = false, DataContext = sample, Width = 150, Height = 64 }; // not hit-testable first: no handles on a preview
+            var sizes = Segmented([.. Enum.GetValues<WidgetSize>().Select(size => (size.ToString()[..1], false, (Action)(() =>
+            {
+                CloseGallery();
+                AddWidgetRequested?.Invoke(kind, size);
+            })))]);
+            sizes.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
+            sizes.Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 0, 0);
+            var name = new TextBlock { Text = Widgets.NameOf(kind), FontSize = 12.5, FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold, Margin = new Thickness(2, 0, 0, 4) };
+            name.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "FenceText");
+            var card = new Border
+            {
+                CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(12), Padding = new Thickness(10, 8, 10, 10), Margin = new Thickness(3), Width = 178,
+                Child = new StackPanel { Children = { name, preview, sizes } },
+            };
+            card.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "FenceHover");
+            GalleryCards.Children.Add(card);
+        }
+        GalleryHost.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
+        if (_motion != Motion.Off) GalleryHost.BeginAnimation(OpacityProperty, new DoubleAnimation(0, 1, Flip));
+        Serilog.Log.Information("widget gallery opened");
+    }
+
+    private void CloseGallery()
+    {
+        GalleryHost.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
+        GalleryCards.Children.Clear();
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A fence is never the active window, so WPF does not make its popups topmost and the gallery opened under the fence
+    /// (VM probe): the popup's window is raised above everything while it is open.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void RaisePopup(Visual content)
+    {
+        if (PresentationSource.FromVisual(content) is System.Windows.Interop.HwndSource source) NeoFences.Shell.FenceWindowChrome.SetTopmost(source.Handle, topmost: true);
+    }
+
+    private static T? FindChild<T>(DependencyObject parent) where T : DependencyObject
+    {
+        for (var index = 0; index < VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(parent); index++)
+        {
+            var child = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(parent, index);
+            if (child is T match) return match;
+            if (FindChild<T>(child) is { } deeper) return deeper;
+        }
+        return null;
+    }
+}
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.xaml b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.xaml
index 9a83c25..a29f9b9 100644
--- a/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.xaml
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/FenceWindow.xaml
@@ -67,98 +67,69 @@
         <!-- M31: the widgets' fonts (Windows 11's; Segoe UI where they are missing). -->
         <FontFamily x:Key="WidgetDisplayFont">Segoe UI Variable Display, Segoe UI</FontFamily>
         <FontFamily x:Key="WidgetTextFont">Segoe UI Variable Text, Segoe UI</FontFamily>
+        <!-- M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6): a widget is its face — a glass card in its size (Small, Medium, Large),
+             built in code (WidgetFace; Now playing: NowPlayingCard). Its buttons (the calendar's arrows, the player's) take clicks. -->
         <DataTemplate x:Key="WidgetTemplate">
-            <Grid Width="{Binding WidgetWidth}" Height="{Binding WidgetHeight}"
-                  HorizontalAlignment="Center" IsHitTestVisible="False">
-                <!-- M31 (spec 2026-10-06-modern-widgets-design §1): Windows 11's display font, semibold tight numbers, a soft shadow. -->
-                <Viewbox x:Name="ClockView" Visibility="Collapsed" Stretch="Uniform" Margin="6">
-                    <StackPanel>
-                        <TextBlock Text="{Binding WidgetMain}" Foreground="{DynamicResource FenceText}" FontFamily="{StaticResource WidgetDisplayFont}"
-                                   FontSize="34" FontWeight="SemiBold" Typography.NumeralAlignment="Tabular" HorizontalAlignment="Center"
-                                   Effect="{DynamicResource WidgetShadow}" />
-                        <TextBlock x:Name="ClockDate" Text="{Binding WidgetSub}" Foreground="{DynamicResource FenceText}" Opacity="0.7"
-                                   FontFamily="{StaticResource WidgetTextFont}" FontSize="11" FontWeight="Medium" HorizontalAlignment="Center"
-                                   Effect="{DynamicResource WidgetShadow}" />
-                    </StackPanel>
-                </Viewbox>
-                <!-- The date reads sideways: the day's number beside the weekday over the month and year. -->
-                <Viewbox x:Name="DateView" Visibility="Collapsed" Stretch="Uniform" Margin="6">
-                    <StackPanel Orientation="Horizontal">
-                        <TextBlock Text="{Binding WidgetSub}" Foreground="{DynamicResource FenceText}" FontFamily="{StaticResource WidgetDisplayFont}"
-                                   FontSize="46" FontWeight="SemiBold" Typography.NumeralAlignment="Tabular" VerticalAlignment="Center"
-                                   Effect="{DynamicResource WidgetShadow}" />
-                        <StackPanel Margin="10,0,0,0" VerticalAlignment="Center">
-                            <TextBlock Text="{Binding WidgetMain}" Foreground="{DynamicResource FenceText}" FontFamily="{StaticResource WidgetTextFont}"
-                                       FontSize="14" FontWeight="SemiBold" Effect="{DynamicResource WidgetShadow}" />
-                            <TextBlock Text="{Binding WidgetFoot}" Foreground="{DynamicResource FenceText}" Opacity="0.7" FontFamily="{StaticResource WidgetTextFont}"
-                                       FontSize="12" Effect="{DynamicResource WidgetShadow}" />
-                        </StackPanel>
-                    </StackPanel>
-                </Viewbox>
-                <!-- Stats as tiles (style B) with thin accent bars (style A): two columns, RAM across both. -->
-                <Viewbox x:Name="StatsView" Visibility="Collapsed" Stretch="Uniform" Margin="4">
-                    <ItemsControl ItemsSource="{Binding StatTiles}" Width="160">
-                        <ItemsControl.ItemsPanel>
-                            <ItemsPanelTemplate>
-                                <WrapPanel />
-                            </ItemsPanelTemplate>

+    private FenceMenuStyle _menuStyle = FenceMenuStyle.Guided;
+    private readonly Button _addButton = new(), _lockButton = new(), _widgetButton = new();
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// M41a: the same entries in either shape — Guided: headings over "Add to this fence" (Add opened up), "This fence",
+    /// "Everything"; Compact: an icon row (add, rename, settings, lock) over a short list with Add as a submenu.
+    /// </summary>
+    private void ArrangeMenu()
+    {
+        MenuItem[] all = [SectionAdd, SectionFence, SectionAll, IconRow, AddMenu, AddItemItem, AddFromDesktopItem, AddGamesItem, AddFolderPanelItem,
+            AddWidgetItem, SortItem, AutoCollectItem, RenameItem, FenceSettingsItem, TabColorItem, LockItem, RefreshItem, NewFenceMenu, SettingsItem,
+            DetachTabItem, DeleteItem];
+        foreach (var entry in all) (ItemsControl.ItemsControlFromItemContainer(entry) ?? entry.Parent as ItemsControl)?.Items.Remove(entry);
+        BodyContextMenu.Items.Clear();
+        AddMenu.Items.Clear();
+        object[] order = _menuStyle == FenceMenuStyle.Compact
+            ?
+            [
+                IconRow, new Separator(), AddMenu, SortItem, AutoCollectItem, TabColorItem, RefreshItem, new Separator(), NewFenceMenu, SettingsItem,
+                new Separator(), DetachTabItem, DeleteItem,
+            ]
+            :
+            [
+                SectionAdd, AddItemItem, AddFromDesktopItem, AddGamesItem, AddFolderPanelItem, AddWidgetItem,
+                SectionFence, RenameItem, FenceSettingsItem, TabColorItem, LockItem, SortItem, AutoCollectItem, RefreshItem,
+                SectionAll, NewFenceMenu, SettingsItem, new Separator(), DetachTabItem, DeleteItem,
+            ];
+        foreach (var entry in order) BodyContextMenu.Items.Add(entry);
+        if (_menuStyle == FenceMenuStyle.Compact)
+            foreach (var entry in new[] { AddFromDesktopItem, AddGamesItem, AddFolderPanelItem, AddWidgetItem }) AddMenu.Items.Add(entry);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The Compact menu's icon row (M41a): each button closes the menu and does what its entry does.</summary>
+    private void BuildIconRow()
+    {
+        var row = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal };
+        Button Add(Button button, string glyph, string name, Action run)
+        {
+            button.Style = (Style)FindResource("MenuIconButton");
+            button.Content = new TextBlock { Text = glyph, FontFamily = (FontFamily)FindResource("MenuIconFont"), FontSize = 16 };
+            button.ToolTip = name;
+            System.Windows.Automation.AutomationProperties.SetName(button, name);
+            button.Click += (_, _) =>
+            {
+                BodyContextMenu.IsOpen = false;
+                run();
+            };
+            row.Children.Add(button);
+            return button;
+        }
+        Add(_addButton, MenuGlyph.Item, "Add item…", () => AddItemRequested?.Invoke());
+        Add(new Button(), MenuGlyph.Rename, "Rename", BeginRename);
+        Add(new Button(), MenuGlyph.Settings, "Fence settings…", () => FenceSettingsRequested?.Invoke());
+        Add(_lockButton, MenuGlyph.Lock, "Lock position", () => LockToggled?.Invoke(!LockItem.IsChecked));
+        Add(_widgetButton, MenuGlyph.Widget, "Add a widget…", () => OpenGallery());
+        IconRow.Header = row;
+        BodyContextMenu.Opened += (_, _) => _lockButton.Opacity = LockItem.IsChecked ? 1 : 0.55; // locked: the lock shows lit
+    }

+    /// <summary>

+    /// <summary>The theme's accent: the fence's colour, HUD's cyan, else Windows' accent (lighter on a dark tone).</summary>
+    private Color AccentColour(FenceStyle style) =>
+        style.Accent is { } accent ? Color.FromArgb(0xFF, accent.R, accent.G, accent.B)
+        : style.LightTone ? SystemColors.AccentColorDark1 : SystemColors.AccentColorLight1;

+    /// <summary>M41a (ADR-063): the theme's own drawing; HUD draws the veil inside its cut-corner frame.</summary>
+    private void ApplyThemeLayer()
+    {
+        if (_style is not { } style) return;
+        _themeLayer.Apply(_widgetsFloat ? FenceTheme.Classic : style.Theme, AccentColour(style), Frozen(style.Veil), _motion); // a board draws nothing behind
+        var hud = _themeLayer.DrawsVeil;
+        if (hud || _widgetsFloat) Frame.Background = Brushes.Transparent;
+        else Frame.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "FenceVeil");
+        Frame.BorderThickness = new Thickness(hud || _widgetsFloat || style.Theme == FenceTheme.Neon ? 0 : 1); // drawn by the theme, or none
+        Frame.CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(hud ? 0 : 8);

+        UpdateTitleBar(PointerInside());

+    }
+
+    private void UpdateThemePause() => _themeLayer.Pause(_hiddenByUser || !ItemsShown);

+    /// <summary>
+    /// M41a (spec §2): Simple — the title as before, in display type; Clean — an accent underline and the count; Pill — emoji,
+    /// name and count in a soft pill; Caps — a glowing accent marker, spaced capitals, the count. The emoji shows in all.
+    /// </summary>
+    private void ApplyTitleStyle()
+    {
+        if (_style is not { } style) return;
+        var titleStyle = style.TitleStyle;

+        var accent = new SolidColorBrush(AccentColour(style));
+        accent.Freeze();
+        TitleText.Text = caps ? Spaced(_title.ToUpper(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)) : _title;
+        TitleText.FontFamily = style.Font.Family == "Segoe UI" && !caps ? DisplayFont : new FontFamily(style.Font.Family);
+        TitleText.FontSize = caps ? Math.Max(12.5, style.Font.Size - 1.5) : style.Font.Size;
+        TitleText.FontWeight = caps ? FontWeights.Bold : ToWeight(style.Font.Weight);
+        TitleText.TextAlignment = clean || titleStyle == TitleStyle.Simple
+            ? style.Align switch { TitleAlign.Centre => TextAlignment.Center, TitleAlign.Right => TextAlignment.Right, _ => TextAlignment.Left }
+            : TextAlignment.Left;
+        TitleText.Effect = style.Theme == FenceTheme.Neon ? Glow(AccentColour(style), radius: 10) : null; // Neon: a lit title
+        TitleMarker.Visibility = caps ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
+        TitleMarker.Background = accent;
+        TitleMarker.Effect = caps ? Glow(AccentColour(style), radius: 8) : null;
+        TitleEmoji.Text = _emoji ?? "";
+        TitleEmoji.FontSize = style.Font.Size;
+        TitleEmoji.Visibility = _emoji is null ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
+        TitleCount.Text = _countText;
+        TitleCount.Visibility = (clean || pill || caps) && _countText.Length > 0 ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
+        TitleUnderline.Visibility = clean ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
+        TitleUnderline.Fill = accent;
+        TitleUnderline.HorizontalAlignment = style.Align switch { TitleAlign.Centre => HorizontalAlignment.Center, TitleAlign.Right => HorizontalAlignment.Right, _ => HorizontalAlignment.Left };
+        TitleChrome.HorizontalAlignment = pill ? HorizontalAlignment.Left : HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
+        TitleChrome.Padding = pill ? new Thickness(4, 1, 6, 1) : new Thickness(0);
+        if (pill)
+        {

diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/Menus.xaml b/src/NeoFences.App/Menus.xaml
index 05c5083..42a57de 100644
--- a/src/NeoFences.App/Menus.xaml
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/Menus.xaml
@@ -104,4 +104,57 @@
             </Setter.Value>
         </Setter>
     </Style>
+
+    <!-- M41a (spec §5): the Guided menu's small headings — text only, never a target for the mouse or the keyboard. -->
+    <Style x:Key="MenuSection" TargetType="MenuItem">
+        <Setter Property="OverridesDefaultStyle" Value="True" />
+        <Setter Property="Focusable" Value="False" />
+        <Setter Property="IsHitTestVisible" Value="False" />
+        <Setter Property="Template">
+            <Setter.Value>
+                <ControlTemplate TargetType="MenuItem">
+                    <TextBlock Text="{TemplateBinding Header}" Margin="10,8,8,3" FontSize="11" FontWeight="SemiBold" Opacity="0.6"
+                               Foreground="{DynamicResource MenuText}" />
+                </ControlTemplate>
+            </Setter.Value>
+        </Setter>
+    </Style>
+
+    <!-- M41a (spec §5): the Compact menu's row of icon buttons on top (like the Explorer menu of Windows 11). -->
+    <Style x:Key="MenuIconRow" TargetType="MenuItem">
+        <Setter Property="OverridesDefaultStyle" Value="True" />
+        <Setter Property="Focusable" Value="False" />
+        <Setter Property="Template">
+            <Setter.Value>
+                <ControlTemplate TargetType="MenuItem">
+                    <ContentPresenter ContentSource="Header" Margin="2,2,2,4" />
+                </ControlTemplate>
+            </Setter.Value>
+        </Setter>
+    </Style>
+
+    <Style x:Key="MenuIconButton" TargetType="Button">
+        <Setter Property="OverridesDefaultStyle" Value="True" />
+        <Setter Property="Width" Value="40" />
+        <Setter Property="Height" Value="36" />
+        <Setter Property="Margin" Value="0,0,2,0" />
+        <Setter Property="Foreground" Value="{DynamicResource MenuText}" />
+        <Setter Property="Template">
+            <Setter.Value>
+                <ControlTemplate TargetType="Button">
+                    <Border x:Name="Face" CornerRadius="6" Background="Transparent">
+                        <ContentPresenter HorizontalAlignment="Center" VerticalAlignment="Center" />
+                    </Border>
+                    <ControlTemplate.Triggers>
+                        <Trigger Property="IsMouseOver" Value="True">
+                            <Setter TargetName="Face" Property="Background" Value="{DynamicResource MenuHover}" />
+                        </Trigger>
+                        <Trigger Property="IsPressed" Value="True">
+                            <Setter TargetName="Face" Property="Opacity" Value="0.7" />
+                        </Trigger>
+                    </ControlTemplate.Triggers>
+                </ControlTemplate>
+            </Setter.Value>
+        </Setter>
+    </Style>
 </ResourceDictionary>
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/NeoFences.App.csproj b/src/NeoFences.App/NeoFences.App.csproj

     <OutputType>WinExe</OutputType>

+    <!-- M41a (ADR-065): the Windows SDK projection for Now playing (Windows' media sessions, WinRT); Windows 11 22H2 (ADR-059). -->

diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/NowPlayingCard.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/NowPlayingCard.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e98f29d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/NowPlayingCard.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,419 @@
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Windows;
+using System.Windows.Controls;
+using System.Windows.Input;
+using System.Windows.Media;
+using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
+using System.Windows.Shapes;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+using NeoFences.Core.Model;
+using NeoFences.Shell;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>What a Now playing widget shows (M41a): the sessions, the one shown, its face, its cover and the cover's glow.</summary>
+/// <param name="CoverGlow">The cover decoded a few pixels wide: scaled up smoothly it is the soft colour glow behind the glass.</param>
+public sealed record NowPlayingData(IReadOnlyList<MediaSessionInfo> Sessions, MediaSessionInfo? Shown, NowPlayingFace Face, ImageSource? Cover, ImageSource? CoverGlow);
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Now playing (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6.6; the owner's now-playing-v3): the cover's colours glow behind
+/// the glass, the record slides out of its sleeve and spins while playing (the tonearm lowers on Large); pause and the arm
+/// lifts, the record spins down and slides home. A scrub bar, previous / play-pause / next, shuffle and repeat (Large), the
+/// players to switch between. Small is the island pill. Nothing moves with motion off (the play/pause icon tells).
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class NowPlayingCard : Grid
+{
+    private const double SpinSeconds = 2.4;
+    private FenceItemView? _view;
+    private WidgetSize? _built;
+    private readonly RotateTransform _spin = new();
+    private readonly TranslateTransform _out = new();
+    private readonly RotateTransform _arm = new(-4);
+    private bool? _spinning;
+    private double _recordOut;
+
+    // filled by Build for the size
+    private Image? _glow, _sleeve, _label;
+    private TextBlock? _title, _artist, _album, _elapsed, _left, _live;
+    private Path? _playIcon;
+    private Border? _scrubFill, _scrubTrack;
+    private Button? _shuffle, _repeat;
+    private StackPanel? _players;
+    private FrameworkElement? _art;
+
+    public NowPlayingCard()
+    {
+        DataContextChanged += (_, change) =>
+        {
+            if (change.OldValue is FenceItemView old) old.PropertyChanged -= OnViewChanged;
+            _view = change.NewValue as FenceItemView;
+            if (_view is not null) _view.PropertyChanged += OnViewChanged;
+            Build();
+        };
+        PreviewMouseWheel += (_, wheel) => // the Windows volume, with its own flyout
+        {
+            wheel.Handled = true;
+            VolumeKeys.Step(up: wheel.Delta > 0);
+        };
+        Unloaded += (_, _) => Spin(false);
+    }
+
+    private FenceWindow? Window => System.Windows.Window.GetWindow(this) as FenceWindow;
+    private Motion Motion => Window?.Motion ?? Motion.Off;
+
+    private void OnViewChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs change)
+    {
+        if (change.PropertyName == nameof(FenceItemView.WidgetSize)) Build();
+        else if (change.PropertyName is nameof(FenceItemView.Media) or nameof(FenceItemView.WidgetNow)) Show();
+    }
+
+    private void Build()
+    {
+        if (_view is not { } view || view.WidgetSize == _built) return;
+        _built = view.WidgetSize;
+        Children.Clear();
+        _spinning = null;
+        var (content, nominal) = view.WidgetSize switch
+        {
+            WidgetSize.Small => (Pill(), new Size(300, 58)),
+            WidgetSize.Medium => (Square(), new Size(206, 214)),
+            _ => (Wide(), new Size(420, 214)),
+        };
+        content.Width = nominal.Width;
+        content.Height = nominal.Height;
+        Children.Add(new Viewbox { Stretch = Stretch.Uniform, Child = content });
+        Show();
+    }
+
+    // ---------- the three sizes ----------
+
+    private FrameworkElement Wide()
+    {
+        var root = Card(radius: 20);
+        _art = Art(left: 20, top: 31, size: 152, outBy: 66, arm: true, root);
+        var meta = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(262, 14, 16, 12) };
+        _players = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 10) };
+        DockPanel.SetDock(_players, Dock.Top);
+        meta.Children.Add(_players);
+        var words = WidgetWords(19, 12.5, album: true);
+        DockPanel.SetDock(words, Dock.Top);
+        meta.Children.Add(words);
+        var bottom = new StackPanel { VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom };
+        bottom.Children.Add(Scrub());
+        bottom.Children.Add(Times());
+        bottom.Children.Add(Controls(full: true));
+        meta.Children.Add(bottom);
+        root.Children.Add(meta);
+        return root;
+    }
+
+    private FrameworkElement Square()
+    {
+        var root = Card(radius: 20);
+        _art = Art(left: 16, top: 16, size: 100, outBy: 72, arm: false, root);
+        var meta = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(16, 124, 14, 10) };
+        var words = WidgetWords(15, 11.5, album: false);
+        DockPanel.SetDock(words, Dock.Top);
+        meta.Children.Add(words);
+        var bottom = new StackPanel { VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom };
+        bottom.Children.Add(Scrub());
+        bottom.Children.Add(Controls(full: false));
+        meta.Children.Add(bottom);
+        root.Children.Add(meta);
+        return root;
+    }
+
+    private FrameworkElement Pill()
+    {
+        var root = Card(radius: 29);
+        _art = Art(left: 9, top: 9, size: 40, outBy: 0, arm: false, root, sleeve: false);
+        var words = WidgetWords(13, 11, album: false);
+        words.Margin = new Thickness(58, 11, 60, 0);
+        root.Children.Add(words);
+        var play = PlayButton(40);
+        play.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right;
+        play.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
+        play.Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 9, 0);
+        root.Children.Add(play);
+        return root;
+    }
+
+    // ---------- parts ----------
+
+    /// <summary>The glass card with the cover's colours behind it: the cover decoded tiny and scaled up smooth is the blur.</summary>
+    private Grid Card(double radius)
+    {
+        var root = new Grid { ClipToBounds = true };
+        _glow = new Image { Stretch = Stretch.UniformToFill, Opacity = 0.85, Margin = new Thickness(-30) };
+        RenderOptions.SetBitmapScalingMode(_glow, BitmapScalingMode.HighQuality);
+        var shade = new Border
+        {
+            Background = new LinearGradientBrush(new GradientStopCollection { new(Color.FromArgb(0x14, 8, 8, 14), 0), new(Color.FromArgb(0x8C, 8, 8, 14), 0.55), new(Color.FromArgb(0xB8, 8, 8, 14), 1) }, 0),
+        };
+        root.Children.Add(_glow);
+        root.Children.Add(shade);
+        root.Clip = new RectangleGeometry { RadiusX = radius, RadiusY = radius };
+        root.SizeChanged += (_, size) => ((RectangleGeometry)root.Clip).Rect = new Rect(size.NewSize);
+        var edge = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(radius), BorderThickness = new Thickness(1), BorderBrush = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(0x30, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF)), IsHitTestVisible = false };
+        root.Children.Add(edge);
+        return root;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The record (grooves, the cover as its label, a fixed sheen) under the cover sleeve; the tonearm on Large.</summary>
+    private FrameworkElement Art(double left, double top, double size, double outBy, bool arm, Grid root, bool sleeve = true)
+    {
+        _recordOut = outBy;
+        var canvas = new Canvas { IsHitTestVisible = true, Cursor = Cursors.Hand, Background = Brushes.Transparent, Width = left + size + outBy + 4, Height = top + size + 4, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top };
+        var record = new Grid { Width = size - 2, Height = size - 2, RenderTransform = _out };
+        var disc = new Grid { RenderTransform = _spin, RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0.5, 0.5) };
+        disc.Children.Add(new Ellipse { Fill = Grooves });
+        disc.Children.Add(new Ellipse { Width = size * 0.34, Height = size * 0.34, Fill = Placeholder }); // a label until a cover comes
+        _label = new Image { Stretch = Stretch.UniformToFill, Margin = new Thickness(size * 0.33), Clip = new EllipseGeometry(new Point(size * 0.17 - 1, size * 0.17 - 1), size * 0.17 - 1, size * 0.17 - 1) };
+        disc.Children.Add(_label);
+        disc.Children.Add(new Ellipse { Width = size * 0.08, Height = size * 0.08, Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xCF, 0xD3, 0xDA)) });
+        record.Children.Add(disc);
+        record.Children.Add(new Ellipse { Fill = Sheen, IsHitTestVisible = false });
+        Canvas.SetLeft(record, left + 1);
+        Canvas.SetTop(record, top + 1);
+        canvas.Children.Add(record);
+        if (sleeve)
+        {
+            _sleeve = new Image { Width = size, Height = size, Stretch = Stretch.UniformToFill, Clip = new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(0, 0, size, size), 7, 7) };
+            var shadow = new Border { Width = size, Height = size, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(7), Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(0x55, 0, 0, 0)), RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform(5, 4) };
+            // No cover (a player without one, nothing playing): a soft sleeve with a note, so the record never sits bare.
+            var blank = new Border
+            {
+                Width = size, Height = size, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(7), Background = Placeholder,
+                Child = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontFamily = new FontFamily("Segoe Fluent Icons, Segoe MDL2 Assets"), FontSize = size * 0.32, Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(0xB0, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF)), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center },
+            };
+            foreach (var (part, x) in new (UIElement, double)[] { (shadow, left), (blank, left), (_sleeve, left) })
+            {
+                Canvas.SetLeft(part, x);
+                Canvas.SetTop(part, top);
+                canvas.Children.Add(part);
+            }
+        }
+        else _sleeve = null;
+        if (arm)
+        {
+            var tonearm = new Path
+            {
+                Data = Geometry.Parse("M 9,9 L 9,112 Q 9,122 14,128 M 9,124 h 12 v 16 h -12 z"), Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xCF, 0xD3, 0xDA)), StrokeThickness = 3,
+                StrokeLineJoin = PenLineJoin.Round, StrokeEndLineCap = PenLineCap.Round, RenderTransform = _arm, RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0, 0),
+            };
+            _arm.CenterX = 9;
+            _arm.CenterY = 9;
+            var pivot = new Ellipse { Width = 16, Height = 16, Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x2A, 0x2D, 0x35)), Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x9A, 0xA0, 0xAA)), StrokeThickness = 1.5 };
+            Canvas.SetLeft(tonearm, 209);
+            Canvas.SetTop(tonearm, 16);
+            Canvas.SetLeft(pivot, 210);
+            Canvas.SetTop(pivot, 17);
+            canvas.Children.Add(tonearm);
+            canvas.Children.Add(pivot);
+        }
+        canvas.MouseLeftButtonUp += (_, click) => // the cover brings the player to the front
+        {
+            click.Handled = true;
+            if (_view?.Media?.Shown is { } shown) Window?.RequestMedia(shown.AppId, command: null);
+        };
+        root.Children.Add(canvas);
+        return canvas;
+    }
+
+    private StackPanel WidgetWords(double titleSize, double artistSize, bool album)
+    {
+        _title = Text(titleSize, FontWeights.SemiBold);
+        _artist = Text(artistSize, FontWeights.Normal, 0.78);
+        _album = album ? Text(11, FontWeights.Normal, 0.5) : null;
+        var words = new StackPanel();
+        words.Children.Add(_title);
+        words.Children.Add(_artist);
+        if (_album is not null) words.Children.Add(_album);
+        return words;
+    }
+
+    private FrameworkElement Scrub()
+    {
+        _scrubFill = new Border { Background = Brushes.White, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(3), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left };
+        _scrubTrack = new Border { Height = 4, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(3), Background = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(0x38, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF)), Child = _scrubFill };
+        var hit = new Grid { Height = 14, Background = Brushes.Transparent, Cursor = Cursors.Hand, Children = { _scrubTrack } };
+        hit.MouseEnter += (_, _) => _scrubTrack.Height = 6;
+        hit.MouseLeave += (_, _) => _scrubTrack.Height = 4;
+        hit.MouseLeftButtonUp += (_, click) =>
+        {
+            click.Handled = true;
+            if (_view?.Media?.Shown is not { CanSeek: true, End: { } end } shown) return;
+            var fraction = Math.Clamp(click.GetPosition(hit).X / Math.Max(1, hit.ActualWidth), 0, 1);
+            Window?.RequestMedia(shown.AppId, MediaCommand.Seek, end * fraction);
+        };
+        return hit;
+    }
+
+    private FrameworkElement Times()
+    {
+        _elapsed = Text(10, FontWeights.SemiBold, 0.6);
+        _left = Text(10, FontWeights.SemiBold, 0.6);
+        _live = Text(10, FontWeights.Bold, 1);
+        _live.Text = "● LIVE";
+        _live.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xFF, 0x6B, 0x6B));
+        var row = new DockPanel();
+        DockPanel.SetDock(_left, Dock.Right);
+        row.Children.Add(_left);
+        row.Children.Add(_elapsed);
+        row.Children.Add(_live);
+        return row;
+    }
+
+    private FrameworkElement Controls(bool full)
+    {
+        var row = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(0, 4, 0, 0) };
+        var buttons = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = full ? HorizontalAlignment.Stretch : HorizontalAlignment.Center };
+        if (full) { _shuffle = Glyph("", MediaCommand.Shuffle, small: true); buttons.Children.Add(_shuffle); }
+        buttons.Children.Add(Glyph("", MediaCommand.Previous));
+        buttons.Children.Add(PlayButton(full ? 42 : 36));
+        buttons.Children.Add(Glyph("", MediaCommand.Next));
+        if (full) { _repeat = Glyph("", MediaCommand.Repeat, small: true); buttons.Children.Add(_repeat); }
+        row.Children.Add(buttons);
+        return row;
+    }
+
+    private Button Glyph(string glyph, MediaCommand command, bool small = false)
+    {
+        var button = RoundButton(30);
+        button.Content = new TextBlock { Text = glyph, FontFamily = new FontFamily("Segoe Fluent Icons, Segoe MDL2 Assets"), FontSize = small ? 13 : 16, Foreground = Brushes.White, Opacity = small ? 0.6 : 0.9 };
+        button.Margin = new Thickness(small ? 2 : 4, 0, small ? 2 : 4, 0);
+        System.Windows.Automation.AutomationProperties.SetName(button, command.ToString()); // Shuffle, Previous, Next, Repeat: for screen readers
+        button.ToolTip = command.ToString();
+        button.Click += (_, _) => { if (_view?.Media?.Shown is { } shown) Window?.RequestMedia(shown.AppId, command); };
+        return button;
+    }
+
+    private Button PlayButton(double size)
+    {
+        var button = RoundButton(size);
+        button.Background = Brushes.White;
+        System.Windows.Automation.AutomationProperties.SetName(button, "Play or pause");
+        _playIcon = new Path { Fill = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x12, 0x12, 0x18)), Width = size * 0.42, Height = size * 0.42, Stretch = Stretch.Uniform };
+        button.Content = _playIcon;
+        button.Click += (_, _) =>
+        {
+            if (_view?.Media?.Shown is { } shown) Window?.RequestMedia(shown.AppId, MediaCommand.PlayPause);
+        };
+        return button;
+    }
+
+    private static Button RoundButton(double size)
+    {
+        var button = new Button { Width = size, Height = size, Cursor = Cursors.Hand, Background = Brushes.Transparent, Focusable = false };
+        button.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "WidgetRoundButton");
+        return button;
+    }
+
+    private static TextBlock Text(double size, FontWeight weight, double opacity = 1)
+    {
+        var text = new TextBlock { FontSize = size, FontWeight = weight, Opacity = opacity, Foreground = Brushes.White, TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis };
+        text.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontFamilyProperty, size >= 15 ? "WidgetDisplayFont" : "WidgetTextFont");
+        return text;
+    }
+
+    private static readonly Geometry PlayShape = Freeze(Geometry.Parse("M 2,0 L 14,8 L 2,16 Z"));
+    private static readonly Geometry PauseShape = Freeze(Geometry.Parse("M 1,0 H 6 V 16 H 1 Z M 10,0 H 15 V 16 H 10 Z"));
+
+    private static readonly Brush Grooves = Freeze(new RadialGradientBrush(new GradientStopCollection(
+        Enumerable.Range(0, 24).Select(ring => new GradientStop(ring % 2 == 0 ? Color.FromRgb(0x10, 0x10, 0x15) : Color.FromRgb(0x1C, 0x1C, 0x23), 0.3 + ring * 0.029)))));
+
+    private static readonly Brush Placeholder = Freeze(new LinearGradientBrush(Color.FromRgb(0x5A, 0x3F, 0xB8), Color.FromRgb(0xD1, 0x4A, 0x8E), 45));
+
+    private static readonly Brush Sheen = Freeze(new LinearGradientBrush(new GradientStopCollection
+    {
+        new(Color.FromArgb(0, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF), 0.3), new(Color.FromArgb(0x22, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF), 0.45), new(Color.FromArgb(0, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF), 0.6),
+    }, new Point(0, 0), new Point(1, 1)));
+
+    private static T Freeze<T>(T frozen) where T : Freezable
+    {
+        frozen.Freeze();
+        return frozen;
+    }
+
+    // ---------- showing the state ----------
+
+    private void Show()
+    {
+        if (_view is not { } view || _title is null) return;
+        var media = view.Media;
+        var face = media?.Face ?? NowPlaying.Face(current: null, last: null);
+        var shown = face.Idle ? null : media?.Shown;
+        _title.Text = face.Title;
+        _artist!.Text = face.Subtitle;
+        if (_album is not null) _album.Text = face.Idle ? "" : face.Track?.Album ?? "";
+        var cover = media?.Cover;
+        foreach (var image in new[] { _sleeve, _label }) if (image is not null && !ReferenceEquals(image.Source, cover)) image.Source = cover;
+        if (_glow is not null && !ReferenceEquals(_glow.Source, media?.CoverGlow)) _glow.Source = media?.CoverGlow;
+        if (_art is not null) _art.Opacity = face.Idle ? 0.55 : 1;
+        var playing = shown?.Playing == true;
+        if (_playIcon is not null) _playIcon.Data = playing ? PauseShape : PlayShape;
+        if (shown is { } session && _scrubFill is not null && _scrubTrack is not null)
+        {
+            var position = NowPlaying.LivePosition(session.Position, session.Updated, DateTimeOffset.Now, session.Playing, session.End);
+            var (elapsed, left, live) = NowPlaying.Timeline(position, session.End);
+            _scrubFill.Width = live || session.End is not { } end ? _scrubTrack.ActualWidth : _scrubTrack.ActualWidth * Math.Clamp(position / end, 0, 1);
+            if (_elapsed is not null) (_elapsed.Text, _left!.Text, _live!.Visibility) = (elapsed, left, live ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed);
+        }
+        else if (_scrubFill is not null)
+        {
+            _scrubFill.Width = 0;
+            if (_elapsed is not null) (_elapsed.Text, _left!.Text, _live!.Visibility) = ("", "", Visibility.Collapsed);
+        }
+        if (_shuffle is not null) _shuffle.Visibility = shown?.Shuffle is null ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible;
+        if (_shuffle?.Content is TextBlock shuffleGlyph) shuffleGlyph.Opacity = shown?.Shuffle == true ? 1 : 0.5;
+        if (_repeat is not null) _repeat.Visibility = shown?.Repeat is null ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible;
+        if (_repeat?.Content is TextBlock repeatGlyph) (repeatGlyph.Text, repeatGlyph.Opacity) = shown?.Repeat == MediaRepeat.Track ? ("", 1) : ("", shown?.Repeat == MediaRepeat.List ? 1 : 0.5);
+        ShowPlayers(media);
+        Spin(playing);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Small app pills when several players have a session; the one shown is lit.</summary>
+    private void ShowPlayers(NowPlayingData? media)
+    {
+        if (_players is null) return;
+        var sessions = media?.Sessions ?? [];
+        var key = string.Join("|", sessions.Select(session => session.AppId)) + "#" + media?.Shown?.AppId;
+        if (Equals(_players.Tag, key)) return;
+        _players.Tag = key;
+        _players.Children.Clear();
+        if (sessions.Count < 2) return;
+        foreach (var session in sessions)
+        {
+            var pill = new Button { Content = session.Track.App, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 5, 0), Focusable = false, Cursor = Cursors.Hand, FontSize = 10.5 };
+            pill.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "WidgetPill");
+            pill.Opacity = session.AppId == media?.Shown?.AppId ? 1 : 0.6;
+            pill.Click += (_, _) => Window?.ShowMediaPlayer(_view!.Key, session.AppId);
+            _players.Children.Add(pill);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Playing (and motion on): the record slides out, spins, the arm lowers. Else it spins down, the arm lifts, it slides home.</summary>
+    private void Spin(bool playing)
+    {
+        var moving = playing && Motion != Motion.Off && IsLoaded;
+        if (_spinning == moving) return;
+        _spinning = moving;
+        var duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(Motion == Motion.Off ? 1 : 700));
+        var ease = new CubicEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut };
+        if (moving)
+        {
+            var spin = new DoubleAnimation(_spin.Angle % 360, _spin.Angle % 360 + 360, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(SpinSeconds)) { RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever };
+            Timeline.SetDesiredFrameRate(spin, 30);
+            _spin.BeginAnimation(RotateTransform.AngleProperty, spin);
+            _out.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.XProperty, new DoubleAnimation(_recordOut, duration) { EasingFunction = ease });
+            _arm.BeginAnimation(RotateTransform.AngleProperty, new DoubleAnimation(21, duration) { BeginTime = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(450), EasingFunction = ease });
+            return;
+        }
+        var angle = _spin.Angle;
+        var stop = new DoubleAnimation(angle, angle + (Motion == Motion.Off ? 0 : 80), new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(800))) { EasingFunction = new QuadraticEase { EasingMode = EasingMode.EaseOut } };
+        Timeline.SetDesiredFrameRate(stop, 30);
+        _spin.BeginAnimation(RotateTransform.AngleProperty, stop);
+        _arm.BeginAnimation(RotateTransform.AngleProperty, new DoubleAnimation(-4, duration) { EasingFunction = ease });
+        _out.BeginAnimation(TranslateTransform.XProperty, new DoubleAnimation(playing ? _recordOut : 0, duration) { BeginTime = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(350), EasingFunction = ease });
+    }
+}

+        FenceMenuBox.SelectionChanged += (_, _) =>
+        {
+            if (!_updating && FenceMenuBox.SelectedItem is ComboBoxItem { Tag: string style }) FenceMenuChanged?.Invoke(Enum.Parse<FenceMenuStyle>(style));
+        };
         BannerLogsButton.Click += (_, _) => OpenLogsRequested?.Invoke();
         OnToggled(GameModeBox, isChecked => GameModeChanged?.Invoke(isChecked));
         RollupBox.SelectionChanged += (_, _) =>
@@ -96,8 +110,8 @@ public partial class SettingsWindow : Window
         LabelsApplyAllButton.Click += (_, _) => LabelsAppliedToAll?.Invoke(SelectedLabels);
         // Screen readers read each setting's description with it (M6b review carry-over).
         foreach (var (control, description) in new (UIElement, TextBlock)[]
-                 { (StartupBox, StartupDescription), (HideIconsBox, HideIconsDescription), (QuickHideGestureBox, QuickHideGestureDescription), (DrawGestureBox, DrawGestureDescription), (HotkeyBox, HotkeyDescription),
-                   (LabelsBox, LabelsDescription), (ArrowsBox, ArrowsDescription), (RollupBox, RollupDescription), (GameModeBox, GameModeDescription) })
+                 { (StartupBox, StartupDescription), (HideIconsBox, HideIconsDescription), (QuickHideGestureBox, QuickHideGestureDescription), (DrawGestureBox, DrawGestureDescription), (RevealGestureBox, RevealGestureDescription), (HotkeyBox, HotkeyDescription),
+                   (LabelsBox, LabelsDescription), (ArrowsBox, ArrowsDescription), (RollupBox, RollupDescription), (GameModeBox, GameModeDescription), (OpenWithBox, OpenWithDescription), (FenceMenuBox, FenceMenuDescription) })
         {
             System.Windows.Automation.AutomationProperties.SetHelpText(control, description.Text);
         }
@@ -280,6 +294,9 @@ public partial class SettingsWindow : Window
         HideIconsBox.IsChecked = view.HideDesktopIcons;
         QuickHideGestureBox.IsChecked = view.QuickHideGesture; // M33
         DrawGestureBox.IsChecked = view.DrawGesture;
+        RevealGestureBox.IsChecked = view.RevealGesture; // M41a
+        OpenWithBox.SelectedIndex = view.OpenItemsWith == OpenWith.DoubleClick ? 1 : 0;
