diff --git a/src/NeoFences.App/WidgetFace.cs b/src/NeoFences.App/WidgetFace.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5f8691e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.App/WidgetFace.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,411 @@
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Globalization;
+using System.Windows;
+using System.Windows.Controls;
+using System.Windows.Documents;
+using System.Windows.Media;
+using System.Windows.Shapes;
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+
+namespace NeoFences.App;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// A widget's face (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6.1–6.2): one glass card per widget, in the widget's size —
+/// Small, Medium or Large, each its own layout (a bigger one shows more). Built in code for its kind and size, scaled into
+/// its cells by a Viewbox; its texts and bars change in place every tick. Now playing has its own card (NowPlayingCard).
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class WidgetFace : Grid
+{
+    private FenceItemView? _view;
+    private (WidgetKind? Kind, WidgetSize Size) _built = (null, WidgetSize.Small);
+    private Action? _refresh;
+    private int _monthOffset; // the calendar's ‹ ›: months away from today's
+
+    public WidgetFace()
+    {
+        DataContextChanged += (_, change) =>
+        {
+            if (change.OldValue is FenceItemView old) old.PropertyChanged -= OnViewChanged;
+            _view = change.NewValue as FenceItemView;
+            if (_view is not null) _view.PropertyChanged += OnViewChanged;
+            Build();
+        };
+    }
+
+    private void OnViewChanged(object? sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs change)
+    {
+        if (change.PropertyName is nameof(FenceItemView.Widget) or nameof(FenceItemView.WidgetSize)) Build();
+        else if (change.PropertyName is nameof(FenceItemView.WidgetNow) or nameof(FenceItemView.StatTiles) or nameof(FenceItemView.Options)) _refresh?.Invoke();
+    }
+
+    private void Build()
+    {
+        if (_view?.Widget is not { } kind || _view.Widget == WidgetKind.NowPlaying)
+        {
+            Children.Clear();
+            _refresh = null;
+            _built = (null, WidgetSize.Small);
+            if (_view?.Widget != WidgetKind.NowPlaying) return;
+            Children.Add(new NowPlayingCard { DataContext = _view });
+            AddHandles();
+            return;
+        }
+        if (_built == (kind, _view.WidgetSize) && Children.Count > 0) return;
+        _built = (kind, _view.WidgetSize);
+        Children.Clear();
+        var (content, nominal) = kind switch
+        {
+            WidgetKind.Clock => Clock(_view.WidgetSize),
+            WidgetKind.Stats => Stats(_view.WidgetSize),
+            _ => Calendar(_view.WidgetSize),
+        };
+        content.Width = nominal.Width;
+        content.Height = nominal.Height;
+        var card = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(14), BorderThickness = new Thickness(1), Padding = new Thickness(2) };
+        card.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "WidgetCard");
+        card.SetResourceReference(Border.BorderBrushProperty, "WidgetCardEdge");
+        card.Child = new Viewbox { Stretch = Stretch.Uniform, Child = content };
+        Children.Add(card);
+        AddHandles();
+        _refresh?.Invoke();
+    }
+
+    // ---------- the handles: ⚙ flips the widget to its settings, the corner snaps it to Small, Medium or Large ----------
+
+    private void AddHandles()
+    {
+        if (!IsHitTestVisible) return; // a gallery preview
+        var gear = new Button
+        {
+            Width = 24, Height = 22, Margin = new Thickness(0, 6, 6, 0), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top,
+            Focusable = false, Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Hand, ToolTip = "Widget settings", Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed,
+            Content = new TextBlock { Text = "", FontFamily = new FontFamily("Segoe Fluent Icons, Segoe MDL2 Assets"), FontSize = 12 },
+        };
+        ((TextBlock)gear.Content).SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "FenceText");
+        gear.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "WidgetArrow");
+        System.Windows.Automation.AutomationProperties.SetName(gear, "Widget settings");
+        gear.Click += (_, _) => { if (_view is { } view) Owner?.FlipWidget(view.Key); };
+        var grip = new System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.Thumb
+        {
+            Width = 16, Height = 16, HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Right, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 3, 3),
+            Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNWSE, Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed, Template = GripTemplate,
+        };
+        var label = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8), Padding = new Thickness(10, 3, 10, 4), HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed };
+        label.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "HoverLabelBackground");
+        var labelText = new TextBlock { FontSize = 12, FontWeight = FontWeights.SemiBold };
+        labelText.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "FenceText");
+        label.Child = labelText;
+        Size start = default;
+        var (dragX, dragY) = (0.0, 0.0);
+        WidgetSize? snapped = null;
+        grip.DragStarted += (_, _) => { start = new Size(ActualWidth, ActualHeight); (dragX, dragY) = (0, 0); };
+        grip.DragDelta += (_, drag) =>
+        {
+            if (Owner is not { } window) return;
+            (dragX, dragY) = (dragX + drag.HorizontalChange, dragY + drag.VerticalChange);
+            var cell = window.CellSize;
+            snapped = WidgetSizes.Snap((start.Width + dragX) / cell.Width, (start.Height + dragY) / cell.Height);
+            labelText.Text = snapped.ToString();
+            label.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
+        };
+        grip.DragCompleted += (_, _) =>
+        {
+            label.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
+            if (snapped is { } size && _view is { } view && size != view.WidgetSize) Owner?.ChooseWidgetSize(view.Key, size);
+            snapped = null;
+        };
+        MouseEnter += (_, _) => gear.Visibility = grip.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
+        MouseLeave += (_, _) => { if (!grip.IsDragging) gear.Visibility = grip.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed; };
+        Children.Add(gear);
+        Children.Add(grip);
+        Children.Add(label);
+    }
+
+    private FenceWindow? Owner => Window.GetWindow(this) as FenceWindow;
+
+    /// <summary>The corner grip: three short diagonal lines, like a window's size grip.</summary>
+    private static readonly ControlTemplate GripTemplate = Grip();
+
+    private static ControlTemplate Grip()
+    {
+        var path = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Path));
+        path.SetValue(Path.DataProperty, Geometry.Parse("M 14,4 L 4,14 M 14,8 L 8,14 M 14,12 L 12,14"));
+        path.SetResourceReference(Shape.StrokeProperty, "FenceSubtleText");
+        path.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, 1.2);
+        var back = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Border));
+        back.SetValue(Border.BackgroundProperty, Brushes.Transparent);
+        back.AppendChild(path);
+        var template = new ControlTemplate(typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.Thumb)) { VisualTree = back };
+        template.Seal();
+        return template;
+    }
+
+    // ---------- pieces ----------
+
+    private static TextBlock Text(double size, FontWeight weight, double opacity = 1, bool display = false, bool caps = false)
+    {
+        var text = new TextBlock { FontSize = size, FontWeight = weight, Opacity = opacity, TextTrimming = TextTrimming.CharacterEllipsis };
+        text.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.ForegroundProperty, "FenceText");
+        text.SetResourceReference(TextBlock.FontFamilyProperty, display ? "WidgetDisplayFont" : "WidgetTextFont");
+        if (display) Typography.SetNumeralAlignment(text, FontNumeralAlignment.Tabular);
+        if (caps) Typography.SetCapitals(text, FontCapitals.AllSmallCaps);
+        return text;
+    }
+
+    private static StackPanel Stack(params UIElement[] children)
+    {
+        var stack = new StackPanel();
+        foreach (var child in children) stack.Children.Add(child);
+        return stack;
+    }
+
+    private static Grid Columns(params GridLength[] widths)
+    {
+        var grid = new Grid();
+        foreach (var width in widths) grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = width });
+        return grid;
+    }
+
+    private static T At<T>(T element, int column = 0, int row = 0, int columnSpan = 1) where T : UIElement
+    {
+        SetColumn(element, column);
+        SetRow(element, row);
+        SetColumnSpan(element, columnSpan);
+        return element;
+    }
+
+    private static readonly GridLength Star = new(1, GridUnitType.Star);
+
+    // ---------- clock ----------
+
+    private (FrameworkElement, Size) Clock(WidgetSize size)
+    {
+        var time = Text(size switch { WidgetSize.Small => 30, WidgetSize.Medium => 44, _ => 54 }, FontWeights.SemiBold, display: true);
+        var seconds = Text(18, FontWeights.SemiBold, opacity: 0.55, display: true);
+        var weekday = Text(size == WidgetSize.Small ? 11 : 13, FontWeights.SemiBold, opacity: 0.8);
+        var date = Text(12, FontWeights.Normal, opacity: 0.65);
+        var timeRow = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal };
+        timeRow.Children.Add(time);
+        if (size == WidgetSize.Large) timeRow.Children.Add(seconds);
+        seconds.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
+        seconds.Margin = new Thickness(4, 0, 0, 7);
+        var left = Stack(timeRow, weekday, date);
+        left.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
+        left.Margin = new Thickness(12, 0, 8, 0);
+        var cities = new StackPanel { VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center, Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 14, 0) };
+        _refresh = () =>
+        {
+            if (_view is not { } view) return;
+            var culture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
+            var now = view.WidgetNow;
+            time.Text = Widgets.ClockText(now, seconds: false, culture);
+            seconds.Text = now.ToString("ss", culture);
+            weekday.Text = culture.DateTimeFormat.GetDayName(now.DayOfWeek);
+            date.Text = size == WidgetSize.Small ? "" : now.ToString(culture.DateTimeFormat.MonthDayPattern, culture);
+            date.Visibility = size == WidgetSize.Small ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
+            if (size != WidgetSize.Large) return;
+            cities.Children.Clear();
+            foreach (var id in view.Options.Cities ?? [])
+            {
+                var (name, at) = WorldClocks.Show(id, new DateTimeOffset(now), culture);
+                cities.Children.Add(Text(10, FontWeights.SemiBold, opacity: 0.65, caps: true).With(name));
+                cities.Children.Add(Text(19, FontWeights.SemiBold, display: true).With(at, margin: new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 6)));
+            }
+        };
+        if (size != WidgetSize.Large) return (left, size == WidgetSize.Small ? new Size(160, 66) : new Size(160, 150));
+        var grid = Columns(Star, GridLength.Auto);
+        grid.Children.Add(At(left));
+        grid.Children.Add(At(cities, column: 1));
+        return (grid, new Size(330, 150));
+    }
+
+    // ---------- system stats ----------
+
+    private (FrameworkElement, Size) Stats(WidgetSize size)
+    {
+        int[] shown = size switch { WidgetSize.Small => [0, 2], WidgetSize.Medium => [0, 1, 2, 3], _ => [0, 1, 2, 3, 4] };
+        var columns = size == WidgetSize.Large ? 3 : 2;
+        var grid = new Grid { Margin = new Thickness(6) };
+        for (var column = 0; column < columns; column++) grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition());
+        var rows = size == WidgetSize.Small ? 1 : 2;
+        for (var row = 0; row < rows; row++) grid.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition());
+        var tiles = new List<(TextBlock Label, TextBlock Value, TextBlock Unit, ScaleTransform Bar, Border Track)>();
+        for (var index = 0; index < shown.Length; index++)
+        {
+            var label = Text(9.5, FontWeights.SemiBold, opacity: 0.65, caps: true);
+            var value = Text(19, FontWeights.SemiBold, display: true);
+            var unit = Text(10, FontWeights.SemiBold, opacity: 0.6);
+            unit.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom;
+            unit.Margin = new Thickness(2, 0, 0, 3);
+            var line = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal };
+            line.Children.Add(value);
+            line.Children.Add(unit);
+            var bar = new ScaleTransform(0, 1);
+            var warm = shown[index] is 1 or 3; // temperatures: a warm bar
+            var fill = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(2.5), RenderTransform = bar, RenderTransformOrigin = new Point(0, 0.5) };
+            if (warm) fill.Background = WarmBar;
+            else fill.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, shown[index] == 4 ? "WidgetBarRam" : "WidgetBar");
+            var track = new Border { Height = 5, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(2.5), Margin = new Thickness(0, 5, 0, 0), Child = fill };
+            track.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "FenceHover");
+            var tile = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(10), Padding = new Thickness(9, 6, 9, 8), Margin = new Thickness(3), Child = Stack(label, line, track) };
+            tile.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "FenceHover");
+            var (column, row) = (index % columns, index / columns);
+            grid.Children.Add(At(tile, column, row, columnSpan: size == WidgetSize.Large && index == 4 ? 2 : 1));
+            tiles.Add((label, value, unit, bar, track));
+        }
+        _refresh = () =>
+        {
+            if (_view is not { StatTiles: { Count: >= 5 } all }) return;
+            for (var index = 0; index < shown.Length; index++)
+            {
+                var stat = all[shown[index]];
+                var (label, value, unit, bar, track) = tiles[index];
+                label.Text = stat.Label;
+                value.Text = stat.Value;
+                unit.Text = stat.Unit;
+                bar.ScaleX = Math.Clamp(stat.Percent / 100, 0, 1);
+                track.Visibility = stat.Value == "—" ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible;
+            }
+        };
+        return (grid, size switch { WidgetSize.Small => new Size(170, 66), WidgetSize.Medium => new Size(170, 150), _ => new Size(330, 150) });
+    }
+
+    private static readonly Brush WarmBar = Freeze(new LinearGradientBrush(Color.FromRgb(0xFF, 0xB3, 0x47), Color.FromRgb(0xFF, 0x6A, 0x3D), 0));
+
+    private static T Freeze<T>(T brush) where T : Freezable
+    {
+        brush.Freeze();
+        return brush;
+    }
+
+    // ---------- calendar ----------
+
+    private (FrameworkElement, Size) Calendar(WidgetSize size)
+    {
+        if (size == WidgetSize.Small) return (CalendarSmall(), new Size(160, 66));
+        var month = MonthGrid(out var refreshMonth);
+        if (size == WidgetSize.Medium)
+        {
+            month.Margin = new Thickness(10, 6, 10, 6);
+            _refresh = refreshMonth;
+            return (month, new Size(170, 160));
+        }
+        var day = Text(46, FontWeights.SemiBold, display: true);
+        var weekday = Text(15, FontWeights.SemiBold);
+        var line = Text(11.5, FontWeights.Normal, opacity: 0.65);
+        var today = Stack(Text(9.5, FontWeights.SemiBold, opacity: 0.65, caps: true).With("Today"), day, weekday, line);
+        today.Margin = new Thickness(14, 10, 8, 10);
+        today.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
+        var grid = Columns(new GridLength(120), Star);
+        grid.Children.Add(At(today));
+        month.Margin = new Thickness(4, 8, 12, 8);
+        grid.Children.Add(At(month, column: 1));
+        _refresh = () =>
+        {
+            refreshMonth();
+            if (_view is not { } view) return;
+            var culture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
+            var now = view.WidgetNow;
+            day.Text = now.Day.ToString(culture);
+            weekday.Text = culture.DateTimeFormat.GetDayName(now.DayOfWeek);
+            line.Text = $"{now.ToString(culture.DateTimeFormat.YearMonthPattern, culture)} · week {CalendarMonth.WeekOf(DateOnly.FromDateTime(now))}";
+        };
+        return (grid, new Size(340, 160));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The old Date widget's page as the Calendar's Small size: a tear-off date tile beside the weekday and month.</summary>
+    private FrameworkElement CalendarSmall()
+    {
+        var band = Text(9, FontWeights.Bold).With("", align: TextAlignment.Center);
+        band.Foreground = Brushes.White;
+        var bandBox = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8, 8, 0, 0), Padding = new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 2), Child = band };
+        bandBox.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "WidgetBar");
+        var day = Text(23, FontWeights.SemiBold, display: true).With("", align: TextAlignment.Center);
+        var tile = new Border { Width = 46, Height = 52, CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(8), Child = Stack(bandBox, day), VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center };
+        tile.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "FenceHover");
+        var weekday = Text(16, FontWeights.SemiBold);
+        var month = Text(11.5, FontWeights.Normal, opacity: 0.65);
+        var words = Stack(weekday, month);
+        words.VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
+        words.Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 0, 0);
+        var row = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal, Margin = new Thickness(10, 0, 8, 0) };
+        row.Children.Add(tile);
+        row.Children.Add(words);
+        _refresh = () =>
+        {
+            if (_view is not { } view) return;
+            var culture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
+            var now = view.WidgetNow;
+            band.Text = culture.DateTimeFormat.GetAbbreviatedMonthName(now.Month).ToUpper(culture);
+            day.Text = now.Day.ToString(culture);
+            weekday.Text = culture.DateTimeFormat.GetDayName(now.DayOfWeek);
+            month.Text = now.ToString(culture.DateTimeFormat.YearMonthPattern, culture);
+        };
+        return row;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The month with ‹ › to the months around it; today in the accent, weekends tinted, days of the weeks around dimmed.</summary>
+    private FrameworkElement MonthGrid(out Action refresh)
+    {
+        var title = Text(12.5, FontWeights.SemiBold);
+        Button Arrow(string glyph, int step)
+        {
+            var arrow = new Button { Content = glyph, Width = 22, Height = 20, Padding = new Thickness(0), FontSize = 13, Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Hand };
+            arrow.SetResourceReference(StyleProperty, "WidgetArrow");
+            arrow.Click += (_, _) => { _monthOffset += step; _refresh?.Invoke(); };
+            return arrow;
+        }
+        var header = new DockPanel { Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 4) };
+        var arrows = new StackPanel { Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal };
+        arrows.Children.Add(Arrow("‹", -1));
+        arrows.Children.Add(Arrow("›", 1));
+        DockPanel.SetDock(arrows, Dock.Right);
+        header.Children.Add(arrows);
+        header.Children.Add(title);
+        var days = new UniformGrid7();
+        var panel = new DockPanel();
+        DockPanel.SetDock(header, Dock.Top);
+        panel.Children.Add(header);
+        panel.Children.Add(days);
+        refresh = () =>
+        {
+            if (_view is not { } view) return;
+            var culture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
+            var today = DateOnly.FromDateTime(view.WidgetNow);
+            var shownMonth = new DateOnly(today.Year, today.Month, 1).AddMonths(_monthOffset);
+            title.Text = shownMonth.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToString(culture.DateTimeFormat.YearMonthPattern, culture);
+            var firstDay = culture.DateTimeFormat.FirstDayOfWeek;
+            days.Children.Clear();
+            foreach (var letter in CalendarMonth.Headers(culture, firstDay)) days.Children.Add(Text(8.5, FontWeights.Bold, opacity: 0.55).With(letter, align: TextAlignment.Center));
+            foreach (var day in CalendarMonth.Build(shownMonth, firstDay, today).Days)
+            {
+                var number = Text(10.5, day.Today ? FontWeights.Bold : FontWeights.Normal, opacity: !day.InMonth ? 0.32 : day.Weekend ? 0.7 : 0.92)
+                    .With(day.Day.ToString(culture), align: TextAlignment.Center);
+                if (!day.Today) { days.Children.Add(number); continue; }
+                number.Foreground = Brushes.White;
+                var mark = new Border { CornerRadius = new CornerRadius(6), Child = number, Margin = new Thickness(1, 0, 1, 0) };
+                mark.SetResourceReference(Border.BackgroundProperty, "WidgetBar");
+                days.Children.Add(mark);
+            }
+        };
+        return panel;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Seven equal columns, as many rows as the month needs.</summary>
+    private sealed class UniformGrid7 : System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.UniformGrid
+    {
+        public UniformGrid7() => Columns = 7;
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>Small fluent helpers for the faces' text.</summary>
+internal static class WidgetText
+{
+    public static TextBlock With(this TextBlock text, string value, TextAlignment align = TextAlignment.Left, Thickness? margin = null)
+    {
+        text.Text = value;
+        text.TextAlignment = align;
+        if (margin is { } room) text.Margin = room;
+        return text;
+    }
+}
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopMouseHook.cs b/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopMouseHook.cs
index 43c5bf4..541fac9 100644
--- a/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopMouseHook.cs
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopMouseHook.cs
@@ -15,6 +15,7 @@ namespace NeoFences.Shell;
 /// <item>right-drag of 8 px or more → <see cref="DesktopGesture.RightDragStarted"/> / <see cref="DesktopGesture.RightDragCompleted"/>
 /// (draw a fence). Every desktop right-press is swallowed and, if it was a plain click, replayed so Explorer's desktop
 /// menu still appears (S2, proven in M0; S1 broke Explorer's desktop state).</item>
+/// <item>a left press and release in place → <see cref="DesktopGesture.Click"/> (M41a: "Click wallpaper to reveal desktop").</item>
 /// </list>
 /// While <see cref="PeekActive"/>, a click outside NeoFences' windows raises <see cref="PeekClickOutside"/> (and goes through).
 /// Callbacks run on the hook thread: marshal to the UI thread and return fast.
@@ -39,7 +40,8 @@ public sealed class DesktopMouseHook : IDisposable
     /// <summary>Set by the UI while fences are shown over windows (Peek).</summary>
     public volatile bool PeekActive;
 
-    /// <summary>False when the draw gesture is off (M33): right-presses go to Windows untouched, no right-drag is tracked.</summary>
+    /// <summary>False when the draw gesture is off (M33): right-presses go to Windows untouched, no right-drag is tracked. M41a
+    /// (ADR-066): also false while the fences are quick-hidden — the hook then only watches.</summary>
     public volatile bool HandleRightButton = true;
 
     public bool IsInstalled { get; private set; }
@@ -98,6 +100,7 @@ public sealed class DesktopMouseHook : IDisposable
             MouseAction? action = (uint)wParam.Value switch
             {
                 PInvoke.WM_LBUTTONDOWN => MouseAction.LeftDown,
+                PInvoke.WM_LBUTTONUP => MouseAction.LeftUp, // M41a: a click on empty wallpaper (reveal)
                 PInvoke.WM_RBUTTONDOWN => MouseAction.RightDown,
                 PInvoke.WM_RBUTTONUP => MouseAction.RightUp,
                 PInvoke.WM_MOUSEMOVE => MouseAction.Move,
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopReveal.cs b/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopReveal.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b07bedc
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Shell/DesktopReveal.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,38 @@
+namespace NeoFences.Shell;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// "Click wallpaper to reveal desktop" (M41a, ADR-066): the shell's own "minimize all" moves the windows aside, so the
+/// fences and the wallpaper come into view, and its "undo minimize all" brings them back. Not ToggleDesktop: right after a
+/// click on the wallpaper the desktop has the focus, and ToggleDesktop then takes the desktop as already shown and does
+/// nothing (VM probe, 2026-10-11).
+/// </summary>
+public static class DesktopReveal
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Shell.Application's MinimizeAll / UndoMinimizeALL, late-bound: CsWin32 has no projection of the shell's automation
+    /// object (its IDispatch interfaces live in a type library, not in the Win32 metadata). Call from the UI thread (an STA).
+    /// </summary>
+    public static bool Set(bool revealed, Action<string> log)
+    {
+        object? shell = null;
+        try
+        {
+            shell = Activator.CreateInstance(Type.GetTypeFromProgID("Shell.Application", throwOnError: true)!);
+            shell!.GetType().InvokeMember(revealed ? "MinimizeAll" : "UndoMinimizeALL", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.InvokeMethod,
+                binder: null, target: shell, args: null);
+            return true;
+        }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException)
+        {
+            log($"reveal desktop: the shell refused ({failure.Message})");
+            return false;
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            if (shell is not null) System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.ReleaseComObject(shell);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Windows' double-click time (ms): a click becomes a reveal only once a second click can no longer follow.</summary>
+    public static uint DoubleClickTime => Windows.Win32.PInvoke.GetDoubleClickTime();
+}
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Shell/FenceWindowChrome.cs b/src/NeoFences.Shell/FenceWindowChrome.cs
index 5085c31..aa7a599 100644
--- a/src/NeoFences.Shell/FenceWindowChrome.cs
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Shell/FenceWindowChrome.cs
@@ -33,9 +33,10 @@ public static class FenceWindowChrome
 
     /// <summary>Blur of whatever is behind the window, focus-independent. Requires a layered (AllowsTransparency) window.</summary>
     /// <returns>False if Windows rejected the call; the fence then shows its plain tint (fallback).</returns>
-    public static unsafe bool ApplyAccentBlur(nint handle, uint tintAbgr = DefaultTint)
+    public static unsafe bool ApplyAccentBlur(nint handle, uint tintAbgr = DefaultTint, bool blur = true)
     {
-        var policy = new AccentPolicy { AccentState = AccentEnableBlurBehind, GradientColor = tintAbgr };
+        // M41a: blur = false for a floating widget board — no glass behind the widgets, only their own cards.
+        var policy = new AccentPolicy { AccentState = blur ? AccentEnableBlurBehind : AccentDisabled, GradientColor = tintAbgr };
         var data = new WindowCompositionAttributeData { Attribute = WcaAccentPolicy, Data = (nint)(&policy), SizeOfData = sizeof(AccentPolicy) };
         return SetWindowCompositionAttribute(handle, ref data) != 0;
     }
@@ -157,4 +158,5 @@ public static class FenceWindowChrome
 
     private const int WcaAccentPolicy = 19;
     private const int AccentEnableBlurBehind = 3;
+    private const int AccentDisabled = 0;
 }
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Shell/MediaKeys.cs b/src/NeoFences.Shell/MediaKeys.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..085c4f2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Shell/MediaKeys.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,51 @@
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using Windows.Win32;
+using Windows.Win32.Foundation;
+using Windows.Win32.UI.Input.KeyboardAndMouse;
+using Windows.Win32.UI.WindowsAndMessaging;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Shell;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Now playing's mouse wheel (M41a): Windows' own volume keys, so Windows' volume flyout shows the level.
+/// ponytail: the volume keys' 2 % steps and Windows' flyout, not Core Audio and an in-widget bar; IAudioEndpointVolume if the
+/// owner wants the level drawn inside the widget.
+/// </summary>
+public static class VolumeKeys
+{
+    public static void Step(bool up)
+    {
+        var key = up ? VIRTUAL_KEY.VK_VOLUME_UP : VIRTUAL_KEY.VK_VOLUME_DOWN;
+        Span<INPUT> inputs =
+        [
+            new INPUT { type = INPUT_TYPE.INPUT_KEYBOARD, Anonymous = { ki = new KEYBDINPUT { wVk = key } } },
+            new INPUT { type = INPUT_TYPE.INPUT_KEYBOARD, Anonymous = { ki = new KEYBDINPUT { wVk = key, dwFlags = KEYBD_EVENT_FLAGS.KEYEVENTF_KEYUP } } },
+        ];
+        PInvoke.SendInput(inputs, System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.SizeOf<INPUT>());
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>Now playing's cover click (M41a): the player's window to the front, best effort (a Store app's id names no process).</summary>
+public static class PlayerWindows
+{
+    public static bool BringToFront(string appId, Action<string> log)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            var name = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(appId[(appId.LastIndexOfAny(['!', '\\', '/']) + 1)..]);
+            var window = Process.GetProcessesByName(name).Select(process => (HWND)process.MainWindowHandle).FirstOrDefault(handle => handle != HWND.Null);
+            if (window == HWND.Null)
+            {
+                log($"now playing: no window found for {appId}");
+                return false;
+            }
+            if (PInvoke.IsIconic(window)) PInvoke.ShowWindow(window, SHOW_WINDOW_CMD.SW_RESTORE);
+            return PInvoke.SetForegroundWindow(window);
+        }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException)
+        {
+            log($"now playing: the player's window could not come to the front ({failure.Message})");
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/src/NeoFences.Shell/MediaSessions.cs b/src/NeoFences.Shell/MediaSessions.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..092d8be
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/NeoFences.Shell/MediaSessions.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,116 @@
+using NeoFences.Core.Items;
+using Windows.Media.Control;
+
+namespace NeoFences.Shell;
+
+/// <summary>A player's session as Now playing shows it (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design §6.6).</summary>
+/// <param name="AppId">Windows' id of the player ("Spotify.exe", a Store app's AUMID): the key to switch and command it.</param>
+/// <param name="Updated">When Windows last updated <paramref name="Position"/> (it runs on from there while playing).</param>
+/// <param name="CoverKey">Changes with the track: the cover is read again only then.</param>
+public sealed record MediaSessionInfo(
+    string AppId, MediaTrack Track, bool Playing, TimeSpan Position, TimeSpan? End, DateTimeOffset Updated,
+    bool CanPlayPause, bool CanNext, bool CanPrevious, bool CanSeek, bool? Shuffle, MediaRepeat? Repeat, string CoverKey);
+
+public enum MediaRepeat { None, Track, List }
+
+public enum MediaCommand { PlayPause, Next, Previous, Shuffle, Repeat, Seek }
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Windows' media sessions (M41a, ADR-065): whatever plays with Windows' media controls — Spotify, browsers, Media Player.
+/// Read on demand (the widget's tick), never kept open beyond the manager; every failure is logged and reads as nothing
+/// playing (hard rule 7). Covers are kept in memory only, never written to disk. WinRT through the Windows SDK projection,
+/// not CsWin32: these are WinRT APIs, which CsWin32 does not cover (hard rule 5's exception, ADR-065).
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class MediaSessions(Action<string> log)
+{
+    private GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager? _manager;
+    private readonly HashSet<string> _failuresLogged = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+    private async Task<GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager?> ManagerAsync()
+    {
+        try { return _manager ??= await GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager.RequestAsync(); }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException) { Once("the media sessions API is not available", failure); return null; }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Every session, Windows' current one first; empty when nothing plays or Windows refuses.</summary>
+    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<MediaSessionInfo>> ReadAsync()
+    {
+        if (await ManagerAsync() is not { } manager) return [];
+        var current = manager.GetCurrentSession()?.SourceAppUserModelId;
+        var shown = new List<MediaSessionInfo>();
+        foreach (var session in manager.GetSessions())
+        {
+            try
+            {
+                var properties = await session.TryGetMediaPropertiesAsync();
+                var playback = session.GetPlaybackInfo();
+                var timeline = session.GetTimelineProperties();
+                var controls = playback.Controls;
+                var title = properties?.Title ?? "";
+                if (title.Length == 0 && playback.PlaybackStatus is not GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionPlaybackStatus.Playing) continue; // an idle, empty session
+                var end = timeline.EndTime - timeline.StartTime;
+                var track = new MediaTrack(title, properties?.Artist ?? "", properties?.AlbumTitle ?? "", NowPlaying.AppName(session.SourceAppUserModelId));
+                shown.Add(new MediaSessionInfo(session.SourceAppUserModelId, track,
+                    playback.PlaybackStatus == GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionPlaybackStatus.Playing,
+                    timeline.Position - timeline.StartTime, end > TimeSpan.Zero ? end : null, timeline.LastUpdatedTime,
+                    controls.IsPlayPauseToggleEnabled || controls.IsPlayEnabled || controls.IsPauseEnabled, controls.IsNextEnabled, controls.IsPreviousEnabled,
+                    controls.IsPlaybackPositionEnabled && end > TimeSpan.Zero,
+                    controls.IsShuffleEnabled ? playback.IsShuffleActive == true : null,
+                    controls.IsRepeatEnabled ? (MediaRepeat)(int)(playback.AutoRepeatMode ?? Windows.Media.MediaPlaybackAutoRepeatMode.None) : null,
+                    $"{session.SourceAppUserModelId}|{title}|{properties?.Artist}|{properties?.AlbumTitle}"));
+            }
+            catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException) { Once($"a player's session could not be read ({session.SourceAppUserModelId})", failure); }
+        }
+        return [.. shown.OrderBy(info => info.AppId == current ? 0 : 1)];
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The cover of a session's track (encoded image bytes, for the UI to decode), or null.</summary>
+    public async Task<byte[]?> CoverAsync(string appId)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            if (await ManagerAsync() is not { } manager || Session(manager, appId) is not { } session) return null;
+            if ((await session.TryGetMediaPropertiesAsync())?.Thumbnail is not { } thumbnail) return null;
+            using var stream = await thumbnail.OpenReadAsync();
+            if (stream.Size is 0 or > 8 * 1024 * 1024) return null; // a broken or absurd cover
+            using var copy = new MemoryStream();
+            await System.IO.WindowsRuntimeStreamExtensions.AsStreamForRead(stream).CopyToAsync(copy);
+            return copy.ToArray();
+        }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException) { Once($"a cover could not be read ({appId})", failure); return null; }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Play/pause, next, previous, shuffle on/off, the next repeat mode, or a seek: true when the player took it.</summary>
+    public async Task<bool> CommandAsync(string appId, MediaCommand command, TimeSpan? seekTo = null)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            if (await ManagerAsync() is not { } manager || Session(manager, appId) is not { } session) return false;
+            var playback = session.GetPlaybackInfo();
+            return command switch
+            {
+                MediaCommand.PlayPause => await session.TryTogglePlayPauseAsync(),
+                MediaCommand.Next => await session.TrySkipNextAsync(),
+                MediaCommand.Previous => await session.TrySkipPreviousAsync(),
+                MediaCommand.Shuffle => await session.TryChangeShuffleActiveAsync(playback.IsShuffleActive != true),
+                MediaCommand.Repeat => await session.TryChangeAutoRepeatModeAsync(playback.AutoRepeatMode switch
+                {
+                    Windows.Media.MediaPlaybackAutoRepeatMode.None => Windows.Media.MediaPlaybackAutoRepeatMode.List,
+                    Windows.Media.MediaPlaybackAutoRepeatMode.List => Windows.Media.MediaPlaybackAutoRepeatMode.Track,
+                    _ => Windows.Media.MediaPlaybackAutoRepeatMode.None,
+                }),
+                MediaCommand.Seek when seekTo is { } at => await session.TryChangePlaybackPositionAsync((session.GetTimelineProperties().StartTime + at).Ticks),
+                _ => false,
+            };
+        }
+        catch (Exception failure) when (failure is not OutOfMemoryException) { Once($"the player refused {command} ({appId})", failure); return false; }
+    }
+
+    private static GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSession? Session(GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager manager, string appId) =>
+        manager.GetSessions().FirstOrDefault(session => string.Equals(session.SourceAppUserModelId, appId, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
+
+    private void Once(string what, Exception failure)
+    {
+        if (_failuresLogged.Add(what)) log($"now playing: {what}: {failure.GetType().Name}: {failure.Message}");
+    }
+}

diff --git a/PRIVACY.md b/PRIVACY.md

 Your fences, items, settings, snapshots, backups, the icon cache and the logs live in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\NeoFences` and
 never leave your PC. NeoFences never moves, renames, changes or deletes your files: fences hold links to them.
 
+A **Now playing** widget reads what plays from Windows itself (the media controls Windows shows for Spotify, a browser or
+Media Player); the song, its cover and the player stay in memory on your PC and are never saved or sent anywhere.
+
 ## Logs and bug reports
 
 The logs help find bugs. They never contain your Windows user name (your profile folder is written as `%USERPROFILE%`),

 4. **Quick-hide:** double-click empty desktop to hide all fences (and the icons); double-click again to bring them back.

diff --git a/docs/DECISIONS.md b/docs/DECISIONS.md
index 8f7558c..99c9984 100644
--- a/docs/DECISIONS.md
+++ b/docs/DECISIONS.md
@@ -1598,3 +1598,105 @@ installable, and a friend on rc.1 should get rc.2 and 1.0.0 without reinstalling
 
 **Consequences.** The owner and other stable users stay on 0.26.x until 1.0.0; release candidates are tested in the same
 VM pass as any release; feedback comes through the issue forms or through the owner.
+
+## ADR-063 — Themes with a little life, title styles, name styles and motion
+**Date:** 2026-10-11 · **Status:** Accepted · **Builds on:** ADR-011 (layered window, accent blur), ADR-057 (a fence's own look)
+
+**Context.** Friends on rc.1: fences look "dead", titles weak, text soft; they liked the idea of game-themed fences. The
+owner picked four themes, four title styles, three name styles and the B3 motion in the visual brainstorm (2026-10-11).
+
+**Decision.**
+- **Themes** (`FenceTheme`, per fence in `OwnLook.Theme`, a default in Settings → Appearance): **Classic** (the look
+  before, its colour styles kept), **Aurora glass** (accent-tinted light drifting behind the items), **Game HUD** (cut
+  corners, accent lines, a scan line, a mono title), **Neon** (a breathing lit edge, a glowing title), **Light Mica**
+  (bright glass, grain, a top highlight, dark ink). A theme has its own tone (Light Mica light, the others dark) whatever
+  Windows uses; the strength slider is shown as **Transparency** and stays the veil's density; the fence's colour is the
+  accent (HUD's cyan without one). Drawn with brushes and shapes in the layered window (no per-frame effects, no Mica —
+  impossible on a layered window); what moves runs at most 20 frames a second, only while shown and not rolled up.
+- **Title styles** (`TitleStyle`): Simple, Clean (accent underline, count), Pill (emoji, name and count in a pill), Caps
+  (a glowing marker, spaced capitals, count); a per-fence **emoji** (`Fence.Emoji`, one grapheme). WPF draws emoji in one
+  colour (no colour-font support).
+- **Item names** (`NameStyle`, every fence): Crisp (a tight 1 px shadow, the default), Chips (an opaque pill, so ClearType
+  can work in the layered window), Plain.
+- **Motion** (`Motion`: Lively by default, Calm, Off): hover lift and glow, press dip, items arriving (Calm); tilt, light
+  sweep, ripple, staggered rise (Lively). Off in a game, in safe mode and with Windows' animation effects off
+  (`MotionPolicy`). Covers and folder panels keep their own hover.
+- **Defaults:** a new install (and Reset settings) gets Aurora glass with 30 % transparency and Clean titles
+  (`Settings.NewInstall`); an existing setup keeps Classic and Simple (the record's defaults) and sees one "New: fence
+  themes · Try them" tip (`Settings.ThemesIntroduced`).
+
+**Consequences.** The look is a per-fence choice like the rest of M36's; no schema change. A theme costs a little CPU while
+it moves (measured on the owner's PC before rc.3).
+
+## ADR-064 — One click opens; the fence menu comes Guided or Compact
+**Date:** 2026-10-11 · **Status:** Accepted · **Amends:** ADR-056 (the fence menu's entries), M34's selection look
+
+**Context.** Friends on rc.1: a click "just selects" an item and leaves an accent ring behind; the owner wants a click to
+"just go or do". The fence menu repeated what Fence settings… already holds (icon size, labels, layout).
+
+**Decision.**
+- **One click opens** an item, a game or a folder (press and release without moving; 4 px or more is a drag as before).
+  Nothing stays selected after it. **Ctrl+click** and the drag box still select several, and that selection shows until
+  the next plain click; a right-click's selection lasts only while its menu is open. Widgets keep the double-click (their
+  face is for reading); folder panels keep Explorer's double-click inside their file list.
+- **Settings → Fences → Open items with: One click** (default, existing setups too) **/ Double click**; each fence may
+  choose its own in Fence settings (`Fence.OpenWith`, null = like all fences).
+- **Settings → Appearance → Fence menu: Guided** (default; headings *Add to this fence*, *This fence*, *Everything*, Add
+  opened up) **/ Compact** (an icon row — add an item, rename, fence settings, lock — over a short list with Add as a
+  submenu). One set of entries, arranged in code. View ▸ (icon size, labels, layout) left the menu for Fence settings;
+  Sort by moved up a level. Colour stays until Fence settings gets accent swatches (M41b); Refresh is an action and stays.
+
+**Consequences.** Opening is one action less; selecting several is deliberate. No schema change: the new fields default
+when absent.
+
+## ADR-065 — Widgets in three sizes, a gallery, a flip side, floating boards; Now playing through the Windows SDK projection
+**Date:** 2026-10-11 · **Status:** Accepted · **Amends:** ADR-047 (widgets), ADR-059 (Windows 11 only: now also in the
+build target)
+
+**Context.** Friends found the widgets washed out; the owner picked glass cards in Small/Medium/Large, a gallery from the
+fence, settings on the widget's back, floating widget-only fences, a Calendar (the Date widget's successor) and a polished
+Now playing (now-playing-v3).
+
+**Decision.**
+- **Sizes** (`WidgetSize`): Small 2×1, Medium 2×2, Large 4×2 cells, each its own layout; a stored free span maps to the
+  nearest (`WidgetSizes.Of`, nothing rewritten); a corner drag snaps (`WidgetSizes.Snap`); the widget menu has Size ▸.
+- **Gallery:** "+" on the title (on hover) and the fence menu's **Widget…** open live previews, drawn inside the fence (a popup over a fence that is never the active window stayed invisible); a click on S / M / L adds
+  that size. (Dragging a widget from the gallery onto another fence: not yet.)
+- **Flip side:** ⚙ on hover (and the menu's Widget settings…) squashes the widget and opens its back — size and options
+  (the clock's seconds, date line and up to two world clocks; the stats' °F) — in a popup in the menus' colours; Done
+  flips back. Mouse only: a fence never takes the keyboard outside Peek (rc.2's Win+D fix).
+- **Floating boards:** `Fence.WidgetsFloat` drops the fence's glass, blur and title (they show on hover with a dashed
+  outline); each widget carries its own denser card. Offered once when a fence comes to hold only widgets.
+- **Calendar** is `WidgetKind.Date` shown as "Calendar" (no migration); **Now playing** is `WidgetKind.NowPlaying`.
+- **Now playing** reads Windows' media sessions (`GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager`, WinRT) in Shell
+  (`MediaSessions`): title, artist, album, cover (memory only), playing, timeline (LIVE without an end), shuffle, repeat,
+  the players; commands play/pause, previous, next, shuffle, repeat, seek. The wheel sends Windows' volume keys (its own
+  flyout shows the level); a click on the cover brings the player forward (best effort). Read on the widget tick only
+  while one is shown; never in a game, paused or hidden.
+- **Build target:** Shell and App move to `net10.0-windows10.0.22621.0` (minimum 10.0.22000) for the SDK projection that
+  ships with the .NET SDK — no NuGet package; Microsoft.Windows.SDK.NET.dll and WinRT.Runtime.dll (MIT) join the
+  self-contained app and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES. Measured: the self-contained publish grows from 144 MB to 170 MB (about +26 MB, mostly the projection).
+
+**Consequences.** Widgets look and size alike; the old free spans keep working. The installer is a little bigger. Sound
+and Game monitor widgets follow in 1.1.
+
+## ADR-066 — A hidden desktop belongs to the user; click wallpaper to reveal it
+**Date:** 2026-10-11 · **Status:** Accepted · **Refines:** ADR-007, ADR-011 (S2 right-click replay), ADR-053
+
+**Context.** The owner double-clicks the desktop to hide every fence and then wants the desktop exactly as Windows gives it
+— the right-click menu, dragging, Wallpaper Engine's interactive wallpapers. The hook (S2) swallowed every desktop
+right-press for the draw gesture and replayed plain clicks, also while the fences were hidden.
+
+**Decision.**
+- While the fences are quick-hidden the mouse hook only **watches**: it swallows and replays nothing; it reacts only to
+  the double-click on empty desktop that shows the fences again (`RunState.HandlesRightButton = DrawGesture && !QuickHidden`).
+  With the fences shown, right-presses are held back only while **Right-drag on the desktop to draw a fence** is on.
+- **Click wallpaper to reveal desktop** (Settings → General, off by default; macOS-style): a left press and release in
+  place on empty wallpaper (`DesktopGesture.Click`) moves the windows aside with the shell's "minimize all"
+  (Shell.Application `MinimizeAll`), and the next such click brings them back (`UndoMinimizeALL`), once the double-click
+  time passes without a second click — a double-click still quick-hides. Not `ToggleDesktop`: right after a click on the
+  wallpaper the desktop has the focus and it does nothing (VM probe). Not over a visible native icon, not while hidden,
+  paused, gaming or in safe mode. The hook is wanted when any of the three gestures is on.
+
+**Consequences.** Hidden means hands off; a fence-drawing right-drag still works while the fences show. The reveal gesture
+adds a left-up to what the hook reads (still `WH_MOUSE_LL` only, hard rule 3).

 ## 5. Widgets
 
-Fence menu → **Add** → **Widget** → **Clock**, **Date** or **System stats**. Size them like anything else.

+  changes show at once; **Done** flips it back.

+  the cities on its back). It follows Windows' time format (12- or 24-hour).
+- **Calendar** (the old Date widget): Small is today's page, Medium the month (**‹** **›** to look at other months), Large
+  both, with the week number. The week starts as in Windows' region settings.
+- **System stats** shows **CPU** and **GPU** use (Small), with **CPU TEMP** and **GPU TEMP** (Medium), and **RAM** in use

+  the bar to jump, **shuffle** and **repeat** on Large (when the player allows them). Several players? Small pills on Large

+  **Float** tip that shows once): each widget becomes its own glass card on the wallpaper. Point at the fence to see its

   set up. Without one, CPU TEMP shows "—" and the GPU temperature comes from Windows. The graphics card shown is the one
   using the most video memory (not a built-in one).

+## AZ — 1.0.0-rc.3 feel (M41a)
+
+| ID | Steps | Expected |
+|---|---|---|
+| AZ1 | `tools/vm/Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Name NF-Win11-Dev -Live` | every check PASS, the M41a ones included: one click opens, Ctrl+click selects, both fence menus, a hidden desktop's own right-click menu, reveal |
+| AZ2 | Click an item, a game cover, a folder; drag one a little and let go; double-click one | one click opens it once; a drag moves it and opens nothing; a double-click opens it once; no ring stays |
+| AZ3 | Ctrl+click two items, then drag them; drag a box over three; right-click one and close its menu | the two move together; the three show selected; after the menu nothing stays selected |
+| AZ4 | Settings → Fences → Open items with → Double click; a fence's Fence settings → Open items with → One click | other fences open on a double-click and select on one; that fence opens on one click |
+| AZ5 | Settings → Appearance → Fence menu: Guided, then Compact; right-click a fence of items, a folder view and a tab | Guided: headings over the right entries (no Add heading on a folder view); Compact: the icon row works and closes the menu; Lock shows lit when locked |
+| AZ6 | Double-click the desktop; right-click it, drag on it, use an interactive Wallpaper Engine wallpaper; double-click again | Windows' own menu and drag; the wallpaper reacts; the fences come back |
+| AZ7 | Settings → General → Click wallpaper to reveal desktop on; click empty wallpaper with windows open; click it again; double-click it | windows step aside, come back; a double-click quick-hides instead |
+| AZ8 | `Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Name NF-Win11-Dev -Live -Script probe-looks.ps1`, then `-Script probe-widgets.ps1` | every step PASS; the screenshots show the five themes, four title styles and three name styles, the widgets in their sizes, a floating board, Now playing with Media Player's track, a widget's back and the gallery |
+| AZ9 | An existing setup from rc.2 updated to rc.3 | its look unchanged (Classic, Simple titles); one "New: fence themes · Try them" tip; Try them opens Fence settings; it never shows again |
+| AZ10 | Settings → Appearance: each theme; Transparency from Clear to Solid; Motion Lively / Calm / Off; Item names Crisp / Chips / Plain; with Windows light and dark | each fence follows; Light Mica stays light in dark mode, the others dark in light mode; names stay readable; nothing moves with Off |
+| AZ11 | A game in full screen with Lively motion and an Aurora fence | game mode: nothing of the theme moves; after the game it moves again |
+| AZ12 | Ten Aurora / HUD / Neon fences, Motion Lively, idle for a minute (Task Manager, NeoFences' CPU) — on the owner's PC, asked first | average under 2 %; else theme motion drops to 10 fps |
+| AZ13 | Spotify (or a YouTube tab) playing, a Now playing widget Large; pause, next, the bar, shuffle, repeat, the wheel, the cover; a second player; stop all | each works or is hidden when the player does not allow it; the pills switch players; the wheel shows Windows' volume flyout; the cover brings the player forward; then "Nothing playing" with the last song |
+| AZ14 | Widgets: add each from the gallery in S, M and L; drag a corner across sizes; ⚙ → change options → Done; an old 3×3 clock from rc.2 | the snap label shows the size; the back flips and closes; the old clock shows Large; nothing else moves |
diff --git a/tools/vm/FakePlayer/FakePlayer.csproj b/tools/vm/FakePlayer/FakePlayer.csproj
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..401b078
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/vm/FakePlayer/FakePlayer.csproj
@@ -0,0 +1,14 @@
+<Project Sdk="Microsoft.NET.Sdk">
+
+  <!-- M41a: a test-only "player" for the VM, which has no audio device: it puts a song into Windows' media controls (no
+       sound needed) and logs the buttons a Now playing widget presses. Never shipped; Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Live publishes
+       it next to the build. -->
+  <PropertyGroup>
+    <OutputType>WinExe</OutputType>
+    <TargetFramework>net10.0-windows10.0.22621.0</TargetFramework>
+    <UseWindowsForms>true</UseWindowsForms>
+    <Nullable>enable</Nullable>
+    <ImplicitUsings>enable</ImplicitUsings>
+  </PropertyGroup>
+
+</Project>
diff --git a/tools/vm/FakePlayer/Program.cs b/tools/vm/FakePlayer/Program.cs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..81c29c7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/vm/FakePlayer/Program.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,46 @@
+using Windows.Media;
+using Windows.Storage;
+using Windows.Storage.Streams;
+
+// FakePlayer <log file> [cover.png]: a hidden window whose media controls say "Midnight City · M83" is playing (4:03 long);
+// play / pause / next / previous from Windows (a Now playing widget) are logged one per line and change what it shows.
+var log = args.Length > 0 ? args[0] : Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "fakeplayer.log");
+var cover = args.Length > 1 ? args[1] : null;
+ApplicationConfiguration.Initialize();
+using var form = new Form { ShowInTaskbar = false, Opacity = 0, Text = "FakePlayer" };
+form.Load += async (_, _) =>
+{
+    var controls = SystemMediaTransportControlsInterop.GetForWindow(form.Handle);
+    controls.IsEnabled = controls.IsPlayEnabled = controls.IsPauseEnabled = controls.IsNextEnabled = controls.IsPreviousEnabled = true;
+    string[] titles = ["Midnight City", "Wait", "Outro"];
+    var track = 0;
+    async Task Show()
+    {
+        var updater = controls.DisplayUpdater;
+        updater.Type = MediaPlaybackType.Music;
+        updater.MusicProperties.Title = titles[track % titles.Length];
+        updater.MusicProperties.Artist = "M83";
+        updater.MusicProperties.AlbumTitle = "Hurry Up, We're Dreaming";
+        if (cover is not null && File.Exists(cover)) updater.Thumbnail = RandomAccessStreamReference.CreateFromFile(await StorageFile.GetFileFromPathAsync(cover));
+        updater.Update();
+        controls.UpdateTimelineProperties(new SystemMediaTransportControlsTimelineProperties
+        {
+            StartTime = TimeSpan.Zero, EndTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243), MinSeekTime = TimeSpan.Zero, MaxSeekTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(243), Position = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(101),
+        });
+    }
+    controls.ButtonPressed += (_, pressed) => form.BeginInvoke(async () =>
+    {
+        File.AppendAllText(log, pressed.Button + Environment.NewLine);
+        switch (pressed.Button)
+        {
+            case SystemMediaTransportControlsButton.Pause: controls.PlaybackStatus = MediaPlaybackStatus.Paused; break;
+            case SystemMediaTransportControlsButton.Play: controls.PlaybackStatus = MediaPlaybackStatus.Playing; break;
+            case SystemMediaTransportControlsButton.Next: track++; await Show(); break;
+            case SystemMediaTransportControlsButton.Previous: track = Math.Max(0, track - 1); await Show(); break;
+        }
+    });
+    controls.PlaybackStatus = MediaPlaybackStatus.Playing;
+    await Show();
+    File.AppendAllText(log, "ready" + Environment.NewLine);
+};
+Application.Run(form);

--- a/tools/vm/Invoke-VmChecks.ps1

 )

+  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'dotnet publish of FakePlayer failed' }
+}
 if ($Live -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Build 'NeoFences.exe'))) { throw "no NeoFences.exe in $Build" }
 
 "${Name}: back to the clean checkpoint"

   if ($Live) {
     Copy-Item -ToSession $session -Path $Build -Destination 'C:\NeoFencesTest\run\build' -Recurse
+    Copy-Item -ToSession $session -Path $fakePlayer -Destination 'C:\NeoFencesTest\run\fakeplayer' -Recurse
     if ($Data) { Copy-Item -ToSession $session -Path $Data -Destination 'C:\NeoFencesTest\run\data' -Recurse }
     foreach ($folder in $Files) { Copy-Item -ToSession $session -Path $folder -Destination "C:\Users\tester\Desktop\$(Split-Path $folder -Leaf)" -Recurse }
-    $runJson = [ordered]@{ machine = $machine; candidate = 'this checkout'; script = 'live-checks.ps1' } | ConvertTo-Json

   } else {
     Copy-Item -ToSession $session -LiteralPath $PreviousSetup -Destination 'C:\NeoFencesTest\run\previous-Setup.exe'

index 3550836..685291b 100644

@@ -67,8 +67,15 @@ pwsh -File tools\vm\Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Name NF-Win11-Dev -Live [-Data <a copy
 It publishes the checkout self-contained (the VM has no .NET; or pass `-Build <publish folder>`), starts it on a copy of
 `-Data` (else a fresh start) with the `-Files` folders on the tester's Desktop, and runs `guest\live-checks.ps1`: Peek

+Ctrl+click selecting, both fence menus, a quick-hidden desktop's own right-click menu, Click wallpaper to reveal desktop.
+Same report as above. Only copies go into the VM; the originals are never changed. The release-candidate pass runs the

+hover, Fence settings) or `-Script probe-widgets.ps1` (the widgets in their sizes, a floating board, Now playing with a
+song Edge plays, a widget's back, the gallery) run instead of the live checks; the screenshots in the output folder are

   # Terminal stays open: on Windows 11 it also hosts this hidden test script, so ending its process would end the run.
   "desktop shown by the first Win+D: $hidden; Terminal back after the second: $back"; $hidden -and $back
 }

+. (Join-Path $root 'live-helpers.ps1')
