```diff
diff --git a/tools/vm/guest/live-checks.ps1 b/tools/vm/guest/live-checks.ps1
index 0ad21b7..5727824 100644
--- a/tools/vm/guest/live-checks.ps1
+++ b/tools/vm/guest/live-checks.ps1
@@ -1,24 +1,9 @@
 # Inside a NeoFences test VM (M38.1): the live checks of a branch build, dot-sourced by guest-checks.ps1 (its helpers,
 # Check, Shot and the report). The build is a self-contained publish in run\build; run\data, when there, is a copy of a
 # NeoFences data folder to start from (else a fresh start). Peek and the keyboard (ADR-060). Windows PowerShell 5.1. ASCII only.
-Add-Type -Namespace NfVmL -Name W -MemberDefinition @'
-[DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
-[DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
-public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }
-'@
-$exe = Join-Path $run 'build\NeoFences.exe'
+. (Join-Path $root 'live-helpers.ps1')
+$exe = Join-Path $run 'build\NeoFences.exe' # this checkout's build, not an installed one
 $withData = Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $run 'data')
-function Fg { [NfVm.W]::GetForegroundWindow() }
-function IsFence([IntPtr]$window) { [NfVm.W]::Title($window) -eq 'NeoFences fence' }
-function Rect([IntPtr]$window) { $rect = New-Object NfVmL.W+RECT; [void][NfVmL.W]::GetWindowRect($window, [ref]$rect); $rect }
-function Height([IntPtr]$window) { $rect = Rect $window; $rect.Bottom - $rect.Top }
-function Pointer([int]$x, [int]$y) { [void][NfVmL.W]::SetCursorPos($x, $y); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400 }
-function Taskbar { $bounds = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.Bounds; Pointer ([int]($bounds.Width / 2)) ($bounds.Height - 10) }
-function Peek { Keys @(0x11, 0x12, 0x20) }
-function PeekEnds { @(LogText | Select-String -SimpleMatch 'peek: False').Count }
-function Front([IntPtr]$window) { Keys @(0x12); [void][NfVm.W]::SetForegroundWindow($window); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800 }
-function WindowLike([string]$pattern) { @([NfVm.W]::TopLevel() | Where-Object { [NfVm.W]::IsWindowVisible($_) -and [NfVm.W]::Title($_) -like $pattern }) | Select-Object -First 1 }
-
 "machine: $($settings.machine); live checks of $exe; data: $(if ($withData) { 'a copy' } else { 'fresh start' })"
 Check 'start' {
   if ($withData) {
@@ -85,6 +70,51 @@ Check 'game-ends-peek' {
   Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
   "game mode on: $on (Windows reports: $windows); Peek ended: $((PeekEnds) -gt $before)"; $on -and (PeekEnds) -gt $before
 }
+# ---------- M41a (spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design sections 4, 5, 7) ----------
+Check 'one-click-open' { OneClickOpen }
+Check 'ctrl-click-selects' {
+  $item = Element 'OneClick' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::ListItem); $x, $y = Center $item
+  [NfVm.W]::keybd_event(0x11, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); LeftClick $x $y; [NfVm.W]::keybd_event(0x11, 0, 2, [UIntPtr]::Zero)
+  Start-Sleep -Seconds 3; $opened = [bool](WindowLike '*OneClick*'); $selected = $item.GetCurrentPattern([System.Windows.Automation.SelectionItemPattern]::Pattern).Current.IsSelected
+  Shot 'ctrl-click-selected'; LeftClick (EmptyWallpaper)[0] (EmptyWallpaper)[1]
+  "Ctrl+click opened nothing: $(-not $opened); selected: $selected"; (-not $opened) -and $selected
+}
+Check 'menu-guided' {
+  $fence = @(Fences)[0]; $rect = Rect $fence; RightClick ([int](($rect.Left + $rect.Right) / 2)) ($rect.Bottom - 12)
+  Shot 'menu-guided'; $heading = [bool](Element 'This fence' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::MenuItem)); Keys @(0x1B)
+  "Guided headings: $heading"; $heading
+}
+Check 'menu-compact' {
+  StopNeoFences; SetConfig 'fenceMenu' 'compact'; StartNeoFences
+  $fence = @(Fences)[0]; $rect = Rect $fence; RightClick ([int](($rect.Left + $rect.Right) / 2)) ($rect.Bottom - 12)
+  Shot 'menu-compact'; $row = [bool](Element "Fence settings$([char]0x2026)" ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Button)); Keys @(0x1B)
+  "Compact icon row: $row"; $row
+}
+Check 'hidden-desktop-passthrough' { HiddenDesktopPassthrough }
+Check 'reveal' { # Click wallpaper to reveal desktop: Notepad steps aside, a second click brings it back
+  StopNeoFences; SetConfig 'revealGesture' $true; StartNeoFences
+  $notepad = Notepad; $x, $y = EmptyWallpaper; LeftClick $x $y; Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
+  $aside = [NfVm.W]::IsIconic($notepad) -or -not [NfVm.W]::IsWindowVisible($notepad); Shot 'reveal-aside'
+  LeftClick $x $y; Start-Sleep -Seconds 2; $back = -not [NfVm.W]::IsIconic($notepad) -and [NfVm.W]::IsWindowVisible($notepad)
+  StopNeoFences; SetConfig 'revealGesture' $false; StartNeoFences
+  "Notepad aside after a click: $aside; back after a second click: $back"; $aside -and $back
+}
+Check 'now-playing' { # spec section 6.6: a song in Windows' media controls shows in the widget; its buttons reach the player
+  StartFakePlayer; $ready = FakePressed 'ready'
+  StopNeoFences; CloseApps
+  $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json; $fenceId = $config.fences[0].id
+  $items = [ordered]@{ schema = 1; fences = @{ $fenceId = @(@{ id = 'music'; target = 'neofences:widget/nowplaying'; size = @{ columns = 4; rows = 2 } }) } }
+  $items | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'items.json') -Encoding UTF8
+  StartNeoFences
+  $shown = Wait { [bool](Element 'Midnight City' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Text)) } 20; Start-Sleep -Seconds 2; Shot 'now-playing'
+  $pause = Element 'Play or pause' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Button); if ($pause) { Invoke $pause }
+  $paused = Wait { FakePressed 'Pause' } 10; Start-Sleep -Seconds 2; Shot 'now-playing-paused'
+  $next = Element 'Next' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Button); if ($next) { Invoke $next }
+  $nexted = (Wait { FakePressed 'Next' } 10) -and (Wait { [bool](Element 'Wait' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Text)) } 10); Shot 'now-playing-next'
+  Get-Process FakePlayer -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
+  "player ready: $ready; song shown: $shown; pause reached the player: $paused; next reached it and the next song shows: $nexted"
+  $ready -and $shown -and $paused -and $nexted
+}
 Check 'rolled-up-click' { # last: every fence rolled up, opening on click (review I4)
   StopNeoFences
   $path = Join-Path $data 'config.json'; $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
diff --git a/tools/vm/guest/live-helpers.ps1 b/tools/vm/guest/live-helpers.ps1
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ad5c0ce
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/vm/guest/live-helpers.ps1
@@ -0,0 +1,79 @@
+# Inside a NeoFences test VM: helpers for the live checks and probes of a branch build (M38.1; M41a: split out of
+# live-checks.ps1 so probe-looks.ps1 shares them), dot-sourced after guest-checks.ps1's own. Windows PowerShell 5.1. ASCII only.
+Add-Type -Namespace NfVmL -Name W -MemberDefinition @'
+[DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);
+[DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr h, out RECT r);
+[DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern void mouse_event(uint flags, int dx, int dy, uint data, UIntPtr extra);
+public struct RECT { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; }
+'@
+function Fg { [NfVm.W]::GetForegroundWindow() }
+function IsFence([IntPtr]$window) { [NfVm.W]::Title($window) -eq 'NeoFences fence' }
+function Rect([IntPtr]$window) { $rect = New-Object NfVmL.W+RECT; [void][NfVmL.W]::GetWindowRect($window, [ref]$rect); $rect }
+function Height([IntPtr]$window) { $rect = Rect $window; $rect.Bottom - $rect.Top }
+function Pointer([int]$x, [int]$y) { [void][NfVmL.W]::SetCursorPos($x, $y); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400 }
+function Taskbar { $bounds = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.Bounds; Pointer ([int]($bounds.Width / 2)) ($bounds.Height - 10) }
+function Peek { Keys @(0x11, 0x12, 0x20) }
+function PeekEnds { @(LogText | Select-String -SimpleMatch 'peek: False').Count }
+function Front([IntPtr]$window) { Keys @(0x12); [void][NfVm.W]::SetForegroundWindow($window); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800 }
+function WindowLike([string]$pattern) { @([NfVm.W]::TopLevel() | Where-Object { [NfVm.W]::IsWindowVisible($_) -and [NfVm.W]::Title($_) -like $pattern }) | Select-Object -First 1 }
+# M41a: real mouse clicks (through Windows' input, so the hook and Explorer see them) and UI Automation to find things.
+Add-Type -AssemblyName UIAutomationClient, UIAutomationTypes
+function Press([int]$x, [int]$y, [uint32]$down, [uint32]$up) { Pointer $x $y; [NfVmL.W]::mouse_event($down, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 60; [NfVmL.W]::mouse_event($up, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero) }
+function LeftClick([int]$x, [int]$y) { Press $x $y 0x02 0x04; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700 }
+function RightClick([int]$x, [int]$y) { Press $x $y 0x08 0x10; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1200 }
+function DoubleClick([int]$x, [int]$y) { Press $x $y 0x02 0x04; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 90
+  [NfVmL.W]::mouse_event(0x02, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 60; [NfVmL.W]::mouse_event(0x04, 0, 0, 0, [UIntPtr]::Zero); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1500 }
+function CloseApps { Get-Process notepad, mspaint -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Stop-Process -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; Start-Sleep -Seconds 1 }
+function Element([string]$name, $type) {
+  $byName = New-Object System.Windows.Automation.PropertyCondition ([System.Windows.Automation.AutomationElement]::NameProperty), $name
+  $byType = New-Object System.Windows.Automation.PropertyCondition ([System.Windows.Automation.AutomationElement]::ControlTypeProperty), $type
+  $condition = New-Object System.Windows.Automation.AndCondition $byName, $byType
+  [System.Windows.Automation.AutomationElement]::RootElement.FindFirst([System.Windows.Automation.TreeScope]::Descendants, $condition) }
+function Center($element) { $box = $element.Current.BoundingRectangle; @([int]($box.X + $box.Width / 2), [int]($box.Y + $box.Height / 2)) }
+function EmptyWallpaper { $bounds = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.Bounds; @([int]($bounds.Width * 0.93), [int]($bounds.Height * 0.8)) }
+function DesktopMenuShown { [bool](Element 'Refresh' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::MenuItem)) }
+
+
+# M41a: a 512 px cover for the fake player (an orange-to-violet gradient with a sun).
+function CoverImage([string]$path) {
+  Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
+  $cover = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap 512, 512; $g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($cover)
+  $brush = New-Object System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush (New-Object System.Drawing.Point 0, 0), (New-Object System.Drawing.Point 512, 512), ([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(255, 255, 106, 61)), ([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(255, 123, 47, 247))
+  $g.FillRectangle($brush, 0, 0, 512, 512); $g.FillEllipse((New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush ([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(230, 255, 210, 122))), 250, 110, 170, 170)
+  $g.Dispose(); $cover.Save($path); $cover.Dispose()
+}
+
+# M41a: FakePlayer (tools\vm\FakePlayer) puts "Midnight City - M83" into Windows' media controls; its log lists the buttons pressed.
+$fakeLog = 'C:\NeoFencesTest\fakeplayer.log'
+function StartFakePlayer {
+  Remove-Item -LiteralPath $fakeLog -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
+  $cover = 'C:\NeoFencesTest\cover.png'; CoverImage $cover
+  Start-Process (Join-Path $run 'fakeplayer\FakePlayer.exe') -ArgumentList $fakeLog, $cover
+  [void](Wait { (Test-Path -LiteralPath $fakeLog) -and @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $fakeLog) -contains 'ready' } 30)
+}
+function FakePressed([string]$button) { (Test-Path -LiteralPath $fakeLog) -and @(Get-Content -LiteralPath $fakeLog) -contains $button }
+function Invoke($element) { $element.GetCurrentPattern([System.Windows.Automation.InvokePattern]::Pattern).Invoke(); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800 }
+
+# M41a: two checks shared by the live checks and the release-candidate pass (guest-checks.ps1).
+function OneClickOpen { # a folder item in the first fence opens with one click
+  StopNeoFences; CloseApps # nothing from the checks before may cover the fence
+  $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json; $fenceId = $config.fences[0].id
+  $folder = 'C:\NeoFencesTest\OneClick'; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $folder | Out-Null
+  $items = [ordered]@{ schema = 1; fences = @{ $fenceId = @(@{ id = 'oneclick'; target = $folder }) } }
+  $items | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'items.json') -Encoding UTF8
+  StartNeoFences
+  $item = Element 'OneClick' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::ListItem); if (-not $item) { throw 'the item is not in the fence' }
+  $x, $y = Center $item; LeftClick $x $y
+  $opened = Wait { [bool](WindowLike '*OneClick*') } 15; Shot 'one-click-opened'
+  if ($opened) { [void][NfVm.W]::SetForegroundWindow((WindowLike '*OneClick*')); Keys @(0x12, 0x73) } # Alt+F4
+  "one click opened the folder: $opened"; $opened
+}
+function HiddenDesktopPassthrough { # ADR-066: hidden by a double-click, the desktop's right-click menu is Windows' own
+  $x, $y = EmptyWallpaper; $before = @(LogText | Select-String -SimpleMatch 'quick-hide: True').Count
+  DoubleClick $x $y; $hidden = @(LogText | Select-String -SimpleMatch 'quick-hide: True').Count -gt $before
+  RightClick $x $y; $menu = DesktopMenuShown; Shot 'hidden-desktop-menu'; Keys @(0x1B); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600
+  DoubleClick $x $y; $back = @(Fences).Count -gt 0
+  RightClick $x $y; $shownMenu = DesktopMenuShown; Keys @(0x1B)
+  "hidden: $hidden; Explorer's menu while hidden: $menu; fences back: $back; menu with fences shown (replayed): $shownMenu"
+  $hidden -and $menu -and $back -and $shownMenu
+}
diff --git a/tools/vm/guest/probe-looks.ps1 b/tools/vm/guest/probe-looks.ps1
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..55de50c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/vm/guest/probe-looks.ps1
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+# Inside a NeoFences test VM (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design sections 1-4): screenshots of the looks - six fences
+# side by side, one per theme and title style, then the name styles, the hover and the two settings windows - for the owner
+# to judge. Dot-sourced by guest-checks.ps1 (run with Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Live -Script probe-looks.ps1). Every step is a
+# check that passes when it ran; the pictures are the result. Windows PowerShell 5.1. ASCII only.
+. (Join-Path $root 'live-helpers.ps1')
+$exe = Join-Path $run 'build\NeoFences.exe' # this checkout's build, not an installed one
+$withData = Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $run 'data')
+function Emoji([int]$code) { [char]::ConvertFromUtf32($code) }
+
+Check 'start' { StartNeoFences; StopNeoFences; Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') }
+Check 'six-fences' {
+  $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
+  $fingerprint = $config.lastLayoutFingerprint
+  $monitor = @($config.layouts.$fingerprint.fences.PSObject.Properties)[0].Value.monitor
+  $fences = @(
+    @{ id = 'aurora'; title = 'Games'; tabColor = 'purple'; emoji = (Emoji 0x1F3AE); look = @{ theme = 'aurora'; titleStyle = 'clean'; strength = 30 } },
+    @{ id = 'hud'; title = 'Arsenal'; look = @{ theme = 'hud'; titleStyle = 'caps'; strength = 55 } },
+    @{ id = 'neon'; title = 'Music'; tabColor = 'pink'; emoji = (Emoji 0x1F3B5); look = @{ theme = 'neon'; titleStyle = 'pill'; strength = 45 } },
+    @{ id = 'mica'; title = 'Work'; tabColor = 'blue'; emoji = (Emoji 0x1F4BC); look = @{ theme = 'lightMica'; titleStyle = 'clean'; strength = 60 } },
+    @{ id = 'classic'; title = 'Downloads'; tabColor = 'orange'; look = @{ theme = 'classic'; titleStyle = 'simple'; strength = 25 } },
+    @{ id = 'pill'; title = 'Projects'; tabColor = 'teal'; emoji = (Emoji 0x1F6E0); look = @{ theme = 'aurora'; titleStyle = 'caps'; strength = 40 } })
+  $rects = @{}; $index = 0
+  foreach ($fence in $fences) {
+    $rects[$fence.id] = @{ monitor = $monitor; x = 12 + ($index % 3) * 336; y = 12 + [math]::Floor($index / 3) * 300; w = 320; h = 270 }; $index++ }
+  $config.fences = $fences
+  $config.layouts.$fingerprint.fences = $rects
+  $config | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 50 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') -Encoding UTF8
+  $targets = @('C:\Windows', 'C:\Users', 'C:\Program Files', 'C:\Windows\notepad.exe', 'C:\Windows\regedit.exe', 'https://github.com', 'C:\Windows\System32\calc.exe')
+  $items = @{}
+  foreach ($fence in $fences) { $items[$fence.id] = @($targets | ForEach-Object -Begin { $n = 0 } -Process { @{ id = "$($fence.id)-$n"; target = $_ }; $n++ }) }
+  [ordered]@{ schema = 1; fences = $items } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'items.json') -Encoding UTF8
+  StartNeoFences; Start-Sleep -Seconds 4; Shot 'looks-lively'
+  "fences: $(@(Fences).Count)"; @(Fences).Count -eq 6
+}
+Check 'hover' {
+  $item = Element 'Windows' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::ListItem)
+  if ($item) { $x, $y = Center $item; Pointer $x $y; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300; Shot 'looks-hover' }
+  [bool]$item
+}
+Check 'scan-later' { Start-Sleep -Seconds 3; Shot 'looks-later'; $true } # the lights drifted, the scan line moved
+foreach ($names in 'chips', 'plain') {
+  Check "names-$names" {
+    StopNeoFences; $path = Join-Path $data 'config.json'; $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
+    $config.settings.appearance | Add-Member -NotePropertyName nameStyle -NotePropertyValue $names -Force
+    $config | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 50 | Set-Content -LiteralPath $path -Encoding UTF8
+    StartNeoFences; Start-Sleep -Seconds 3; Shot "looks-names-$names"; $true
+  }
+}
+Check 'fence-settings' {
+  $fence = @(Fences | Where-Object { (Rect $_).Left -lt 300 -and (Rect $_).Top -lt 200 })[0]; $rect = Rect $fence
+  RightClick ([int](($rect.Left + $rect.Right) / 2)) ($rect.Bottom - 12)
+  $entry = Element "Fence settings$([char]0x2026)" ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::MenuItem)
+  if (-not $entry) { Keys @(0x1B); 'no menu entry'; $false; return }
+  $entry.GetCurrentPattern([System.Windows.Automation.InvokePattern]::Pattern).Invoke(); Start-Sleep -Seconds 2; Shot 'looks-fence-settings'
+  $window = WindowLike '*fence settings'; if ($window) { [void][NfVm.W]::SetForegroundWindow($window); Keys @(0x12, 0x73) }
+  [bool]$window
+}
diff --git a/tools/vm/guest/probe-widgets.ps1 b/tools/vm/guest/probe-widgets.ps1
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2606c11
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/vm/guest/probe-widgets.ps1
@@ -0,0 +1,93 @@
+# Inside a NeoFences test VM (M41a, spec 2026-10-11-m41a-feel-design section 6): screenshots of the widgets - a fence with
+# Clock, Calendar, System stats and Now playing in their sizes, a floating board, a sound playing in Media Player, the hover
+# handles, a widget's back and the gallery - for the owner to judge. Dot-sourced by guest-checks.ps1 (Invoke-VmChecks.ps1
+# -Live -Script probe-widgets.ps1). Windows PowerShell 5.1. ASCII only.
+. (Join-Path $root 'live-helpers.ps1')
+$exe = Join-Path $run 'build\NeoFences.exe' # this checkout's build, not an installed one
+$withData = Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $run 'data')
+
+function Widget([string]$id, [string]$kind, [int]$columns, [int]$rows, $options = $null) {
+  $item = [ordered]@{ id = $id; target = "neofences:widget/$kind"; size = @{ columns = $columns; rows = $rows } }
+  if ($options) { $item.widget = $options }
+  $item
+}
+
+# A quiet ninety-second tone as a WAV for the player page below.
+function ToneFile([string]$path) {
+  $rate = 8000; $seconds = 90; $samples = $rate * $seconds
+  $stream = [IO.File]::Create($path); $writer = New-Object IO.BinaryWriter $stream
+  $writer.Write([Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes('RIFF')); $writer.Write([int](36 + $samples * 2)); $writer.Write([Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes('WAVEfmt '))
+  $writer.Write([int]16); $writer.Write([int16]1); $writer.Write([int16]1); $writer.Write([int]$rate); $writer.Write([int]($rate * 2)); $writer.Write([int16]2); $writer.Write([int16]16)
+  $writer.Write([Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes('data')); $writer.Write([int]($samples * 2))
+  $tone = New-Object byte[] ($samples * 2)
+  for ($n = 0; $n -lt $samples; $n++) { $value = [int16](600 * [math]::Sin(2 * [math]::PI * 220 * $n / $rate)); $tone[2 * $n] = $value -band 0xFF; $tone[2 * $n + 1] = ($value -shr 8) -band 0xFF }
+  $writer.Write($tone); $writer.Close()
+}
+
+# A song for Windows' media controls: Edge plays the tone from a page that names it (title, artist, album) and gives it a
+# cover through the Media Session API - the way Spotify or YouTube Music show in Windows' media flyout.
+function Player {
+  $folder = 'C:\NeoFencesTest\player'; New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $folder | Out-Null
+  ToneFile (Join-Path $folder 'tone.wav')
+  $cover = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap 512, 512; $g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($cover)
+  $brush = New-Object System.Drawing.Drawing2D.LinearGradientBrush (New-Object System.Drawing.Point 0, 0), (New-Object System.Drawing.Point 512, 512), ([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(255, 255, 106, 61)), ([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(255, 123, 47, 247))
+  $g.FillRectangle($brush, 0, 0, 512, 512); $g.FillEllipse((New-Object System.Drawing.SolidBrush ([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(230, 255, 210, 122))), 250, 110, 170, 170)
+  $g.Dispose(); $cover.Save((Join-Path $folder 'cover.png')); $cover.Dispose()
+  $page = @'
+<!doctype html><html><body style="background:#111;color:#eee;font:16px sans-serif">
+<audio id="a" src="tone.wav" autoplay loop controls></audio>
+<script>
+navigator.mediaSession.metadata = new MediaMetadata({ title: 'Midnight City', artist: 'M83', album: 'Hurry Up, We Are Dreaming',
+  artwork: [{ src: 'cover.png', sizes: '512x512', type: 'image/png' }] });
+document.getElementById('a').play();
+</script></body></html>
+'@
+  Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $folder 'player.html') -Value $page -Encoding ASCII
+  $edge = 'C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe'
+  Start-Process $edge -ArgumentList "--app=file:///C:/NeoFencesTest/player/player.html", '--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required', '--no-first-run', "--user-data-dir=$env:TEMP\nf-player", '--window-size=400,200', '--window-position=1200,900'
+  Start-Sleep -Seconds 6
+}
+
+Check 'start' { StartNeoFences; StopNeoFences; Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') }
+Check 'widget-fences' {
+  $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
+  $fingerprint = $config.lastLayoutFingerprint
+  $monitor = @($config.layouts.$fingerprint.fences.PSObject.Properties)[0].Value.monitor
+  $config.fences = @(
+    @{ id = 'desk'; title = 'Desk'; tabColor = 'blue'; look = @{ theme = 'aurora'; titleStyle = 'clean'; strength = 35 } },
+    @{ id = 'board'; title = 'Board'; widgetsFloat = $true })
+  $config.layouts.$fingerprint.fences = @{
+    desk = @{ monitor = $monitor; x = 12; y = 12; w = 470; h = 520 }; board = @{ monitor = $monitor; x = 500; y = 12; w = 500; h = 420 } }
+  $config | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 50 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'config.json') -Encoding UTF8
+  $items = @{
+    desk = @((Widget 'clock' 'clock' 2 1), (Widget 'calendar' 'date' 2 2), (Widget 'stats' 'stats' 2 2), (Widget 'music' 'nowplaying' 4 2))
+    board = @((Widget 'bigclock' 'clock' 4 2 @{ date = $true; cities = @('Europe/London', 'Asia/Tokyo') }), (Widget 'month' 'date' 4 2), (Widget 'pill' 'nowplaying' 2 1)) }
+  [ordered]@{ schema = 1; fences = $items } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $data 'items.json') -Encoding UTF8
+  Player; StartNeoFences; Start-Sleep -Seconds 10; Shot 'widgets'
+  "fences: $(@(Fences).Count)"; @(Fences).Count -eq 2
+}
+Check 'later' { Start-Sleep -Seconds 4; Shot 'widgets-later'; $true } # the record turned, the time ran on
+Check 'handles' {
+  $desk = @(Fences | Where-Object { (Rect $_).Left -lt 300 })[0]; $rect = Rect $desk
+  Pointer ($rect.Left + 120) ($rect.Top + 120); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500; Shot 'widgets-hover'; $true
+}
+Check 'back' {
+  $desk = @(Fences | Where-Object { (Rect $_).Left -lt 300 })[0]; $rect = Rect $desk
+  RightClick ($rect.Left + 80) ($rect.Top + 60)
+  $entry = Element "Widget settings$([char]0x2026)" ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::MenuItem)
+  if (-not $entry) { Keys @(0x1B); 'no menu entry'; $false; return }
+  $entry.GetCurrentPattern([System.Windows.Automation.InvokePattern]::Pattern).Invoke(); Start-Sleep -Seconds 1; Shot 'widget-back'
+  Keys @(0x1B); LeftClick (EmptyWallpaper)[0] (EmptyWallpaper)[1]; $true
+}
+Check 'gallery' {
+  $desk = @(Fences | Where-Object { (Rect $_).Left -lt 300 })[0]; $rect = Rect $desk
+  Pointer ($rect.Left + 200) ($rect.Top + 14); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600
+  Pointer ($rect.Left + 200) ($rect.Top + 60); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300; Pointer ($rect.Left + 200) ($rect.Top + 14); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
+  $plus = Element 'Add a widget' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Button)
+  if (-not $plus) { 'no + on the title'; $false; return }
+  $x, $y = Center $plus; LeftClick $x $y; Start-Sleep -Seconds 1; Shot 'gallery' # a real click, as a person does
+  $title = Element 'Add a widget' ([System.Windows.Automation.ControlType]::Text)
+  $where = if ($title) { "$($title.Current.BoundingRectangle), offscreen: $($title.Current.IsOffscreen)" } else { 'none' }
+  LeftClick (EmptyWallpaper)[0] (EmptyWallpaper)[1]
+  "gallery title at: $where"; [bool]$title
+}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Check.** Every `tools/vm/guest/*.ps1` parses (`[System.Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseFile`,
  0 errors) and is ASCII only; `dotnet build tools/vm/FakePlayer` → 0 errors.
- [ ] **Step 3: Commit.** `git add -A && git commit -m "docs: recorded the M41a feel decisions, guide and checks"`.

### Task 4: Final review, VM checks, the owner's look (asked first), merge

  one fix pass for Critical / Important findings, each with a failing test first where Core.
- [ ] `tools/vm/Invoke-VmChecks.ps1 -Name NF-Win11-Dev -Live` → every check PASS (**14 of 14** — the M38 Peek checks, and one click opens a folder, Ctrl+click selects without opening, the Guided headings and the Compact icon row, a quick-hidden desktop shows Explorer's own menu (and the replayed one with fences shown), Click wallpaper to reveal moves Notepad aside and back, Now playing shows FakePlayer's song and its pause and next reach the player).
- [ ] The probes `-Script probe-looks.ps1` and `-Script probe-widgets.ps1` → all steps PASS; send the owner the
  screenshots.
- [ ] **On the owner's PC — ask first, say when it starts and stops:** ten themed fences with Motion Lively idle for a
  minute (NeoFences' CPU in Task Manager, < 2 % average; else drop theme motion to 10 fps), and a look at Now playing with
  Spotify or a YouTube tab (AZ12, AZ13).
- [ ] Merge locally into `main` (fast-forward), ROADMAP (M41a done), SESSION-LOG, the hub; push, tag and the rc.3 release
  only with the owner's go (`docs/RELEASING.md`, RC section).
